### [1] [State-selective dynamical freezing in driven Rydberg atom chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28215)
*Madhumita Sarkar,Sougato Bose,Roopayan Ghosh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究周期驱动Rydberg原子链中动力学冻结的状态依赖性，利用Floquet微扰理论表明每个自旋翻转由以其局域相互作用移动决定的单一干涉振幅刻画，状态仅在全部可用翻转振幅同时消失时冻结，从而定量解释了冻结频率随原子间距的移动与增殖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学冻结（特频驱动下近乎完全抑制动力学）是缓解Floquet加热的途径，但在相互作用体系中每个跃迁的能量代价依赖多体构型，使冻结条件本身具有状态依赖性，需要厘清这一依赖性。

Method: 研究单频与双频周期驱动的Rydberg原子链，采用Floquet微扰理论，将各状态冻结条件归约为局域翻转振幅的集体消失，并按局域motif对初态族进行分类。

Result: 该图像定量再现了冻结频率随原子间距的移动与增殖，以及整族初态（按单一局域motif排序）的响应；冻结仅在间距窗口内较强，该窗口一侧受相互作用尾部限制、另一侧受最近邻激发facilitation限制，窗口外双频驱动的优势消失。在大失谐振幅区，冻结点众多且微扰描述可控，但该窗口几乎不给PXP极限留出空间。

Conclusion: 冻结的强弱与可用性由相互作用尾部与最近邻facilitation共同界定的间距窗口决定，双频驱动的优势在其中才存在；大失谐区与PXP极限几乎不兼容。

Abstract: Dynamical freezing, the near-complete suppression of dynamics at special drive frequencies, offers a route to mitigating Floquet heating in periodically driven quantum systems. In interacting systems, however, the energy cost of each transition depends on the many-body configuration, so that the freezing condition itself becomes state dependent. We study this dependence in periodically driven Rydberg atom chains under single- and bi-frequency driving. Using Floquet perturbation theory, we show that each spin flip is characterised by a single interference amplitude set by its local interaction shift, and that a state freezes only where the amplitudes of all its available flips vanish simultaneously. This picture accounts quantitatively for the shift and proliferation of freezing frequencies with interatomic spacing and for the response of entire families of initial states, which are sorted by a single local motif. Freezing is strong only within a window of spacings bounded by interaction tails on one side and nearest-neighbour excitation facilitation on the other, outside of which the advantage of bi-frequency driving is lost. In the regime of large detuning amplitude, where freezing points are numerous and the perturbative description is controlled, this window leaves almost no room for the PXP limit.

</details>


### [2] [Foliated surface codes for optimal skip-gate leakage correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28219)
*Runjiang Bi,Nikita Astrakhantsev,Thomas B. Smith,Margaret Pavlovich,Ming Li,Shruti Puri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究"跳过门泄漏"（skip-gate leakage，如原子丢失、光子丢失导致量子比特离开计算子空间并使后续门失效）的纠错问题，比较基于SWAP的与foliated的面码线路在类空与类时泄漏错误下的最优性，并通过数值模拟与中性原子噪声模型验证，提出纠错线路应与逻辑操作协同设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泄漏只有在末态测量时才可检测，因此纠错线路必须周期性测量所有量子比特（如SWAP类线路与foliation）。但不同线路结构对空间型与时间型泄漏错误的纠正能力尚缺乏系统比较，需要明确何种线路在何种泄漏构型下达到最优。

Method: 定义"最优"为能纠正其可检测的全部泄漏错误；分别对类空与类时泄漏错误构型分析最佳SWAP面码与foliated面码的纠错能力，并以数值模拟验证；在0.5%双比特门泄漏下比较distance-4旋转稳定子实验与存储实验；并构建实验启发的neutral-atom噪声模型，扫描从计算误差主导到原子丢失主导的参数区间，比较旋转与非旋转码几何下两种线路的表现。

Result: 最佳SWAP面码对类空泄漏最优，但对类时泄漏次优；foliated面码对类空与类时构型均最优。0.5%泄漏下，distance-4旋转稳定子实验中每增加两轮，foliation将类时错误抑制1380(50)，而最佳SWAP仅为63(1)；存储实验中两者相当。中性原子噪声模型下，foliation在旋转与非旋转几何的稳定子实验中均优于SWAP线路，且该优势在参数扫描中保持；有趣的是，非旋转码存储的SWAP线路在计算噪声主导时表现最佳。

Conclusion: 纠错线路应与逻辑操作协同设计：类时错误主导时优先foliation，类空错误主导时优先SWAP类线路。

Abstract: Skip-gate leakage --- such as atom loss in neutral-atom arrays or trapped ions, or photon loss in superconducting dual-rail qubits --- takes a qubit out of its computational subspace and removes subsequent gates acting on it. If leakage is only detectable end-of-the-line at qubit measurement, error correction circuits must be designed to periodically measure all qubits, as in SWAP-based circuits and foliation. We show that the best SWAP-based surface code, although optimal against space-like skip-gate leakage errors, is suboptimal against time-like leakage errors. By optimal, we mean that the code corrects as many leakage errors as it can detect. Remarkably, we find that the foliated surface code is optimal against both space- and time-like error configurations. We support our findings with numerical simulations. Under two-qubit gate leakage with probability 0.5%, foliation suppresses time-like errors in a distance-4 rotated stability experiment by 1380(50) per two added rounds, compared to 63(1) for the best SWAP-based circuit. In memory, the two are comparable. Under an experimentally motivated neutral-atom noise model, we find that foliation outperforms SWAP-based circuits in stability for both rotated and unrotated code geometries. This holds true for a parameter sweep that takes the neutral-atom noise model from being dominated by computational errors to atom loss. Interestingly, a SWAP-based circuit for unrotated code memory performs best when computational noise dominates over loss. Our work motivates co-designing error correction circuits with logical operations: preferring foliation when time-like errors dominate and SWAP-based circuits when space-like errors dominate.

</details>


### [3] [Mitigating large circuits with a learned small-circuit mitigator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28261)
*Thi Ha Kyaw,Pablo Díez-Valle,Gaurav Saxena*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出用电路切割(circuit cutting)实现学习式量子误差缓解模型的复用：在小电路上训练一次的mer缓解器可被应用到比训练电路更大、更深的电路上，无需额外电路或噪声放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式量子误差缓解依赖精确训练标签，但标签计算成本随电路规模急剧上升；同时模型难以泛化到训练分布之外的深度与规模电路。需要在无精确标签的大电路场景下使用已训练模型。

Method: 将大电路切割为若干小片段(fragments)，把在小电路上训练的误差缓解器以fragment-wise方式应用于各片段并重构结果。与零噪声外推(ZNE)、Clifford数据回归(CDR)、概率误差消除(PEC)在模拟电路上对比，测试电路宽度与深度为训练电路数倍；并在固定shot预算下的全息Ising链本征求解器上评估。

Result: 在随机电路链上，fragment-wise缓解器消除了重构误差的绝大部分；全息Ising链本征求解场景下恢复约90%或更多的噪声诱导能量误差，优于CDR与PEC。因模型一次训练、推理时无需额外电路或噪声放大，成本前置而非每个新电路重复支付。

Conclusion: 电路切割可使学习式误差缓解扩展到超出精确训练标签可达范围的量子计算，前提是电路能以中等数量切割划分；方法具备模块性，小电路缓解器的改进（如在大片段或器件特定噪声上重训练）可直接迁移到所有可切分为此类片段的电路。

Abstract: Learning-based quantum error mitigation infers noise-free results from noisy measurement data. However, exact training labels become prohibitively expensive to compute as circuits grow, and the generalization capability of a trained model to infer accurate results on circuits of depths and sizes it was not trained on poses a challenge. We show that circuit cutting allows such a model to be reused on circuits larger than those it was trained on. We benchmark this approach against zero-noise extrapolation, Clifford data regression, and probabilistic error cancellation on simulated circuits several times wider and deeper than the training circuits. On random-circuit chains, the fragment-wise mitigator removes most of the reconstructed error. For a holographic Ising-chain eigensolver under a fixed shot budget, it recovers most of the noise-induced energy error, close to 90\% or more, and outperforms Clifford data regression and probabilistic error cancellation. Since the mitigator is trained once and needs no additional circuits or noise amplification at inference, its cost is paid up front rather than for every new circuit. The approach is also modular. Any improvement to the small-circuit mitigator, such as retraining on larger fragments or on device-specific noise, carries over directly to every circuit that can be cut into such fragments. Our proposal thus offers a practical route to bringing learned error mitigation to quantum computations far beyond the reach of exact training labels, provided they can be partitioned with a moderate number of cuts.

</details>


### [4] [Comment on phase vortex lattices in neutron interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28316)
*Wolfgang Treimer,Terrence Jach,Martin suda,Frank Haußer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对四板晶体中子干涉仪（LLLL几何）给出基于相位的理论描述，将其视为两个共享部分光路的耦合LLL干涉环，推导前向强度并复现余弦和结构；并指出参考文献[2]报道的"相位涡旋光栅/OAM"现象可完全由两垂直楔形相位物体引起的相干光路叠加模拟解释，无需引入中子OAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heinrich等提出的四板LLLL中子干涉仪可视为两个耦合的LLL干涉环，具有超越标准三板构型的干涉特性但缺乏系统的理论描述；同时文献[2]据周期性二维强度阵列宣称演示了具有显著外禀轨道角动量（OAM）的相位涡旋光栅，该解释需要严格检验。

Method: 采用相干分束模型，将LLLL干涉仪分解为两个共享部分光路的耦合LLL干涉环，用各环相位和表示前向强度；分析局域相位物体（如插入单个分束中的楔形片）导致的相位依赖，并对不同干涉环光束路径的相干叠加（含两相互垂直楔形引起的环路相移）进行模拟。

Result: 推导出的前向强度以环路相位和表达，复现了文献[1,2]中的余弦和结构；模拟表明，仅由两个相互垂直楔形在各环路引入相移的相干路径叠加即可重现文献[2]观察到的二维周期强度阵列，无需假设中子具有OAM。

Conclusion: LLLL干涉仪的干涉图样可由耦合环的相位和理论完整描述；文献[2]所宣称的相位涡旋光栅与显著外禀OAM的证据不足，其观测图样可由不含OAM的相干路径叠加模型解释，对中子OAM的实验判据提出质疑。

Abstract: We present a phase-based theoretical description of the four-plate crystal neutron interferometer (LLLL geometry) originally introduced by Heinrich et al. [Z. Phys. B 72, 357 (1988)]. The LLLL interferometer can be interpreted as two coupled LLL interferometric loops that share partial beam paths, leading to characteristic interference phenomena beyond the standard three-plate configuration. Using a coherent beam-splitting model we derive the forward intensity in terms of loop phase sums and recover the cosine-sum structure reported in Refs.[1,2]. Based on these results we analyze the phase dependence of the forward beam in the presence of localized phase objects, such as wedges inserted into individual partial beams. In experiments presented in [2], the LLLL interferometer is used to generate a periodic array of intensity in two dimensions by which the authors claim to demonstrate a phase vortex grating of significant extrinsic orbital angular momentum (OAM). However, the same interference patterns are predicted by simulating a coherent superposition of {beam paths} along different interferometer loops and experiencing phase shifts in each loop due to two wedges that are perpendicular to each other, without assuming any neutron OAM.

</details>


### [5] [Distant Bell States via Measured Spin Chain Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28325)
*Ralph Jason Costales,Sougato Bose*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过在自旋链幺正演化中穿插对中间自旋的测量，可加速远程纠缠建立，并在单次使用通道后获得可升级为Bell态的纯纠缠态，无需纯化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋链非平衡幺正动力学常用作片上量子通信通道，但通常只在链两端产生混合纠缠态，需多次使用通道并进行纠缠纯化才能得到Bell态，效率低、复杂度高。

Method: 在1D XY自旋链的幺正演化过程中间歇性地对中间自旋施加测量，考察两类测量：后选择基态的测量，以及计算基下的局域测量；以耦合到链两端的量子比特为例说明机制。

Result: 该方案既加快了纠缠建立速度，又能在通道单次使用后产生纯纠缠态，该态可直接升级为Bell态而无需纠缠纯化。

Conclusion: 测量穿插的链动力学为远程Bell态制备提供了一条更快速、更简洁的途径，可服务于量子网络与分布式量子计算。

Abstract: Preparing distant qubits in Bell states is a core requirement for quantum networking and distributed quantum computation. The nonequilibrium unitary dynamics of spin chains have been envisaged as a convenient quantum channel for intra-chip communications. Typically it establishes mixed entangled states between its remote ends, to be purified by entanglement distillation after several uses of the channel to obtain Bell states. Here we show how the establishment of entanglement via spin chain dynamics can be (a) sped up and (b) made to yield a pure entangled state after a single usage of the channel, which is upgradable to a Bell-state without the complexity of purification. To this end, we intersperse unitary evolution for some period with measurements on intermediary spins. We explore two different measurements: a measurement that post-selects for a ground state; and local measurements in the computational basis. We illustrate the mechanism with qubits coupled to the ends of a 1D $XY$ spin chain.

</details>


### [6] [Tailored Magic States for Algorithmic Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28353)
*Matthew Steinberg,Joseph Sullivan,Chunjun Cao,Michael Perlin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出CSS-T-Product (CSS-TP) 码，一种基于码理论的synthillation方法，用横向单比特相位门实现多比特逻辑对角门，并据其构造定制magic工厂，为具体算法原语（GF(2^8)乘法器、CLA加法器）显著降低延迟与时空开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法原语（加法器、乘法器、布尔逻辑）含有大量多量子比特非Clifford门簇，分解为T与CCZ门会带来调度与时空开销。现有synthillation仅针对对角电路在Clifford层级第三层联合合成与蒸馏非Clifford内容，缺乏与码构造相联系的通用框架。

Method: 引入CSS-TP码：其横向单比特相位门实现多量子比特逻辑对角门；给出基于张量网络运算从码结构预测逻辑门的解析规则；利用quantum LEGO生成作用在Clifford层级第三、四层的码族；将所得资源态分类为规范态（如T、CCZ）、规范态张量积或不可约态（无法经Clifford操作与逻辑基变换化简为乘积）；将码转换为Encode-T-Decode工厂，并用基于表面码的模型在若干算法原语上分析。

Result: 定制magic工厂在相近或更小时空体积下取得显著延迟改善：码距21、每资源族一个工厂时，GF(2^8)乘法器延迟降低至多48%，共享加数超前进位加法器降低至多38%；最小的定制工厂还降低了占用时空体积。同时恢复了已知synthillation资源，并制备了更高Clifford层级的magic态。

Conclusion: CSS-TP码把码构造与面向特定算法原语的资源工厂联系起来，为synthillation提供了码理论视角下的互补方法，实现按需定制的低延迟magic工厂。

Abstract: Algorithmic primitives such as adders, multipliers, and Boolean logic typically contain clusters of multi-qubit non-Clifford gates, whose decomposition into $T$ and $CCZ$ gates adds scheduling and spacetime overhead in fault-tolerant quantum computers. Synthillation addresses this difficulty for diagonal circuits at the third level of the Clifford hierarchy by jointly synthesizing and distilling their non-Clifford content. In this work, we develop a complementary, code-theoretic approach to synthillation. We introduce CSS-T-Product (CSS-TP) codes, whose transversal single-qubit phase gates implement multi-qubit logical diagonal gates, and give analytical rules that predict these logical gates from tensor-network operations. Using quantum LEGO, we generate code families with logical actions at the third and fourth levels of the Clifford hierarchy. We classify the resulting resource states as canonical (such as $T$ or $CCZ$), tensor products of canonical states, or irreducible, i.e., not reducible to a product of non-Clifford gates via Clifford operations and logical basis changes. Conversion into Encode-$T$-Decode factories built from these codes recover known synthillation resources and prepare magic states at higher levels of the Clifford hierarchy; we analyze them with a surface-code-based model for several algorithmic primitives. We demonstrate that tailored magic factories give significant latency improvements at similar or smaller occupied spacetime volume: at code distance $21$ with one factory per resource family, they reduce the latency of a $GF(2^{8})$ multiplier and a shared-addend carry-lookahead adder by up to $48\%$ and $38\%$, respectively, and the smallest tailored factory also lowers the occupied spacetime volume. CSS-TP codes thus connect code construction to resource factories for specific algorithmic primitives.

</details>


### [7] [Incompressible quantum information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28361)
*Lasse H. Wolff,Matthias Christandl,Bergfinnur Durhuus,Christoph Hirche,Andreas Winter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子情形下"压缩大系统而不损失相关性"的经典结论不成立：存在二分量子态，对大系统的任何非平凡压缩都会以互信息度量破坏显著相关性，从而揭示量子机器学习中信息瓶颈方法的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典关联系统中，与大系统相关的小系统可把大系统压缩到仅依赖小系统维度的规模而不损失关联（误差可控）。作者追问这一结论在量子系统是否成立，并由此考察量子机器学习中信息瓶颈范式的适用边界。

Method: 构造并分析二分量子态，考察对大系统做任意非平凡相对比例的压缩（维度约化）时互信息的变化，给出下界论证说明相关性必然大幅损失；与经典情形做对照。

Result: 证明存在二分量子态，使得对大系统的任何非平凡压缩都必然导致互信息意义下显著相关性的破坏；即经典的可压缩性陈述在量子情形下完全失效。

Conclusion: 量子大系统不存在经典的"压缩保持关联"保证；仅用一个指定相关特征的小系统并不足以保证存在保持相关性的紧致编码，这对基于信息瓶颈的量子机器学习方法构成原理性限制。

Abstract: For a small classical system correlated with a large one, it is always possible to compress the large system down to a size only dependent on the size of the smaller one, while preserving all correlations up to a given controllable error. Here, we show that such a statement does not hold at all when the large system is quantum mechanical. More precisely, we show that there are bipartite states such that any compression of the large system, by a non-trivial relative factor, will necessarily lead to the destruction of a significant amount of correlation, measured in mutual information. Our results therefore identify a limitation of information-bottleneck approaches to quantum machine learning, showing that a small system specifying the relevant features contained in a large quantum data set does not guarantee a compact encoding of the latter that preserves the relevant correlations.

</details>


### [8] [Online Scheduling for Entanglement Distillation: Learning When to Wait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28373)
*Panagiotis Promponas,Spyros Tserkis,Leandros Tassiulas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究纠缠蒸馏的在线调度问题：动态构建蒸馏树，并对每次蒸馏操作决定是否等待结果，以最大化满足目标保真度的纠缠对吞吐量；给出吞吐量上界，用MDP+强化学习训练可适用于任意存储器数量的策略，性能不劣于择优的极端基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠蒸馏以消耗纠缠对数量为代价概率性提升保真度；每次蒸馏结果需通信时间，期间空闲纠缠对在存储器中退相干。因此面临两难：等待结果再决策，还是乐观地继续（风险是白白消耗更多纠缠对）。需研究在线调度策略以最大化满足保真度要求的纠缠对吞吐量。

Method: 将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程；策略需决定哪些纠缠对配对蒸馏，以及每次操作是否等待其结果；先推导所有调度策略的吞吐量上界，再用强化学习训练可推广到任意存储器数量的策略。

Result: 学到策略与"总是等待"和"从不等待"两个基线相比：当某一极端最优时，其吞吐量与之相当；否则更优。

Conclusion: 在线蒸馏调度中"是否等待"的决策需自适应，强化学习得到的策略可泛化到任意规模存储器并优于固定极端策略，同时理论上界为性能评估提供基准。

Abstract: Entanglement distillation probabilistically increases the fidelity of entangled pairs at the cost of reducing their number. Compared with two baselines that always or never wait, the learned policy delivers as many pairs of the required fidelity as the better one when one of these two extremes is the best choice, and more otherwise.

The previous QBlue compiler verifies a Hamiltonian simulation compiler containing only Trotter-based algorithms. It takes a Hamiltonian written over Pauli strings, a simulation time, and an error bound, and produces a circuit or analog schedule that is proven, in the Rocq proof assistant, to approximate the ideal time evolution within that bound and proves a single end-to-end error bound that composes the errors of the individual stages; the compiler uses it to select, by resource estimation, the cheapest algorithm that meets the user's bound. This paper advances the verification efforts with two further steps. First, we extend QBlue to $\textsf{QBlue}_{\texttt{BE}}$ by verifying another important compilation method for Hamiltonian simulation: block-encoding-based (BE-based) algorithms. Second, we empirically evaluate 279 Hamiltonian simulation programs against the state-of-the-art BE-based compiler, PennyLane. For many programs, our compiler shows a $94\%$ gate reduction compared to PennyLane. Additionally, we also discover issues and bugs in PennyLane. We also compare Trotter- and BE-based algorithms, providing a practical comparison for the long-standing debate over which algorithms perform better. Our results surprisingly show that Trotter-based algorithms may gain a practical advantage, even though BE algorithms have a theoretical advantage.

</details>

However, quantum processors could frequently stall due to slow classical error-correction decoders. Extending speculative execution to the quantum side is fundamentally challenging. Unlike classical processor states, quantum states cannot be arbitrarily copied or checkpointed for rollback, making recovery from mispredictions difficult. In this work, we propose Froq to enable safe speculative execution on live quantum states. Froq uses compiler-assisted recovery contracts to track how mispredictions propagate through quantum operations. It further employs a resource-aware runtime mechanism to reserve recovery workspace and dynamically balance the benefits of speculative execution against the expected costs of recovery. We evaluate Froq on eight FTQC programs across 88 configurations, building on an existing speculative window decoder. Froq reduces quantum-side time by 9.81% across all 88 configurations. The reduction reaches 18.34% across the 45 configurations allowing concurrent predictions, and peaks at 31.1% at code distance d=21, under limited resources.

</details>

Abstract: An unknown quantum state can supply the information needed to control a computation, but how much quantum data must be consumed to perform a prescribed action? No-signaling requires identical observable predictions for remotely chosen ensemble preparations of the same complete input; quantum distinguishability then converts this requirement into finite-copy bounds. For amplitude amplification, we identify an exact low-copy obstruction and establish a dimension-independent, optimal quadratic dependence of the copy cost on angular gain at fixed accuracy, uniformly over arbitrary pure inputs. A coherent protocol attains this scaling without reconstructing a classical state description. Prior knowledge of a real two-level family additionally permits exponentially decreasing error with the copy budget at fixed gain. These results separate the resources needed to perform a specified quantum operation from those needed for universal state reconstruction. The central connection is operational: physical consistency restricts which distinctions a controller can expose, and the information carried by its complete input determines the cost of doing so. This links a causal requirement to quantitative limits on converting quantum data into computational control.

</details>

Abstract: A source in the Schrodinger equation changes the norm of the guiding wave, apparently conflicting with the conservation of probability. In source-extended de Broglie-Bohm dynamics the conflict disappears: probability is carried by the particle ensemble, while the wavefunction guides its motion. The difficulty is then different. What does quantum equilibrium mean when the particle distribution remains normalized but the guiding density does not? We show that the departure has two independent components: a structural one, measured by the Kullback--Leibler divergence between the particle distribution and the normalized guiding density, and an amplitude one, measured by the Born weight. The Born rule holds precisely when both are equilibrated. Their evolution is governed by the complex source-to-wavefunction ratio; scale covariance further distinguishes an independently prescribed source, against which the amplitude of the wave matters, from state-dependent generators for which rescaling remains a symmetry. We develop a source-aware analytical--computational method, combining exact or semi-analytical guiding fields with Bohmian trajectory transport, ensemble quadrature and independent numerical checks. Free-space impulses expose the limits of global diagnostics and an exact jump rule; a driven ring has constant Born weight but either periodic or secular structural departure; oscillators show both source-generated structural change and amplitude-only evolution, including a finite-horizon weak-drive plateau far below its frozen-particle value; and a coupled two-body model exhibits nonlocal guidance and evolving particle entanglement. The source-free, initially equilibrated sector recovers standard quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [15] [Quantum nonclassicality as a marginal problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28674)
*Karl Svozil*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子统计的经典解释（局域性、非语境性或因果性）之所以失效，根源同一：不同语境下的概率分布并非某个单一联合分布的边缘分布。单个语境均可经典建模，这正是Boole的“可能经验条件”问题；Garg与Mermin将其视为线性问题，借助Farkas引理证明可解性等价于一组有限的Bell型不等式，Pitowsky则给出关联多面体形式。论文梳理这一共同结构，并讨论弹性带模型与因果数据融合两个例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对量子统计非经典性的解释路径（局域隐变量、非语境性、因果模型）通常被分开处理，作者希望揭示它们失效的共同数学根源，从而统一理解各类Bell型定理。

Method: 把“不同语境分布能否来自单一联合分布”的形式化为Boole问题，采用Garg–Mermin的线性规划视角与Farkas引理，将可解性等价为对应凸多面体锥每条极射线一条Bell型不等式，并以Pitowsky的关联多面体刻画可行域。

Result: 刻画了统一结构后应用于两个案例：Aerts弹性带模型在单一语境中复现量子余弦关联，但语境合并后受经典界约束；在因果数据融合中，非经典性源于对因果结构施加干预所得的多个语境，其数据无法由单一联合分布给出。

Conclusion: 各类非经典性可归结为同一“边缘一致性问题”：局部经典模型的存在性与联合分布的存在性等价，并可由Bell型不等式的有限族完全刻画；该框架为弹性带模型、因果数据融合等看似不同的现象提供统一诊断工具。

Abstract: Classical explanations of quantum statistics---local, noncontextual, or causal---fail for one and the same reason: the probability distributions observed in different contexts are not the marginals of a single joint distribution. Every single context admits a classical model. This is Boole's problem of the "conditions of possible experience." Garg and Mermin treated it as a linear problem and showed, via Farkas's lemma, that its solvability is equivalent to a finite list of Bell-type inequalities, one for each extreme ray of a convex polyhedral cone; Pitowsky gave it the form of correlation polytopes. We sketch this common structure and discuss two examples: Aerts's elastic-band model, which reproduces the quantum cosine in one context but obeys the classical bound when contexts are combined; and nonclassicality in causal data fusion, where the contexts are supplied by interventions on a causal structure.

</details>

Abstract: Bivariate bicycle quantum low-density parity-check codes combine efficient quantum storage with a rich Abelian anyon structure, providing a compelling platform for exploring the interplay between quantum error correction and topological order. A central challenge is how to realize domain walls that host non-Abelian twist defects at their endpoints. Here we explicitly construct electric-magnetic duality walls for arbitrary bivariate bicycle codes with finite anyon content using composite, interblock, and patch checks. These checks remove the unwanted local modes while preserving charge conversion, ensuring that topologically contractible closed ribbons act trivially on encoded states. For each fixed code, check weights and interaction ranges remain bounded independently of wall length. The wall endpoints have quantum dimension $2^{Q/2}$, where $Q$ counts the independent generators in each electric or magnetic charge space. As an application, we illustrate measurement-only braiding for $Q=3$ under the correspondence between measurements and defect transport. Our construction provides a valuable basis for exploring non-Abelian defect physics and developing logical operations in quantum low-density parity-check codes with Abelian topological order in future.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 非厄米趋肤效应通过塑造右/左本征态的空间轮廓来决定相位刚度的标度规律，从而可在没有例外点的情况下调控本征态的非正交性。

Abstract: Phase rigidity vanishes as a power law near exceptional points, reflecting the coalescence of eigenstates at these points. In this Letter, we show that non-Hermitian skin effects induce distinct asymptotic behaviors of phase rigidity without eigenstate coalescence. To demonstrate this, we derive exact analytical results for three non-Hermitian chain models and verify them numerically. For the ordinary skin effect, phase rigidity decays exponentially with increasing system size $N$, as the right and left eigenstates are exponentially localized at opposite boundaries. For the scale-free skin effect, phase rigidity exhibits an $N^{-2}$ power-law approach to a nonzero constant as the right and left eigenstates have decay lengths proportional to $N$. For the algebraic skin effect studied here, phase rigidity can exhibit an $N^{-1/2}$ power-law decay to zero, with the right eigenstate algebraically localized near a boundary and the left eigenstate spread across the chain. These findings show that non-Hermitian skin effects govern phase-rigidity scaling by shaping the spatial profiles of the right and left eigenstates, enabling control of eigenstate nonorthogonality without exceptional points.

</details>

Conclusion: 以诚实设备流形为基准的序贯检验框架可显著提升低误报率下的攻击检出与泛化能力，D*给出延迟的普适下界（对滤波后向反射的木马探测趋于消失），并在真实量子处理器上验证了可行性与信息-扰动理论一致性。

Abstract: Practical quantum key distribution (QKD) is secure only while its devices match the assumptions of the security proof, and implementation attacks exploit mismatches while keeping the quantum bit error rate (QBER) low. We formulate attack detection in decoy-state BB84 as a sequential test against the honest-device manifold, the set of receiver statistics an attack-free link can produce under arbitrary channel loss. The resulting monitor, Q-TRACE (Quantum Telemetry Residuals Against Calibrated Expectations), calibrates a physical model of each link on commissioning data, profiles out the transmittance, and combines omnibus residual, physics score and setting-allocation tests with drift-robust references; no attack data are used. The formulation also yields a detectability exponent D* that lower-bounds the delay of any telemetry monitor and vanishes for a Trojan-horse probe with filtered back-reflection. On a count-level twin with model mismatch, over five held-out seeds and at a per-window false-alarm rate of 10^-3, Q-TRACE detects 63.6% of links under known attacks, 28.0% under unseen attack families and 65.3% under composite attacks, versus at most 51.2%, 6.7% and 42.3% for industry monitors, unsupervised and quantum-kernel detectors and a supervised classifier. Its delay attains the bound for strongly detectable attacks. On 33 parallel BB84 links of the IBM Heron processor ibm_marrakesh with physically executed entangling and amplitude-damping eavesdroppers, the measured information-disturbance relations agree with theory; a commissioning reference acquired under different operating conditions triggers alarms in every monitor tested, while a condition-matched reference detects a probe with 0.3% ideal QBER increase on 14 of 14 link-epochs with no false alarm on 66 honest ones.

Method: 以密度矩阵单侧与双侧微扰响应分别定义相干与非相干 Liouvillian 准粒子；设计将系统与辅助能级耦合的谱学协议（相干耦合或耗散脉冲），测量复数准粒子能量；对含 skin effect 的 Liouvillian 做数值模拟验证。

Conclusion: 工作为开放量子多体系统中相干与非相干准粒子的理论描述与实验探测提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Quasiparticles play a central role in understanding closed quantum many-body systems, as they reduce complex many-body problems to effective single-particle descriptions and connect directly to spectroscopic measurements. In open quantum systems, the interplay between coherent dynamics and dissipation gives rise to unique phenomena, such as the Liouvillian skin effect and strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking. This raises a fundamental question: how can the quasiparticle picture be extended to open quantum systems to account for such phenomena and enable the experimental probing of quasiparticle spectra? Here we introduce coherent and incoherent Liouvillian quasiparticles, defined by responses to perturbations acting on one side and both sides of the density matrix, respectively. We propose spectroscopy protocols that couple the system to auxiliary levels, either coherently or via a dissipative pulse, to measure the corresponding complex quasiparticle energies. Numerical simulations of Liouvillians with skin effects validate these protocols, revealing nontrivial spectral winding associated with unidirectional transport in real space. Notably, coherent and incoherent quasiparticles can propagate in opposite directions within the same system. Furthermore, in models exhibiting weak and strong-to-weak $U(1)$ spontaneous symmetry breaking, our results unveil gapless Nambu-Goldstone modes in the coherent and incoherent quasiparticle spectra, respectively; the other quasiparticle spectrum remains gapped in each case. Our work provides a unified framework for theoretically describing and experimentally probing coherent and incoherent quasiparticles in open quantum many-body systems.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We consider damped driven version of the Jaynes-Cummings equation which describes quantised one-mode Maxwell field coupled to a two-level molecule. The pumping and dissipation operator are polynomial in the creation and annihilation operators. The structure of the dissipation operator corresponds to the theory of completely positive trace preserving operators due to Lindblad, Kossakowski and others. We construct examples of {\it locked states} which are stationary states of the corresponding averaged dynamics in the interaction picture. The states are nonnegative Hermitian density operators with finite Hilbert-Schmidt norm. The calculations rely on the recurrence equation for diagonal entries.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We prove that, in the normalized-Laplacian model, perfect state transfer between two vertices of a tree can only happen if they are at distance at most $2$. We show that a conjecture of Coutinho and Baptista, stating that the only trees admitting perfect state transfer are $K_2$ and $P_3$, is true when the two vertices are at odd distance.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构感知优化器多采用基于坐标的范式，依赖量子采样得到的代价函数经典近似做单参数更新；其在大规模真实量子硬件上的表现尚未被验证。ICD虽将此类优化器统一为一个框架，但需预设插值频率集，该频率集理论上可推导但代价函数通常稀疏、往往需人工调参。

Abstract: Structure-aware optimizers have been the focus of recent study for the optimization of parameters in variational quantum circuits. Many of these optimizers follow a coordinate-based paradigm, making single parameter updates by using classical approximations to the cost function generated from quantum sampling. However, the performance of structure-aware optimizers on real quantum hardware at large system size has yet to be demonstrated. Recently, Interpolation-based Coordinate Descent (ICD) has been proposed to unify many of these optimizers into one framework. ICD requires a preset frequency set for interpolation, which can be theoretically derived, but for which the cost function is generally sparse over and may require manual tuning. To address these issues, we propose Frequency-learning Interpolation-based Coordinate Descent (FLICD) to adaptively learn frequencies online, allowing easier scaling up to larger systems. We demonstrate that our algorithm is more accurate than an existing zeroth-order method COBYLA and requires fewer quantum resources than ICD on simulator. Using IBM quantum hardware, we execute FLICD on systems up to 100 qubits and observe robust performance with an increasing number of layers.

Abstract: Charging a quantum battery requires both an energy source and access to a channel through which that energy can be transferred. If that access depends on a quantum state, charging becomes conditional on the state while the external controls remain unchanged. Abstract: Below a threshold noise rate, error correction lets larger quantum memories store a qubit for longer, without limit. Can the noise rate alone tell us whether a given noisy circuit does this? We construct fixed local circuits for which no algorithm can, already in one dimension under single-qubit noise. Each circuit is translation-invariant and time-periodic, with depolarizing noise on every wire after every layer, including all classical control. A computable list of disjoint rate intervals is indexed by the inputs $n$ of a universal Turing machine; local noise statistics select the input that the circuit simulates, and halting triggers erasure. If the machine runs forever on $n$, a qubit is stored throughout the $n$-th interval at entanglement fidelity at least $15/16$ for a time that grows without bound with size: linearly on rings, exponentially in six dimensions. The encoding and decoding maps are noiseless and global; they depend on $n$, the system size and, for decoding, the time, but not on the rate. If it halts, the fidelity of every encoding falls below $1/2+η$, for any fixed $η>0$, after a time independent of size and rate. The labels of the intervals with unbounded storage form a $Π^0_1$-complete set, and intervals of both kinds accumulate at zero, so these circuits have no noise threshold. Without rate selection, the ring construction is a translation-invariant, time-periodic memory: from an initialized classical hierarchy it stores a qubit for a time linear in the ring length at every sufficiently small rate, addressing the memory part of a one-dimensional question raised by Dünnweber, Styliaris and Trivedi. These are existence results: registers and interaction ranges are very large and the rates astronomically small.

Abstract: Trap-based verification of blind quantum computation hides test qubits among the positions of a measurement pattern, and existing protocols choose the trap configuration without regard to the delegated computation. Since the client cannot know how damage from several positions combines, we solve the game for all monotone subadditive damage functions consistent with $w$ at once. In a single round, placing traps in proportion to $w$ guarantees soundness error $1-c/\|w\|_1$ and is optimal up to a factor $1+\max_iw_i$, while the optimistic solution can fail completely. Over $T$ test rounds, the error decays exponentially against a server that repeats the same deviation, but a general server can keep it at $Θ(\|w\|_1/(cT))$, and against such servers the damage-weighted placement is exactly optimal. Damage profiles follow from the flow of the pattern: for brickwork patterns with $m=4h+1$ columns, only $(2h+1)^2$ positions can affect a decision qubit, whatever the width. With $k$ traps per round, placing them on this set reduces the number of test rounds by the same factor as for a single trap; with unrestricted trap sets and flat damage we recover the known optimality of fractional coloring. Finally, trap statistics certify the aggregate damage with a number of rounds independent of the number of positions $N$, whereas locating the damage requires $Ω(Nε^{-2}\logδ^{-1})$ rounds.

Abstract: A parameter-dependent physical inner product makes the comparison of quantum states at different parameter points non-unique. We formulate two geometric prescriptions for this comparison in quasi-Hermitian systems. The Type I QGT is defined by an inner-product-compatible covariant derivative and horizontal projection. The Type II QGT is constructed from the endpoint overlap of physical dual states and is invariant under local $GL(1,\mathbb{C})$ rescalings. We show that, on a normalized section, it coincides with the established left--right (LR) QGT, while providing an endpoint-overlap interpretation of that tensor. Our central result is an exact relation between the two constructions: in the positive Dyson frame, the Type I QGT is the positive Gram block of the dressed-derivative sector, while the real symmetric part of the Type II QGT is this metric minus a positive-semidefinite contribution from the metric connection; the full complex tensor also contains cross terms. This relation clarifies the origin of the possible indefiniteness of the Type II metric and separates it from the positivity of the physical Hilbert-space metric. We further distinguish instantaneous Dyson mappings from the fixed inner-product-preserving dynamics and characterize the conditions under which the corresponding QGTs agree. Three two-level models illustrate these results. The physical content of the two tensors is then identified through the energy uncertainty and evolution speed for Type I, and the symmetrized endpoint overlap, complex holonomy, and signed dynamical response for Type II.

Abstract: Thermofield dynamics (TFD) describes finite-temperature quantum relaxation through a purified wavefunction, without sampling thermally populated initial states. Here, we explore TFD approaches tailored to anharmonic potentials and compatible with efficient tensor network methods for quantum propagation. We show that the conceptually appealing route of converting a harmonic-reference thermal state into the target anharmonic state by imaginary-time propagation can be numerically ill-conditioned. As a practical alternative, we formulate a multilayer multiconfiguration time-dependent Hartree (ML-MCTDH) protocol that separates thermal-state preparation from the harmonic-reference Bogoliubov transformation. We prepare the thermal purification in the bare representation and then apply finite-basis Bogoliubov unitaries to the physical--auxiliary mode pairs. For independent bath modes, direct construction of the thermal amplitude provides a complementary initialization procedure. We illustrate this protocol through vibrational relaxation and spatial-superposition decoherence of a harmonic subsystem coupled to a Morse-oscillator bath. We find non-monotonic energy relaxation, shifts in the dominant resonant response, and oscillatory energy exchange at zero and finite temperature. For decoherence, we recover the expected harmonic-bath scaling and find a pronounced non-monotonic dependence of the decay rate on anharmonicity at finite temperature in a low-frequency model, with a weaker dependence in a high-frequency model.

Result: 推理时间复杂度对隐藏维度的依赖由多项式降为对数，呈指数优势；量子硬件推理实测获得300-1000倍加速；序列到序列任务上的测量验证了其学习能力；训练算法复杂度仅为当前最优量子线路训练算法的25%，且参数规模增大时该比例进一步快速下降。

The dependency between the inference time complexity and the model's hidden dimension has been reduced from polynomial to logarithmic, showing an exponential advantage. The inference executions on the quantum hardware verify our model's 300-1000 times speed-up, and the measurements on the sequence-to-sequence tasks shows its learning ability. The training algorithm achieves only 25% complexity of the best SOTA quantum circuit training algorithms, especially this ratio decreases fast as the parameter scale increases. Our work not only proposes a quantum large model that can significantly reduce the computational cost, but also promotes the quantum computation towards general computing.

</details>

Each block is therefore a standard fixed-length protocol, and composable finite-key security against coherent attacks follows from sequential composition; an inaccurate channel model reduces the key rate but does not weaken security. The controller estimates turbulence from observed error rates, maximizes the expected key under forecast uncertainty, and recalibrates its model online. We evaluate it on a simulated entanglement-based orbital-angular-momentum link between an aircraft and a ground station, using Kolmogorov phase screens and the Hufnagel--Valley turbulence profile. Over night, dawn, daytime, and model-mismatch scenarios, the recalibrated controller retains at least 98.8\% of the key obtained with the best fixed dimension for each scenario, while every fixed dimension drops to 73.5\% or less in at least one scenario. Abstract: We develop a direct quantum simulation algorithm for finite homotopy approximations to the non-isothermal compressible Navier-Stokes equations. At Mach numbers 0.2 and 0.4, the fifth-order hierarchy reproduces viscous dissipation over a longer interval than pressure-dilatation at the same relative tolerance. Together, the Taylor-Green energy budgets quantify the accuracy windows of each homotopy order, while coarse-grid circuits verify the signed readout and measure gate counts. For higher-order evolution on finer grids, merging coincident stencil terms and combining block-encoded products along strictly increasing homotopy-order paths substantially reduce the predicted normalisation cost.

Abstract: The combinatorial growth of network configurations under N-x contingencies presents significant computational challenges for power system analysis. The proposed framework encodes transmission line availability in a quantum register and employs controlled Hamiltonian evolution to account for topology changes induced by contingencies. Numerical experiments on an 8-bus power network under N-2 contingencies demonstrate the implementation of the algorithm and characterize the convergence of the resulting centrality estimates with respect to the observation window, sampling and Trotter steps. Comparison with established classical centrality measures shows a qualitatively consistent characterization of network structure, particularly in identifying the most central nodes. The proposed framework establishes a methodological foundation for quantum-walk-based analysis of power networks under contingencies, providing a starting point for future investigations of quantum algorithms for power system security assessment.

Abstract: We prove a no-go theorem for exponentially long-lived thermal memories, based on the structure of redundancies among Hamiltonian terms. Yet glassy dynamics can still sustain long-lived memories even without a thermodynamic transition, as demonstrated by LDPC codes on non-Euclidean graphs. A longstanding question, exemplified by Haah's cubic code, is whether such a mechanism can also support passive (quantum) memory in finite-dimensional Euclidean systems. We show that this route fails for local stabilizer Hamiltonians without local redundancies: at any constant nonzero temperature, local heat-bath dynamics is fast mixing, with $O(N)$ mixing time, where $N$ is the number of qubits. We establish this by proving a strong decay of correlations property of the thermal Gibbs state. We further prove rapid mixing with $O(\log N)$ mixing time under a stronger condition on induced redundancies in suitably chosen regions. This stronger result applies to several well-studied examples, including Haah's cubic code and its generalizations, as well as three-dimensional layer codes. Our work identifies redundancy structure as a key property for understanding thermal memories.

*Tanish Singh Rajpal,Michael Mattarock*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子网络究竟能对纠缠对保真度提供何种保证：随机秘密抽检可在无模型假设下给出保留对平均保真度的下界，但无法可靠保证"每一对"都达标；单对承诺必须依赖模型，作者给出在模型不乐观或预先声明裕度时限制用户遭遇坏对风险的规则，并用真实数据与36比特离子阱实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户无法在不破坏纠缠对的情况下检测其保真度，因此网络通常以物理模型而非测量来承诺每对的质量；若模型过于乐观，仅凭交付规则可能漏掉误差。作者因此追问：网络实际能保证什么，平均保证与逐对保证的边界在哪里。

Method: （1）理论：分析随机秘密抽检对保留对平均保真度的下界，并证明小比例抽检无法可靠证明每一对均达阈值；（2）给出针对任意数据驱动路由的逐用户风险上界规则，条件是模型不乐观或预先声明足够裕度；（3）在真实数据上评估校准噪声与证书强度随对数规模的变化；（4）在36比特离子阱处理器上以50个预注册任务运行协议，并测试对三、四比特纠缠态的适用性及对乐观模型的检出能力，全部确证性分析预注册并报告失败案例。

Result: 真实链路数据中校准噪声超出偶然范围，模拟网络中该量级的模型误差会破坏逐对承诺，除非预先声明裕度；在离子阱与金刚石链路的真实数据上，约3000对、千赫兹链路、20%抽检时中位证书为0.818，与事先预测的0.817吻合；36比特处理器50个预注册任务中证书与实测保真度相差在0.003以内，秘密损坏三分之一纠缠对时证书按预测幅度下降，且无需修改即可对三、四比特纠缠态出具证书；相同检验也能检出乐观模型，但速度较慢。

Conclusion: 量子网络可在无模型条件下证明保留对的平均保真度下界，但逐对保真度承诺本质上依赖模型假设；所提规则能在模型不乐观或预先声明裕度时给出用户风险上界。实验表明证书准确、可扩展并具鲁棒性，同时揭示对乐观模型的检测较为迟缓，凸显预注册与如实报告失败的重要性。

Abstract: Quantum networks will give users entangled pairs of qubits, the resource behind teleportation and quantum-secure keys. But a user cannot check a pair without destroying it, so networks promise the quality of each pair, its fidelity, from a physical model rather than from a measurement. If the model is too optimistic, the delivery rule alone may miss the error. We ask: what can a network actually guarantee? Our main result has two sides. By testing some pairs at random, in secret, users can put a lower bound on the average fidelity of the pairs they keep, without any model and at every moment. But when they test only a small fraction and keep most pairs, the tests cannot reliably show that every kept pair meets a fidelity threshold: a rare bad pair can still slip through. A promise about every single pair therefore rests on a model, and we give a rule that bounds each user's risk of ever receiving a bad pair under any data-driven routing, as long as the model is not optimistic or a large enough margin is declared in advance. Real data show calibration noise beyond chance, and in simulated networks a model error of the size seen in one experiment breaks the per-pair promise unless the margin was declared. On real data from trapped-ion and diamond links, the number of pairs decided how strong the certificate was: with about 3,000 pairs of a kilohertz link and 20% tested, the median certificate was 0.818, against the 0.817 we predicted in advance. We then ran the protocol ourselves on a 36-qubit trapped-ion processor in 50 pre-registered jobs. The certificate came within 0.003 of the measured fidelity, dropped by the predicted amount when we secretly damaged a third of the pairs, and, without changes, certified three- and four-qubit entangled states. The same tests also catch an optimistic model, but slowly. We pre-registered every confirmatory analysis and report the failures too.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 理论上，服务器最优区分优势等于停止时间分布的全变差距离，轮数是该模型下唯一残差信道；可靠性复合为 δ·E[T]。局部PL比率以1.7轮留一误差预测停止轮（常数预测器为10.3）。12/14配置泄漏显著，泄漏量0.14–1.68 bit，最大熵上界在所有配置中均高于测得值。防御方面：停止后机制只能增加轮数，DP填充需付出整个邻接半径的代价；固定公开预算（取中位运行长度）完全消除信道，但损失剩余能量间隙的10%；基于AboveThreshold的DP停止规则可保护所有实例对，但常被迫过早停止、代价较高。

Conclusion: 轮数计数是变分量子委托中真实且可量化的残差泄漏，且与优化过程的可预测统计量相关；不存在廉价的事后补救，泄漏的消除需以能量间隙损失（固定预算）或过早停止（DP停止）为代价，揭示了隐私与优化效用间的根本权衡。

Abstract: Blind quantum computation lets a client delegate a computation while hiding it from the server, up to a declared leakage. Its security definitions cover a single round. A variational quantum algorithm, however, delegates many rounds, and its classical optimizer decides from private data when to stop. We study what the resulting round count tells an honest-but-curious server. For protocols with one-time-padded transcripts and fresh randomness in every round, we prove that the server's best distinguishing advantage over the whole sequence equals the total-variation distance between the stopping-time distributions, so the round count is the only residual channel in this model. We also show that soundness composes as $δ\,\mathbb{E}[T]$ under adaptive stopping, given a conditional per-round soundness property that composably secure verifiable protocols provide. In simulated delegated eigensolvers for the transverse-field Ising chain, the local Polyak-Lojasiewicz ratio measured in iterations 5 to 15 predicts the noiseless stopping round with a leave-one-out error of 1.7 iterations, compared with 10.3 for a constant predictor. Across 14 configurations that differ in optimizer, stopping rule, initialization, system size, shot budget and problem family, the leakage is significant in 12 and ranges from 0.14 to 1.68 bits, and a maximum-entropy bound estimated on held-out sessions lies above it in all of them. For defense, we show that any mechanism acting after the stopping decision can only add rounds, and that differentially private padding costs the full adjacency radius. A fixed public iteration budget removes the channel completely; set at the median run length, it costs 10% of the remaining energy gap. A differentially private stopping rule based on AboveThreshold protects every pair of instances, but it must often stop too early, which makes it costly.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可观测的多体体系尺寸如何影响估计零温一阶相变点所需状态拷贝数，量化"允许更大体系"在样本复杂度上的收益，并给出极小极大意义下的最优资源标度。

Conclusion: 体系尺寸上限是相变点估计中的关键资源：尺寸受限导致指数级样本复杂度，适度放宽尺寸可实现指数级节省，并达到 Θ(ε⁻³) 的极小极大最优总自旋标度；零温与有限温的标度分别为 ε⁻³ 与 ε⁻²，基态与零温 Gibbs 情形同阶。

Abstract: We study how observed many-body system size affects the number of state copies needed to estimate a zero-temperature first-order transition point. The unknown Hamiltonian has bounded, translation-invariant on-site and nearest-neighbour interactions on a 3D cubic lattice with local dimension three. The field coupling is known, and all models share a known boundary field penalizing one local level. The limiting order parameter is uniformly separated across the transition. Each query returns an independently prepared Gibbs or ground state at an adaptive field and system size.
  For error $ε$ and fixed confidence, we prove an exponential separation between two size caps at the same temperature. There are absolute constants $0<c_0<C$ and $c_1>0$ such that, at inverse temperature $β=c_1/ε$, restricting side lengths to $L\le c_0/ε$ requires $\exp(Ω(1/ε))$ copies in worst-case expectation, even with collective measurements. Allowing $L\le C/ε$ permits $O(\log^2(1/ε))$ copies and $O(ε^{-3})$ total spins, using local measurements and polynomial classical postprocessing. The minimax expected total spin count is $Θ(ε^{-3})$ uniformly for $β\ge c_1/ε$. At fixed positive temperature it is $Θ_β(ε^{-2})$, attained from one- and eight-site Gibbs states followed by exponential classical computation. For ground-state copies it is $Θ(ε^{-3})$.

### [60] [Joint Dynamics of Spins and Frequency-Resolved Bosonic Occupations: A Projection-Operator Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29787)
*Khazret Sh. Meretukov,Alexander E. Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: The paper derives weak-coupling kinetic equations for frequency-resolved bosonic mode occupations in the spin-boson model, keeping mode occupations as independent dynamical variables coupled to spin observables, and solves them numerically for a single spin.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conventional approaches integrate out the bath completely, which discards the frequency-resolved information of bosonic mode occupations; the authors aim to retain these occupations as dynamical variables to access bath-side energy and occupation densities without exponential decomposition of the bath correlation function.

Method: A projection-operator construction closes the dynamics on a product-spin ansatz plus an undisplaced Gaussian environment with diagonal covariance. The framework also captures responses to structured spectra, nonthermal initial occupations, and slow (adiabatic) modulation of the coupling, and yields bath occupation/energy densities p(ω)δ⟨N_ω⟩(t) and ωp(ω)δ⟨N_ω⟩(t).

Conclusion: The approach provides a weak-coupling, frequency-resolved kinetic description of spin-boson dynamics, valid under diagonal Gaussian bath states and, for time-dependent couplings, the adiabatic regime; it reproduces HEOM spin dynamics and gives direct access to bath spectral redistribution.

Abstract: We derive weak-coupling kinetic equations for frequency-resolved bosonic mode occupations in the spin--boson model. Rather than integrating out the bath completely, we retain these mode occupations as independent dynamical variables whose equations are coupled to those for the spin observables. A projection-operator construction closes the dynamics on a product-spin ansatz and an undisplaced Gaussian environment with a diagonal covariance matrix. The equations are obtained to second order in the interaction parameter $λ$ for an arbitrary number of spins within this factorized closure, and are solved numerically for a single spin. A fixed-truncation calculation using the hierarchical equations of motion provides a benchmark for the spin dynamics with the Drude--Lorentz spectral density. The numerical results reveal a redistribution of bosonic mode occupations concentrated near the spin transition frequency $Ω=\sqrt{Δ^2+h^2}$. We also examine the response of the coupled spin--boson system to structured spectra, nonthermal initial occupations, and slow modulation of the coupling. The calculated mode occupations also allow changes in the bath-side occupation and energy densities $p(ω)δ\langle N_ω\rangle(t)$ and $ωp(ω)δ\langle N_ω\rangle(t)$ to be obtained without requiring an exponential decomposition of the bath correlation function. Its scope is restricted to weak coupling, diagonal Gaussian bath states, and, for time-dependent couplings, the adiabatic regime.

</details>


### [61] [When Does Fine-Tuning Beat Prompting for Qiskit-to-CUDA-Q Translation? An Execution-Verified Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29793)

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 基准度量的是源码级翻译而非执行后移植能力；微调是否划算是基座模型相关的，且普遍存在Qiskit能力回归，可通过混合重放缓解。语言模型已能做简单参数化代码的源码级翻译，但尚不能胜任真实程序。

Abstract: Moving quantum code from Qiskit to NVIDIA CUDA-Q is a practical adoption cost that language models are starting to automate. We ask whether a small open model can learn this translation, and how to measure it so that the result can be trusted. Both programs can be executed and their state vectors compared, so a kernel counts as correct only if it prepares the same state as the Qiskit reference (fidelity >= 0.999), with no text matching and no LLM judge. We build a 640-task benchmark with three test sets (larger circuits, an unseen circuit family, an unseen Qiskit coding style) and a QuantumKatas regression probe, and we test the evaluator before training. Measurement counts miss every phase-only error; a surface rule-based translator solves two of three original splits (text substitution); a converter that executes the Qiskit input and maps the circuit solves all 640 tasks -- so the benchmark measures source-level translation, not whether a circuit can be ported after running it. No base model emits a valid CUDA-Q kernel zero-shot. On Nemotron 3 Nano 4B, LoRA reaches 83.9%, tying a 4-shot prompt (83.1%), but generalizes worse to unseen style (48.5% vs. 65.5%) and cuts QuantumKatas from 23.8% to 8.9%; on Qwen2.5-Coder 7B, LoRA beats the prompt overall (92.0% vs. 82.8%) and on unseen style (78.5% vs. 65.0%), and on the 1.5B model as well, so whether fine-tuning pays off depends on the base model, while the Qiskit regression appears in all three. Mixing two replay formats is the best mitigation trade-off (84.4% translation, Katas 21.5%). On 48 parametric functions that must become kernels with arguments -- where executing the input cannot yield a parametric kernel -- models reach up to 83%; on 27 real QED-C kernels, every model scores 0%. Language models can do source-level translation of simple parametric code, but not yet of real programs.

</details>

*Jaeyun Jeong,Zehua Wang,Lilia Tightiz,Ki-Woong Park,Victor C. M. Leung*

Method: 每个密封单元为Bell对的一半，另一半存于受保护量子存储；秘密Pauli框架使任意攻击转化为Pauli信道；三层报告分别实现篡改定位、工具隐藏奇偶指纹学习、以及按到达顺序检测工具切换。结果按四类验证方给出，per-unit验证方不耦合密封单元且无未扰动参考，通过已知单奇偶强度≤1/3下界归约得到分离度。

Conclusion: 量子密封取证可同时实现篡改定位、工具指纹归因与工具切换检测，且分离度在per-unit验证方下呈指数级；归因有效性取决于攻击是否擦除指纹，实际收益受攻击类型与验证方能力类别共同制约。

Abstract: Quantum seals reveal that an object was disturbed, but not where, with which tool, or when the tool changed. We study quantum-memory forensics for sealed items returning through an untrusted network where one tool opens many. Each seal cell is half of a Bell pair whose partner the verifier keeps in a protected quantum memory, a secret Pauli frame turns any attack into a Pauli channel, and a three-tier report localizes the tampering, learns a hidden-parity fingerprint of the tool and detects a tool switch in arrival order, not necessarily in attack order. Attribution concerns attackers who do not erase that fingerprint, which a random Pauli layer would do cheaply. Each result is stated within one of four verifier classes. For per-unit verifiers, which never couple two seal units and hold no untouched reference, the sample separation is asymptotically exponential, by reduction from a known lower bound for one parity of strength up to 1/3. On ten tampered cells it is proved to be at least \qnProvedUniformMin-fold against non-adaptive stabilizer probes and observed in simulation at \qnEoneRatioFine-fold. Against an implemented self-referencing strategy the simulated gain is about twice the inverse squared strength. The value also depends on the attack: destroying the untouched cells makes the reference necessary, whereas copying errors exposes the fingerprint to reference-free probes. We derive the vault's qubit inventory and simulate a seal requirement curve: ten times more seal noise lengthens the seal's lifetime \qnSealLifeGainFive-fold at \qnSealRhoFive{} times the attribution cost at $r=10$.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate generalized Bloch oscillations of a single doublon defect in a one-dimensional antiferromagnetic $t$-$J_{\rm{z}}$ chain subjected to linear and quadratic magnetic field gradients. By mapping the spin-charge coupled many-body dynamics onto an effective single-particle model, we reveal how the Néel background mediates parity-dependent coupling between the defect and the external fields. The linear gradient induces a staggered potential that splits the Wannier-Stark ladder into two intertwined subladders, producing a characteristic beating pattern in the center-of-mass motion with a subband offset accurately captured by third-order perturbation theory. The quadratic gradient generates sublattice-dependent tilts that shear the ladder into a scissor-like profile, yielding site-dependent beating periods that encode the nonlocal spatial averaging of the defect wave function. When both gradients coexist, their interplay is fundamentally nonadditive: the quadratic gradient breaks the two-site periodicity that forbids second-order linear responses, unlocking a dominant cross-coupling proportional to $B_{\rm{z,1}}B_{\rm{z,2}}$. This leaves distinct fingerprints on the Bloch-oscillation spectrum---a rigid baseline hift of the fundamental doublet and asymmetric broadening of the first harmonic. These predictions are quantitatively corroborated by exact many-body simulations of the real-time density evolution and center-of-mass dynamics. Our work establishes a gradient-induced unlocking mechanism for multi-gradient defect spectroscopy in quantum magnets, offering a framework for probing parity-protected selection rules and their controlled breaking via generalized Bloch oscillations in tunable quantum simulators.

Abstract: Non-Gaussian bosonic states such as the Schrödinger cat states are essential for continuous-variable quantum-information processing. A convenient and deterministic process for generating cat states is through the use of a Rabi interaction between a single bosonic mode and a spin which facilitates a state-dependent displacement. The lack of a Rabi interaction for propagating optical photons has rendered optical cat states elusive. Here, we show that the complex nonlinear scattering dynamics of a single-mode squeezed-vacuum state interacting with a quantum emitter can be engineered to produce a Rabi interaction between a quantum emitter and a propagating photon mode while the contributions from other orthogonal modes destructively interfere. Our protocol can deterministically prepare large-scale high-fidelity squeezed cat states from squeezed-vacuum pulses and is robust to photon loss due to spontaneous emission to non-guided modes. Extending to scattering multiple input squeezed-vacuum pulses off the quantum emitter, we propose generating linear cluster state strings with logical qubits encoded as squeezed cat states, which we refer as the cat cluster state. Our interaction scheme can potentially produce strings of entangled cat states at GHz rates using semiconductor quantum dots.

</details>


### [65] [Cqlib: A High-Performance Software Stack for Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29983)
*Tianyan Quantum Group*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Cqlib 是一个以统一量子线路模型为核心的高性能量子计算软件栈，Rust 原生核心实现线路处理、态与算符计算、编译优化，提供 Python/C 接口，覆盖程序构建、模拟、分析、编译、设备适配与结果处理，并通过 OpenQASM 与 QCIS 实现程序表示与面向硬件执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向量子算法开发、模拟、编译与 QCIS 兼容硬件执行之间缺乏统一、可扩展的软件基础这一问题，构建贯通全流程的软件栈。

Method: 采用 Rust 原生核心实现电路处理、量子态与算符计算、编译与优化；提供 Python 与 C 接口以支持算法开发与系统集成；通过 OpenQASM 与 Quantum Control Instruction Set (QCIS) 支持程序表示与硬件导向执行；提供后端接口并以 Cqlib-Tianyan 实现 QCIS 兼容硬件执行；构建 Cqlib-Algorithm 模块化算子与可组合组件库（含量子搜索、相位估计、变分算法及误差缓解），并派生 Cqlib-VQE、Cqlib-QAOA、Cqlib-QML 应用层库；集成基于来源的技能（Skills）与 MCP 工具支持智能体辅助的程序生成、后端执行与结果引导的流程迭代；评测采用 Benchpress 参数化编译任务及相关测试。

Result: 在基准评测中，Cqlib 在所评估任务范围内表现出良好的功能覆盖度、编译正确性与执行效率。

Conclusion: Cqlib 提供了一个统一且可扩展的软件基础，将量子算法开发、模拟、编译与 QCIS 兼容硬件执行连接为一体。

Abstract: This paper presents Cqlib, a high-performance software stack for quantum computing built around a unified quantum circuit model. Its native Rust core provides efficient implementations of circuit processing, quantum-state and operator computation, compilation, and optimization, while Python and C interfaces support algorithm development and system integration. Cqlib covers quantum program construction, simulation, analysis, compilation, device adaptation, and result processing under consistent program semantics. Through OpenQASM and the Quantum Control Instruction Set (QCIS), Cqlib supports program representation and hardware-oriented execution. Cqlib further provides backend interfaces for device execution, with Cqlib-Tianyan serving as one implementation for QCIS-compatible quantum hardware. Evaluations using parameterized compilation tasks from Benchpress and related tests show that Cqlib achieves good functional coverage, compilation correctness, and execution efficiency within the evaluated task scope. We develop Cqlib-Algorithm as a modular library of reusable operators and composable components for widely used quantum algorithms, including quantum search, phase estimation, and variational algorithms, with additional support for error mitigation. To complement these building blocks with domain-specific workflows, we further develop Cqlib-VQE, Cqlib-QAOA, and Cqlib-QML for application-level studies in quantum chemistry, combinatorial optimization, and quantum machine learning, respectively. Cqlib also integrates source-grounded Skills and MCP-based tools to support agent-assisted program generation, backend execution, and result-guided workflow refinement. Overall, Cqlib provides a unified and extensible software foundation that connects quantum algorithm development, simulation, compilation, and execution on QCIS-compatible quantum hardware.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate multiphoton electronic coherences in a series of gas-phase atomic and molecular systems with increasing complexity. Our measurements are driven by pairs of phase-locked, deep-ultraviolet pulses with independent control of the relative phase and time delay. By combining the ionization yields measured at specific relative phases between the pulses, we can isolate different multiphoton orders and enable measurements of coherences between specific pairs of electronic states. We measure electronic coherences between states separated by zero-photon (i.e. within our laser bandwidth), one-photon and two-photons, resulting in signals with modulation periods ranging from 430 attoseconds to several femtoseconds. Fast electronic dynamics are tracked simultaneously with the accompanying nuclear motion. Additionally, we derive analytic expressions for the measured ionization yield as a function of pump-probe delay and demonstrate their use as a quantitative tool for isolating individual photon-order contributions within the total yield.

### [67] [Spin-adapted state preparation for quantum chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30014)

TL;DR: 提出将自旋适配的DMRG-MPS（以UGA步骤标记、每个自旋多重态存一个振幅）视为阿贝尔对称MPS，用现有块稀疏协议按其多重态数成本加载，再逐格施加由Clebsch-Gordan系数确定的态无关旋转回到占据数基。对铁硫簇活性空间单重态基态的电路资源估算显示：Toffoli计数相较稠密方法最多降低135倍、相较同态块稀疏方法降低4.9倍，ancilla量子比特最多减少3.7倍。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 把非阿贝尔自旋对称性显式编码进MPS制备电路可显著压缩态加载的容错成本，为强关联分子的量子相位估计提供了一种更高效的初态制备方案。

Abstract: Quantum phase estimation for strongly-correlated molecules requires an initial state of high overlap with the true ground state, and preparing one is a significant fault-tolerant cost. Matrix product states (MPS) produced by the density matrix renormalisation group (DMRG) are a leading choice, yet existing circuits treat their tensors as dense or do not use the full symmetries present in molecular electronic Hamiltonians. In quantum chemistry, DMRG also exploits total spin, a non-abelian symmetry, storing one amplitude per spin multiplet instead of one per state. We show that the reduced tensors of such a spin-adapted MPS, labelled by the steps of the unitary group approach, form an abelian-symmetric MPS, which existing block-sparse preparation protocols can load at a cost set by the multiplet count. One state-independent rotation per site, determined by a Clebsch-Gordan coefficient, then returns the state to the occupation basis. We provide resource estimates for our circuits on singlet ground states of active space models of iron-sulphur clusters, finding improvements in Toffoli count up to $135\times$ compared to dense methods and $4.9\times$ compared to block-sparse methods applied to the same states, with up to $3.7\times$ fewer ancilla qubits required.

Abstract: Structure-based virtual screening spans a combinatorial configuration space, motivating a quantum screening framework that targets threshold-qualified configuration indices without reconstructing a complete interaction-score table. In an ideal logical benchmark using AutoDock grid interactions as a proof-of-concept model, we evaluated initial FLAG acceptance probability, classification precision, shots required for 95% FLAG detection, and logical Q-operator calls across eight receptor systems and nested libraries of up to 1,024 ligand occupancies. Higher QAE/QPE resolution generally improved classification precision at additional evaluation cost, whereas suitably chosen QSEL iteration counts reduced required shots without improving classification precision, revealing selection and resource trade-offs for extending coherent index retrieval to more physically detailed molecular evaluation models.

TL;DR: 论文证明非交换最小值 m(A,B) 的超乘性，由此得到 hockey-stick 散度在张量积态上的子可加性（参数相乘、无额外因子），并用于量子差分隐私并行组合、假设检验相对熵及对称二元量子假设检验的 Chernoff 界精确前因子与样本复杂度。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hockey-stick 散度是量子信息中的基本可区分性度量，刻画量子差分隐私并决定任意先验下二元量子假设检验的最优错误概率；但其在张量积态上的行为（是否子可加、常数因子如何）此前缺乏精确刻画。

Method: 引入正半定算子的非交换最小值 m(A,B)=(Tr A+Tr B-‖A-B‖₁)/2，证明其超乘性 m(A⊗C,B⊗D)≥m(A,B)m(C,D)；据此推导乘积态上 hockey-stick 散度的子可加性，并推广到 Datta-Leditzky 散度与假设检验相对熵。

Result: 所得子可加界无额外前因子且是紧的（对非交换态亦成立），对忠实态在两散度为正时严格；由此得到量子差分隐私信道的并行组合定理（隐私参数至多线性退化）、归一化假设检验相对熵的尖锐子可加性，以及满足非格点条件的非交换态对下量子 Chernoff 界的精确前因子与 1/n 全渐近展开。

Conclusion: 非交换最小值超乘性是统一工具，导出紧致的散度子可加性与隐私并行组合结果；并确定对称二元量子假设检验的样本复杂度至一个样本（误差趋于零时）。

Abstract: The hockey-stick divergence is a fundamental distinguishability measure in quantum information theory. It characterizes quantum differential privacy and determines the optimal error probability of binary quantum hypothesis testing with arbitrary priors. We study how it behaves on tensor-product states. To this end, we consider the noncommutative minimum $m(A,B):=(\mathrm{Tr}[A]+\mathrm{Tr}[B]-\|A-B\|_1)/2$ of positive semidefinite operators, which reduces to $\sum_x\min\{a_x,b_x\}$ when $A$ and $B$ commute, and we prove that it is super-multiplicative, i.e., $m(A\otimes C,B\otimes D)\ge m(A,B)\,m(C,D)$. As a consequence, we establish a sub-additivity property of the hockey-stick divergence on product states, in which the parameters of the individual systems are multiplied, and no additional prefactor appears. We show that this bound is tight, also for noncommuting states, and that it is strict for faithful states whenever at least two of the divergences are positive. Using this, we obtain sub-additivity of the Datta-Leditzky divergences, a parallel composition theorem for quantum differentially private channels under product neighboring inputs with at most linear degradation of the privacy parameters, and a sharp sub-additivity property of the hypothesis-testing relative entropy normalized by the acceptance probability. Finally, we apply the same tools to symmetric binary quantum hypothesis testing. We determine the exact prefactor of the quantum Chernoff bound for every pair of noncommuting states satisfying mild conditions including a nonlattice condition and, under a quantitative version of this condition, the full asymptotic expansion to every fixed order in $1/n$, with explicitly computable coefficients. As a consequence, we determine the sample complexity of symmetric binary quantum hypothesis testing up to one sample, as the error probability tends to zero.

</details>

Abstract: We show that the connected out-of-time-ordered correlator (OTOC) of any pure state can be reconstructed exactly from commutator linear responses alone. The protocol requires no time reversal, no ancilla, no double copy, and no weak measurement: a unitary string pulse, forward many-body evolution, and a single local readout suffice. An average over a complete basis of pulse generators yields the OTOC exactly. We further show that the measurement can be made sparse by restricting the pulses to a fixed operator weight, which turns the truncation into an analytically controlled spectral filter: the deterministic bias can be computed before the experiment and cleanly separated from the statistical sampling variance. For Gaussian states, the finite-weight response is exactly proportional to the full OTOC after a known combinatorial rescaling. For quantum chaotic systems, the typical residual is suppressed as a power law in the sector size. Exact-diagonalization benchmarks in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model and a nonintegrable Ising chain confirm these predictions.

Abstract: A completely entangled subspace (CES) contains no product vectors, so every state supported on it is entangled. Whether such a subspace can support states with positive partial transpose (PPT) is a more subtle question. The normalized projector onto a CES may be NPT even if there exist PPT states supported on the CES. We investigate this distinction using a semidefinite criterion that optimizes the smallest partial-transpose eigenvalue over all supported density operators. For two- qutrit states supported on CESs, this leads to a complete characterization: a CES supports PPT states exactly when it is four-dimensional and related by invertible local transformations to the complement of an unextendible product basis (UPB). We also establish restrictions on PPT states whose ranges contain only vectors of Schmidt rank at least three, ruling out their existence in $3\otimes n$, $4\otimes4$, and $4\otimes5$, and showing that any such state in $4\otimes6$ must have rank eight. An interesting structure emerges when two orthogonal completely entangled subspaces together span the full Hilbert space, which we term \textit{complementary CESs}. Explicit constructions in $3\otimes4$ demonstrate that both subspaces can support PPT entanglement even when their normalized projectors are NPT. In $4\otimes4$, we construct a complementary pair whose normalized projectors are themselves PPT entangled. These constructions offer an alternative route to obtaining PPT entangled states, beyond the familiar approach based on UPB complements.

</details>

Motivation: 远程无线充电的量子版本中，电池"储能"与"可提取功"并不等价；需要厘清耦合强度、谐振器损耗与比特弛豫如何分别影响可用功，并确定最佳断开时机。

Conclusion: 可用功而非储能才是充电性能的关键指标；固定存储时长时，应在布居峰值处断开以保留最多可提取功。

Abstract: We study the remote charging of a qubit battery by a qubit charger coupled through a finite multimode resonator. The charging protocol uses time-shifted coupling pulses that account for the spatial phases of the resonator modes. We compare the energy stored in the battery with its ergotropy, the maximum work extractable by a local unitary operation. In the single-excitation regime considered here, the battery stores energy at any nonzero excited-state population, but its ergotropy is positive only when that population exceeds one half. In the absence of loss, stronger coupling brings the battery above this threshold earlier, while the final charge remains nearly complete across the coupling range studied. For the parameters considered, qubit relaxation suppresses ergotropy more strongly than resonator loss at comparable decay rates. After the battery is disconnected, relaxation causes its stored energy to decay exponentially, yet its ergotropy vanishes at a finite time while half of its energy capacity remains occupied. For a fixed storage interval, disconnecting at the population maximum therefore preserves the most extractable work.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum networks and repeaters are among the most promising routes to scale up quantum information systems. Nevertheless, various aspects still require significant improvement to achieve practical realization. Multiplexing can enhance the performance in many fundamental steps, thus emerging as a critical strategy for the realization of quantum repeaters. Currently, a major bottleneck in realizing functional quantum repeaters is heralded entanglement distribution over longdistance elementary links, as neither of the two dominant protocols-single-photon nor two-photon interference-can simultaneously achieve high efficiency and strong robustness. Here, we address this challenge by experimentally realizing a method that unifies the efficiency and robustness. By harnessing two 15 * 15 quantum memory arrays, we generate remote matter-matter entanglement at a rate of 546 Hz over 20 km of fiber without active phase stabilization. This rate exceeds that of two-photon and single-photon schemes by factors of 75,932 and 23, respectively, establishing a new benchmark for entangling speed over metropolitan-scale distances and enabling semi-deterministic entanglement distribution. Inherently compatible with multiplexed quantum repeater architectures, our scheme offers a promising approach towards the realization of future high-performance distributed quantum systems.

</details>


### [74] [Observable-Level Expressivity in Fixed-Readout Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30175)
*Iqra Bashir,Yuanfeng Wang,Mingda Huang,Keren Li,Qi Qin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文刻画了固定测量读出、无可训练仿射后处理的量子神经网络在三种物理资源（酉演化、混合酉演化、CPTP演化）下所能达到的有效可观测量集合，分别为固定谱轨道、其凸包与算子区间，且三者严格嵌套，并用闭式损失下界与数值实验加以印证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习中一个核心问题是：当测量由硬件或实验设计固定时，模型能表示输入态的哪些函数。既有工作多关注可训练测量或后处理，而忽略了前测量动力学在Heisenberg图像下对有效可观测量的谱约束，作者希望厘清不同物理资源究竟放松了哪一层谱约束。

Method: 将输出视为固定读出可观测量的期望值，把可训练动力学转到Heisenberg图像分析有效可观测量。按三类资源分类：酉演化（保持读出谱）、混合酉演化（放松为majorization约束并保持迹）、CPTP演化（Heisenberg伴随为unital、不再保迹，放松迹约束）。理论上给出三类可达集并证明对非单位正比的读出严格嵌套；用幅度阻尼可观测量γI+(1-γ)Z_j作局部见证；用Stinespring信道化电路实现CPTP层，推导闭式损失下界4γ²/3与γ²；并做结构化可观测量、Rényi矩与分类实验。

Result: 三个可达集分别是固定谱轨道、其凸包与算子区间，对任意非单位正比读出包含关系严格。对固定Pauli读出Z_j，幅度阻尼可观测量对任意γ>0都落在混合酉层之外。数值实验在精度内复现了损失下界4γ²/3、γ²以及在CPTP层为零的结果；有限深度ansatz上的结构化可观测量、Rényi矩和分类实验展示了该层级如何显现。

Conclusion: 结果为每一类物理资源所放松的谱约束给出了明确刻画，将可达可观测量类别视为决定表达能力的资源，而非架构优势。

Abstract: A central question in quantum machine learning is which functions of an input state a model can represent when its measurement is fixed by hardware or by experimental design. We address this question for quantum neural networks with a fixed readout observable, where the trainable pre-measurement dynamics determine the effective observable in the Heisenberg picture and the scalar output is the measured expectation value itself, without trainable affine post-processing. We characterize the reachable effective observables under three physical resource classes: unitary evolution, which preserves the spectrum of the readout; mixed-unitary evolution, which relaxes this to a majorization constraint while preserving the trace; and completely positive trace-preserving (CPTP) evolution, whose Heisenberg adjoint is unital rather than trace-preserving and therefore lifts the trace constraint inherited at the mixed-unitary level. The three reachable sets are a fixed-spectrum orbit, its convex hull, and an operator interval, and the inclusions are strict for any readout that is not proportional to the identity. The separation is witnessed locally: for a fixed Pauli readout $Z_j$ on an arbitrary number of qubits, the amplitude-damping observable $γ\id+(1-γ)Z_j$ lies outside the mixed-unitary level for every $γ>0$. We realize the CPTP level with a Stinespring-channelized circuit and derive closed-form loss floors, $4γ^2/3$ and $γ^2$ for the unitary and mixed-unitary levels and zero at the CPTP level, which the numerics reproduce within precision. Structured-observable, Rényi-moment and classification experiments illustrate how the hierarchy manifests in finite-depth ansätze. The results identify which spectral constraint each physical resource relaxes, framing reachable-observable classes as a resource for expressivity rather than an architectural advantage.

</details>

*Jiaqing Jiang,Nathan Ju*
