<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型是动力学平均场理论（DMFT）的计算核心，在强关联材料模拟中至关重要；但低温与高精度模拟在经典方法下代价高昂，而常用量子 Gibbs 采样器存在 exp(β) 的低温开销，因此需要新的高效量子算法。

Method: 基于量子 Gibbs 采样器（MCMC 的量子类比）构造算法；关键技巧是采用全局跳跃算符（global jump operators）替代局部跳跃算符，从而规避常规量子 Gibbs 采样器的 exp(β) 开销，使其在低温下对杂质模型依然高效。算法包含：(1) 热态制备流程，并在哈密顿量具逆多项式谱隙时退化为基态制备；(2) 热态纯化（purification）制备，并基于纯化进行静态与动力学性质的估计。

Result: 对任意 n 个费米模式、杂质规模固定的杂质模型：(1) 以精度 δ 制备逆温度 β 的热态，耗时 poly(n,β,log(1/δ))，为首次同时达到逆温度多项式标度与精度多对数标度；若谱隙为逆多项式，则基态制备耗时为 poly(n,log(1/δ))。(2) 制备热态纯化耗时 poly(n,β,log(1/δ))，据此以误差 δ 估计静态与动力学性质，时间在 n、β 上多项式、在 1/δ 上线性（至多对数因子），达到 Heisenberg 标度。

Conclusion: 通过全局跳跃算符设计的量子 Gibbs 采样器可有效消除低温下 exp(β) 开销，为量子杂质模型提供低温高精度的热态与基态制备及性质估计的高效量子算法，对强关联材料的量子模拟具有实用价值。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models, which describe a small but strongly-interacting subsystem coupled to a large noninteracting bath, are the computational core of dynamical mean field theory and play an important role in simulating strongly correlated materials. We develop quantum algorithms for efficiently simulating these models at low temperatures and with high precision. For any impurity model on $n$ fermionic modes with fixed impurity size: (1) We give a quantum algorithm that prepares the thermal state at inverse temperature $β$ to precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,\log(1/δ))$. This algorithm is the first to achieve both polynomial scaling in the inverse temperature and polylogarithmic scaling in the precision. When the Hamiltonian has an inverse-polynomial spectral gap, our quantum algorithm prepares its ground state to precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,\log(1/δ))$. (2) We further give a quantum algorithm that prepares a purification of the thermal state in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,\log(1/δ))$. Using this purification, we estimate static and dynamical properties with error $δ$ in time polynomial in $n$ and $β$ and linear in $1/δ$ up to polylogarithmic factors, achieving the Heisenberg scaling. Our algorithms build on quantum Gibbs samplers, which are quantum analogues of Markov chain Monte Carlo methods. By using global jump operators instead of local ones, we avoid the $\exp(β)$ overhead commonly associated with quantum Gibbs samplers, making them efficient for impurity models even at low temperatures.

</details>

Abstract: Classical simulations of quantum many-body dynamics face complementary limitations: entanglement growth restricts state evolution in the Schrödinger picture, while operator spreading makes Heisenberg-picture calculations increasingly expensive. Here, we combine both approaches by evolving quantum states and observables independently and joining their dynamics through massively parallel tensor-network contractions. We implement this strategy using tree tensor networks and sparse Pauli propagation, extending evolution times beyond those reached by either method separately in our calculations. For a quench of the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model at the critical field, we simulate the magnetization of an $11\times11$ open lattice up to $t=1.20$ by combining state and operator evolution over intervals $t_S=t_H=0.60$. We compare the dynamics with iPEPS, numerical linked-cluster expansion, and X-truncated sparse Pauli dynamics results. We also combine exact state evolution on small clusters with operator propagation on a larger lattice, improving agreement with iPEPS relative to cluster evolution alone. We further derive error bounds showing how the state can suppress operator errors and the evolved observable can reduce sensitivity to state errors.

Abstract: Subentropy gives a tight spectral lower bound on the accessible information of ensembles of pure states. We prove its subadditivity for arbitrary bipartite states in finite dimensions and derive a sharp relation between conditional subentropy and the minimum discrimination error. This relation strengthens a conjectured comparison with conditional min-entropy. Its inverse gives the strongest universal lower bound on the error determined only by conditional subentropy and the number of labels. Both main results follow from a comparison of conditional subentropy with the average subentropy of measurement posteriors, valid for mixed signal states and every finite measurement. We characterize all equality cases in these two results and bound posterior deviations near equality. We also determine the optimal Lipschitz constant in trace distance for conditional subentropy on classical-quantum states, including the exact constant when the quantum marginal is fixed. A determinant inequality strengthens subadditivity and proves strong subadditivity for classical distributions, while an exact counterexample shows that strong subadditivity fails for quantum states.

Abstract: As sensor networks become more interconnected, sensitive information sharing among multiple users poses potential risks of information leakage, making privacy protection a critical challenge. To address this challenge, we quantify network privacy using the maximum information leakage ratio (MILR) and propose a protocol that enhances privacy through causal-order switching and probe squeezing. Causal-order switching harnesses intrinsic network propagation dynamics for privacy encoding, while probe squeezing suppresses information leakage through quantum channels and increases the information available for estimating the global parameter. These mechanisms enable an asymptotic exponential enhancement on the privacy with the squeezing rate. We experimentally demonstrate this mechanism in distributed beam-tilt sensing using spatially structured light without requiring nonclassical light sources. Our work identifies intrinsic network propagation dynamics as a resource for privacy protection and opens a new avenue for continuous-variable sensing networks that combine high precision with enhanced privacy.

Abstract: Bosonic quantum error correction relies on an accurate description of single-photon loss dissipation using an ideal annihilation operator, under which correctable loss errors act equivalently on different logical code states. In realistic weakly nonlinear bosonic modes, however, coupling to a structured environment can render photon loss Fock-state dependent, thereby modifying the effective dissipation operator and deforming the associated error space. Here, we experimentally investigate this effect in a three-dimensional circuit quantum electrodynamics system. By injecting spectrally engineered environmental noise into a weakly nonlinear oscillator, we observe pronounced frequency selectivity in the measured Fock-state dynamics. While the long-time transition rates converge to the Markovian limit, the transient dynamics display distinctly non-Markovian behavior, in quantitative agreement with a second-order time-convolutionless theory. By applying narrowband noise to a binomially encoded logical qubit, we further demonstrate Fock-state-dependent dissipation channels that drive logical states beyond the nominally correctable error space. These results establish structured environmental noise as a mechanism for deforming photon-loss errors in weakly nonlinear bosonic systems and highlight the need to incorporate realistic, state-dependent dissipation into the design and characterization of bosonic quantum error correction.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于压缩真空（squeezed vacuum）相位估计能否在有限样本与真实损耗下真正实现海森堡标度，文献中存在争议；争议焦点涉及有限样本误差、资源计数是否公平、可用相位窗口大小以及损耗是否根本性破坏优势。本文旨在用精确计算一并裁定这些问题。

Method: 对压缩真空相位估计进行精确（非渐近）的有限样本误差计算，并在此基础上分析：资源公平（resource-fair）标度关系的推导；相位窗口内可分辨点数目与所需分支先验位数；以及固定损耗（透过率）对每光子优势的上限约束；同时引入固定10次重复作为确定有限样本前沿的开销设定。

Result: (1) 固定10次重复的固定开销即可确定可达的有限样本前沿；(2) 重复次数在资源公平界中被约去，因此海森堡标度得以保留；(3) 工作窗口可容纳与重复集合数同量级的可分辨点，且仅需一位分支先验；(4) 固定损耗使每光子优势封顶，其上限仅由透过率决定，且该上限已被报道的纪录所达到。

Conclusion: 压缩真空相位估计的争议可得结论：海森堡标度在资源公平意义下依然成立，有限样本与相位窗口的限制是可管理且量化的，而固定损耗才是根本性限制——它把每光子优势封顶为一个由透过率唯一决定的常数，且实验纪录已触及该上限。因此进一步提升需依赖降低损耗/提高透过率，而非单纯增加压缩或重复次数。

Abstract: We settle the dispute over squeezed-vacuum phase estimation by exact computation of the finite-sample errors, resource-fair scaling, phase window, and loss ceilings. A fixed overhead of ten repetitions sets the attainable finite-sample frontier; the repetitions cancel from the resource-fair bound, so Heisenberg scaling survives; the operating window holds a repetition-set number of resolvable points and needs one bit of branch prior; and fixed loss caps the per-photon advantage at a value set only by transmissivity, already reached by the reported record.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与非稳定化性（nonstabilizerness）分别刻画量子态结构的不同侧面，但二者单独都不足以描述混沌本征态中"不贡献非稳定化性"的那部分关联，需要一个联合量来揭示混沌态的特征结构。

Method: 定义稳定子关联为Rényi-2互信息与互稳定子Rényi熵之差；利用随机态系综（GOE、GUE，以及加入自旋反演宇称/半填充磁化、反射或平移对称性）解析求其本征态平均值；并对混沌混合场Ising链做精确对角化验证，同时对比可积自由费米子与强无序本征态。

Conclusion: 混沌本征态存在普适的稳定子关联平台，其值由对称性完全决定，构成超越纠缠或非稳定化性单独视角的混沌态特征结构。

Abstract: Entanglement and nonstabilizerness provide complementary perspectives on quantum-state structure. In this work, we find that the stabilizer correlation, defined as the difference between the Rényi-$2$ mutual information and the mutual stabilizer Rényi entropy, approaches a universal constant for chaotic eigenstates. This constant is determined solely by the symmetries, and quantifies the correlation that does not contribute to nonstabilizerness. We identify this universal stabilizer correlation analytically via random-state ensembles, which give $\log_2 7$ (GOE) and $\log_2 4$(GUE) without extra symmetries, $\log_2 14$ and $\log_2 8$ under spin-inversion parity or half-filling magnetization. While imposing reflection or translation symmetry leaves these values unchanged. Exact calculations for a chaotic mixed-field Ising chain confirm the predicted plateau and finite-size approach. Integrable free-fermion and strongly disordered eigenstates behave qualitatively differently. This universal stabilizer correlation reveals a characteristic structure of chaotic eigenstates beyond entanglement or nonstabilizerness alone.

</details>


### [82] [Binary-Tree Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm for the Maximum $k$-Cut Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30508)
*Zhaoyang Song,Wei Zhang,Ji Cao,Xuexi Yi,Hong-Fu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出BT-QAOA，将Max-$k$-Cut分解为至多$2k-3$个标准MaxCut子问题，按二叉树逐步分裂，显著降低量子资源需求并提升单电路成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QAOA方法直接把每个顶点的$k$种取值编码进量子线路，导致量子比特与门资源随$k$大幅增长，难以在近期量子硬件上实现Max-$k$-Cut。

Method: 先在全体顶点上求解一次标准MaxCut，随后每一步只对能带来最大割值增益的子集进行二分分裂，经$k-1$步形成二叉树的$k$个叶节点作为近似解；每个子问题均用标准MaxCut QAOA求解，共至多$2k-3$个子问题。同时推导了以单步分裂质量为参数的整体近似比理论下界。

Result: 理论上证明整体近似比不会随分裂次数指数衰减；数值模拟显示BT-QAOA在保持高近似比的同时大幅降低单线路的量子比特与门开销，单次编译电路的成功概率提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: BT-QAOA通过问题分解扩展了QAOA在近期量子硬件上的实用范围，为有限量子资源下求解更复杂优化问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: The maximum $k$-cut (Max-$k$-Cut) problem, as a natural generalization of the maximum-cut (MaxCut) problem, has broader applications in practical engineering. To solve this problem on quantum devices, existing studies typically encode the $k$ possible subset assignments of each vertex within the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA), which directly causes the required quantum resources to grow substantially with $k$. Here we propose a binary-tree quantum approximate optimization algorithm (BT-QAOA), which solves the Max-$k$-Cut problem by applying standard MaxCut QAOA to at most $2k-3$ subproblems. Specifically, after solving MaxCut once on the full vertex set, each subsequent step splits only the subset that yields the largest cut-value gain. This process is equivalent to constructing a binary tree whose leaf nodes after $k-1$ steps form an approximate solution to the Max-$k$-Cut problem. Furthermore, we derive a theoretical lower bound on the approximation ratio in terms of the single-step splitting quality, showing that the overall approximation ratio does not decay exponentially across multiple splits. Numerical simulations show that BT-QAOA substantially reduces the single-circuit qubit and gate overhead while maintaining a high approximation ratio, improving the estimated success probability of a single compiled circuit by orders of magnitude. This work effectively expands the practical reach of QAOA on near-term quantum hardware, offering a new route for solving more complex real-world optimization problems with limited quantum resources.

</details>

Abstract: To investigate the rich nonlinear dynamics of magnetic materials, we introduce a stroboscopic quantum diamond microscopy technique to probe the dynamic AC response of a Pt/Co/Pt thin film using nitrogen-vacancy centres in diamond. By overcoming the limited dynamic range of conventional optically detected magnetic resonance based imaging, our approach enables the observation of magnetization reversal under various large-amplitude sinusoidal and square wave excitations. The observed phase delay between the applied field and the sample response reveals information about the energy dissipated within the material. The temporal evolution reveals magnetization reversal through domain nucleation and growth also demonstrating isolated bubble domains associated with the defects. Furthermore, the detection of higher order harmonics provides access to the nonlinear AC susceptibility. These results establish stroboscopic quantum diamond microscope as a promising tool for spatially and temporally resolving nonlinear magnetization dynamics, with potential applications extending beyond ferromagnetic systems to complex magnetic crystals, magnetic phase transitions and superconductor vortex dynamics.

</details>


### [84] [Floquet dark-mode manipulation in a three-mode optomechanical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30645)
*Mian Mao,Jin-Feng Huang,Jie-Qiao Liao*

Main category: quant-ph

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三模光力系统中机械暗模（与腔模解耦）对量子态存储有潜在价值，但需要对暗模效应实现灵活、可控的调控手段，而常规方法缺乏这种调制自由度。

Method: 对其中一个机械模施加周期性频率调制产生Floquet边带，选取某一阶边带与另一机械模的共振频率匹配，利用完全破坏性干涉形成与腔模解耦的机械暗模；在半经典框架内自洽确定线性化工作点，并在旋转波近似下分析线性稳定性；通过比较近似与精确哈密顿量所支配态之间的保真度评估近似有效性；进一步在突然引入频率失谐时考察亮/暗模布居来演示暗模控制。

Result: Floquet边带匹配可实现完全破坏性干涉并形成机械暗模；自洽确定的工作点与线性稳定性得到验证；保真度分析给出近似哈密顿量的适用范围；突然失谐下的布居演化展示了暗模的可控切换。

Conclusion: Floquet边带调制为机械暗模效应提供了可控手段，其结果可为基于Floquet机械暗模的量子态存储研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We propose a Floquet sideband method to manipulate the dark-mode effect in a three-mode optomechanical system consisting of a single cavity mode coupled to two mechanical modes. We introduce a periodic frequency modulation for one mechanical mode to generate the Floquet sidebands, one of which is selected to match the resonance frequency of the other mechanical mode, allowing complete destructive interference to produce a mechanical dark mode decoupled from the cavity mode. The semiclassical operating point is determined self-consistently within the framework for linearization of optomechanical interaction, and the linear stability is examined under the rotating-wave approximation. We evaluate the validity of the approximate Hamiltonian by examining the fidelity between the states governed by the approximate and exact Hamiltonians. We further demonstrate dark-mode control by inspecting the bright/dark-mode populations of the system, when a frequency detuning is introduced suddenly. These results may provide a basis for further study of quantum-state storage using the Floquet mechanical dark modes.

</details>


### [85] [Quantum Cyclic XYZ Stabilizer Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30654)
*Alessio Baldelli,Francisco Lázaro,Davide Orsucci,Massimo Battaglioni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究三分量均为奇数长度二元循环码的XYZ码，利用x^n-1不可约因子分派与互素条件构造正交性，并用分圆陪集给出完整显式刻画；证明循环稳定子码具有循环分量XYZ表示的充要条件是生成元在i↦2i mod n置换下（相差相位）不变，所得码在固定长度与维数下达到已知最优最小距离并超越CSS码及其LP上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSS框架仅含X型与Z型稳定子，限制了二元循环分量构造稳定子码的最小距离；引入Y型稳定子（XYZ码）有望在相同长度和维数下获得更优的距离参数。作者希望系统刻画以循环码为分量的一类XYZ码，并给出其存在性与性能边界。

Method: 取奇数长度n的二元循环码为三个经典分量码；将x^n-1的不可约因子集合互不相交地分配给X、Y、Z三种Pauli类型，以固定编码逻辑量子比特数并保证三个奇偶校验矩阵行空间两两平凡相交；再对各类所分配因子与其余两类因子的互反多项式施加互素条件以保证正交性；用分圆陪集给出该族的完全显式描述。并给出判据：奇数n下循环稳定子码存在循环分量的XYZ表示当且仅当其稳定子生成元在i↦2i mod n的量子比特置换下相差相位不变。

Result: 在固定码长与维数条件下，所构造的循环XYZ码达到稳定子码的已知最优最小距离，超过其底层CSS码的最小距离，甚至超过一般CSS码的线性规划（LP）上界。

Conclusion: 以循环码为分量的XYZ码构成一族可由分圆陪集完全刻画、构造规则清晰的稳定子码，其互素—因子分派准则提供了显式的正交性设计工具；该族在距离上优于CSS码并突破CSS的LP界，说明Y型稳定子对循环结构码具有实质性的参数增益。

Abstract: XYZ codes generalize the Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) framework by adding $\sym{Y}$-type stabilizers to the usual $\sym{X}$- and $\sym{Z}$-type ones. We study XYZ codes whose three classical component codes are binary cyclic codes of odd length~$n$. Assigning disjoint sets of irreducible factors of $x^n-1$ to the three Pauli types fixes the number of encoded logical qubits and ensures that the row spaces of the three parity-check matrices intersect pairwise trivially. Then, a coprimality condition between the factors assigned to each type and the reciprocals of those assigned to the others guarantees the required orthogonality. Cyclotomic cosets give a complete and explicit description of the resulting family. We show that, for odd~$n$, a cyclic stabilizer code admits an XYZ representation with cyclic component codes iff its stabilizer generators are invariant, up to a phase, under the qubit permutation $i\mapsto 2i\bmod n$. By fixing the length and the dimension of the code, the proposed cyclic XYZ codes attain the best known minimum distance of stabilizer codes, exceed the minimum distance of the underlying CSS codes, and even exceed the linear-programming upper bounds for general CSS codes.

</details>


### [86] [Quantifying superposition via coherence by stochastic orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30656)
*Gökhan Torun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过量子相干性量化非正交参考态叠加，提出"正交化"测量映射作为将叠加转化为相干性的自由操作，并定义"叠加度"为可提取的最大平均相干性，从而把每个相干度量转化为具有相同操作意义的叠加度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正交参考态之间的叠加难以量化：任何正交基变换都无法奏效，因为每个正交归一基都会使某些无叠加态显得相干，因此需要一个不依赖基变换的操作性框架来定义和度量叠加。

Method: 提出正交化（orthogonalization）测量映射，将每个参考态映射到各测量结果下某个基向量的倍数；证明此类测量恰为把叠加转化为相干性的自由操作。以"叠加度"（从态中提取的最大平均相干性）定义叠加量，并分析其与干涉项、非正交重叠、确定性转换及猫态的关系。

Result: 干涉项成为相干性的概率至多等于无歧义区分两参考态的概率，且在无其他态时可达；正系数与非负重叠下，当且仅当重叠是随机分组的概率时取等。最大叠加度意味着能以确定性转化为最大相干态，对不超过三个参考态可能、一般情形不可能。不同相干度量给出各自的叠加度；两叠加度给出相反序时二者均无法确定性相互转换，且两个参考态即出现此类对（不同于量子比特相干情形）。等正重叠下同相叠加是刚性的：保持全部成分的确定性转换只能置换系数。参考态趋于合并时叠加度保持有界，而常用度量可能发散；任意振幅下旋转对称猫态都达到最大值。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了叠加的操作性量化，揭示了非正交性造成的转换约束：叠加度有界优于可能发散的常用度量，并按不同相干度量给出不同的转换序，从而在操作层面刻画叠加的资源结构。

Abstract: Here we present an approach to quantifying superposition of nonorthogonal reference states through quantum coherence. No change of basis achieves this: every orthonormal basis makes some superposition-free states appear coherent. We show that orthogonalization succeeds instead as a measurement mapping every reference state onto a multiple of a basis vector in each outcome; such measurements are exactly the free operations that convert superposition into coherence. The largest average coherence they extract from a state, which we call its degree of superposition, turns every coherence measure into a superposition measure with the same operational meaning. An interference term becomes coherence at most with the probability of unambiguously discriminating its two reference states, a bound attained when there are no others and, for positive coefficients and nonnegative overlaps, exactly when the overlaps are probabilities of a random grouping. Maximal degree means conversion into a maximally coherent state with certainty, possible for up to three reference states but not in general. Each coherence measure gives its own degree; when two degrees order two superpositions oppositely, neither converts into the other with certainty, and we find such pairs already for two reference states, unlike in qubit coherence. For equal positive overlaps, in-phase superpositions are rigid: deterministic conversions that keep all components can only permute their coefficients. As the reference states merge, the degree stays bounded where widely used measures can diverge, and at every amplitude a rotation-symmetric cat state attains its maximum. Our results quantify superposition operationally and reveal conversion constraints created by nonorthogonality.

</details>


### [87] [THeatA: Transport- and Heating-aware QEC simulation with Cooling Strategies for QCCD Trapped-Ion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30676)
*Eitaro Ishikawa,Noah Hirai,Koichiro Miyanishi,Hana Ebi,Kazufumi Tanji,Isamu Kudo,Kenji Toyoda,Masahiro Takeoka,Hiroki Takahashi,Takahiko Satoh,Shin Nishio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 面向线性QCCD离子阱系统，提出支持QCCD原生操作的全栈QEC实验与仿真框架（IR、编译器、加热感知噪声注入管线），揭示输运加热会破坏旋转表面码逻辑错误率的距离标度，而通过合并预冷离子间接冷却双比特门离子对可恢复标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCCD架构通过离子在专用阱区间的输运实现可扩展量子计算，但输运积累的振动激发（声子数升高）会降低Molmer-Sorensen双比特门保真度。现有QEC仿真框架无法联合追踪逐离子振动激发，也不能显式调度带输运时间与加热代价的冷却操作，因此无法评估真实QCCD噪声下的逻辑错误率标度。

Method: 构建面向单离子种类线性QCCD的全栈软件框架：1) 支持QCCD原生操作（离子输运、物理SWAP、阱分裂/合并、冷却）的中间表示IR；2) 将syndrome提取电路翻译为IR的编译器，并在调度过程中更新每个离子的平均声子数；3) 加热感知噪声注入管线，依据追踪的声子态与逐离子时序推导门相关错误率，生成带噪Stim电路，从而评估旋转表面码在输运与加热感知噪声下的有限尺寸逻辑错误标度；并扫描抽象冷却指令的持续时间与冷却后平均声子数。

Result: 在所评估条件下，输运诱导加热使逻辑错误率不再随码距增大而下降；若在每个双比特门前将参与门操作的离子对与预冷冷却离子合并以实现间接冷却，则可恢复逻辑错误率随码距下降的趋势。通过扫描冷却指令的持续时间与冷却后平均声子数，刻画二者权衡关系并确定可维持逻辑错误率标度的参数区域。

Conclusion: 冷却调度与加热建模是QCCD上QEC可行性评估的关键环节；本文框架可在全栈层面联合建模输运加热与冷却开销，为QCCD离子阱量子计算中纠错码的规模化设计提供可复现的仿真与优化依据。

Abstract: The quantum charge-coupled device (QCCD) architecture is a promising approach to scalable trapped-ion quantum computing, enabling flexible qubit interactions by transporting ions between dedicated trap zones. Programs for such systems require operations beyond conventional circuit primitives: ion shuttling, physical SWAPs, trap splitting and merging, and cooling. Motional excitation accumulated during transport increases the mean phonon number of ions and degrades the fidelity of the two-qubit Molmer-Sorensen gates. However, existing quantum error correction (QEC) simulation frameworks do not jointly track per-ion motional excitation and explicitly schedule cooling operations with their transport time and heating costs. We develop a full-stack software framework for QEC experiments and simulations on linear QCCD systems with a single ion species, comprising an intermediate representation (IR) supporting QCCD-native operations, a compiler that translates syndrome-extraction circuits into the IR, and a heating-aware noise-injection pipeline that generates noisy Stim circuits. The compiler updates the mean phonon number of each ion throughout the schedule, and the pipeline derives gate-dependent error rates from the tracked phonon states and per-ion timing, enabling the evaluation of finite-size logical-error scaling of the rotated surface code under transport- and heating-aware noise. We show that transport-induced heating prevents logical error rates from decreasing with increasing code distance under the evaluated conditions, and that indirectly cooling the ion pairs involved in two-qubit gates, by merging them with precooled coolant ions immediately before each gate, restores this decrease. By sweeping the duration and post-cooling mean phonon number of an abstract cooling instruction, we characterize their trade-off and identify the region in which logical-error scaling is maintained.

</details>


### [88] [Coherence transfer under bounded channel capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30680)
*Dhruv Baheti,Karol Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究局部参考基失配下，在量子通信信道容量约束下，Alice 受限于 MIO 时向 Bob 转移量子相干性的任务，给出单次协议与渐近速率刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中两方的局部参考基通常不对齐，而信道容量有限；相干性资源理论下需要理解基失配如何限制相干性的可转移量。

Method: 在相干性资源理论框架下，将任务表述为 Alice 仅可用 MIO 时最大化 Bob 接收到的相干性；分析直接传输与局部纠缠传输两种单次协议，推导相位最优的相干性产额，并对 Bob 参考基未知情形按均匀分布的基配置取平均；渐近区利用 MIO 下相干性的可逆性刻画可达转移速率；对单量子比特传输用纯态转换给出解析下界。

Result: 得到两种单次协议的相位优化相干产额；给出 Bob 基未知时的平均表现；渐近可达速率以 Alice 初始相干性表征；单量子比特情形有解析下界，并识别出最优产额饱和信道容量的参数区间。

Conclusion: 基失配与信道容量共同决定相干性转移效率；含纠缠辅助的单次协议与直接协议各有优势，且在某些区间可达到信道容量上限，为参考基失配下的相干性通信提供定量基准。

Abstract: We investigate quantum coherence transfer between two parties whose local reference bases are misaligned, under a constraint on the capacity of the quantum communication channel. Within the resource theory of coherence, we formulate the task as maximizing the coherence received by Bob when Alice is restricted to Maximally Incoherent Operations (MIO) in her local basis. We first analyze two single-shot protocols, based on direct and locally entangled transmission, and derive their phase-optimized coherence yields. We further investigate their performance when Bob's reference basis is unknown, averaging over uniformly distributed basis configurations. In the asymptotic regime, we exploit the reversibility of coherence under MIO to characterize achievable transfer rates in terms of Alice's initial coherence. For single-qubit transmission, we derive an analytical lower bound using pure-state conversions and identify a regime in which the optimal yield saturates the channel capacity.

</details>


### [89] [Optimizing Locally Equivalent Graph States in Quantum Computing Using Greedy Best-First Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30738)
*Sora Harada,Ryo Kuroiwa,Shin Nishio,Takeaki Uno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将"通过局部互补寻找边数与最大度更低的等价图态"形式化为状态空间搜索问题，用贪心最佳优先搜索（GBFS）求解，在随机图、有界度图及多数量子LDPC码构造图上优于ILP与局部搜索方法，并实证表明不能止步于首个局部最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图态是测量型量子计算与量子中继网络的核心资源，其制备成本取决于CZ门数量（边数）与电路深度（最大度）。局部互补可经廉价的局部Clifford操作得到操作上等价的图态，因此寻找边数更少、最大度更低的等价图能直接降低制备成本。已有方法基于整数线性规划或局部搜索，存在效率或解质量不足的问题。

Method: 把"经局部互补得到合意等价图"建模为状态空间搜索：状态为图，动作为局部互补，目标为降低边数与最大度；采用贪心最佳优先搜索（GBFS）作为启发式搜索算法求解，并在多类图上与ILP、局部搜索方法对比，同时经验性分析其搜索行为。

Result: 尽管GBFS结构简单，实验表明其在随机图、有界度图以及大多数由量子LDPC码构造的图上均优于基于整数线性规划和局部搜索的现有方法；搜索行为分析证实，为获得最优解必须持续搜索而非在首个局部极小处停止。

Conclusion: GBFS是求解局部互补等价图优化问题的一种简单而有效的方法，显示出把该问题作为状态空间搜索处理的潜力；简单启发式搜索的优越性提示未来可设计更强启发式或更精细的搜索策略以进一步降低图态制备成本。

Abstract: In measurement-based quantum computation and quantum repeater networks, graph-structured quantum states called graph states play a central role as an essential resource. In a graph state, each vertex corresponds to a qubit, and each edge is prepared by applying one controlled-Z (CZ) gate. Local complementation, which toggles adjacencies among the neighbors of a selected vertex, produces operationally equivalent graph states up to an inexpensive operation called local Clifford gates. Therefore, finding equivalent graphs up to local complementation with fewer edges and lower maximum degree can reduce the preparation cost, such as the number of CZ gates and the circuit depth. In this paper, we formulate the problem of finding a desirable equivalent graph via local complementations as a state space search problem. We apply a heuristic search algorithm, greedy best-first search (GBFS), to the formulated problem. Our experimental evaluation shows that despite its simplicity, GBFS outperforms existing approaches based on integer linear programming or local search across multiple graph classes, including random graphs, bounded-degree graphs, and most of the graphs constructed from quantum LDPC codes. In addition, we empirically analyze GBFS's search behavior, confirming that we need search rather than stopping at the first local minimum to find the best solution.

</details>


### [90] [Geometric properties of concatenated geometric and holonomic quantum gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30744)
*Rasmus Wallentin,Erik Sjöqvist*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究复合（串联）量子门的几何性质，区分几何量子计算与和乐（holonomic）量子计算，证明任意几何门的串联不保持纯几何性，而任意单比特和乐门的串联在和乐条件下仍为和乐门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将"几何保护"从单个门推广到完整量子线路，需先确认复合操作是否保持几何/和乐性质，此前缺乏系统性论证。

Method: 分析单个几何门串联后复合操作循环态上可能累积的非平凡动力学相位；对单比特和乐门，在演化全程满足平行移动条件且计算子空间做循环演化的前提下，论证串联仍为和乐操作。

Result: 单个门虽为纯几何，其串联一般不保持几何性（复合操作循环态可出现非平凡动力学相位）；而任意单比特和乐门在和乐条件下串联后保持和乐性。

Conclusion: 几何性与和乐性在串联下的保持性不同：几何性一般不封闭，和乐性（单比特情形）封闭。该结论是几何保护论证推广至整个量子线路的重要前提。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric properties of concatenated quantum gates, focusing on the distinction between geometric and holonomic quantum computation. We show that, although individual gates may be purely geometric, their concatenation does not generally preserve this property, since the cyclic states of the composite operation can accumulate nontrivial dynamical phases over the course of the concatenated evolution. In contrast, we demonstrate that concatenations of arbitrary single-qubit holonomic gates remain holonomic, provided that the parallel-transport condition holds throughout the evolution and the computational subspace undergoes cyclic evolution. The preservation of geometric or holonomic character under concatenation is an important prerequisite for extending arguments underlying geometric protection from individual gates to complete quantum circuits.

</details>


### [91] [Efficient Shot Allocation and Spectral Reconstruction for Real-Time Quantum Krylov Diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30746)
*Pegah Mohammadipour,Xiantao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出分析与统计方法，在固定总测量预算下优化实时量子Krylov对角化（QKD）的能量估计：对能量方差建模并学习各线路设置的最优测量次数分配，结合广义最小二乘估计器与联合谱一致重构，在自旋与Hubbard模型上降低了Ritz能量估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时量子Krylov对角化中能量由多个线路设置下测得的矩（Hamiltonian与overlap矩）估计，测量噪声通过矩的敏感度和同一次采样内结果的关联传播到能量。在总测量次数固定时，如何分配测量资源、以及如何利用矩之间的结构关系来降低能量估计的均方误差，缺乏系统的统计刻画。

Method: 1）将主导能量方差表示为各线路设置测量次数（shots）的函数，同时计入能量对被测矩的敏感度与同一shot内结果的关联；2）先用一批初始测量数据学习分配策略，将剩余shots按该策略分配，并给出在给定分配类内达到最小主导MSE系数的广义最小二乘能量估计器（初始测量计入总预算）；3）利用同一批数据，将Hamiltonian矩与overlap矩联合拟合到共同的非负谱测度上实现一致重构；4）在矩误差渐近高斯、且可行矩扰动由两个线性无关不等式一阶刻画的情形下，精确给出谱一致重构降低主导能量MSE的条件。

Result: 理论上：广义最小二乘估计器在固定规律性假设下于指定分配类内取得最小主导MSE系数；并刻画了谱一致重构在渐近高斯矩误差下降低主导能量MSE的充要条件。数值上：在自旋模型与Hubbard模型实验中，学习式分配在固定总shot成本下改进了Ritz能量估计，且强制谱一致性可进一步降低能量估计误差。

Conclusion: 测量资源分配与矩的统计重构是提升实时量子Krylov对角化能量精度的有效手段：自适应分配显著降低有限shot下的能量方差，谱一致约束在满足相应条件时带来额外精度收益，为固定测量预算下的量子特征值估计提供了可操作的统计优化框架。

Abstract: We develop analytical and statistical methods to improve the energy estimate from the real-time quantum Krylov diagonalization under a fixed measurement budget. We first express the leading energy variance as a function of the number of shots assigned to each circuit setting, accounting for both the sensitivity of the energy to the measured moments and the correlations among outcomes recorded in the same shot.We use an initial batch of measurements to choose how to allocate the remaining shots. Under fixed-model regularity assumptions, the resulting generalized least-squares energy estimator attains the minimum leading mean-square error (MSE) coefficient within the prescribed allocation class, with the initial measurements included in the total shot budget. Furthermore, we use the same data to reconstruct the Hamiltonian and overlap moments jointly by fitting them to a common nonnegative spectral measure. For asymptotically Gaussian moment errors, we characterize precisely when this reconstruction lowers the leading energy MSE in the case where two linearly independent inequalities describe the feasible moment perturbations to first order. Numerical experiments on spin and Hubbard models show that learned allocation can improve Ritz energy estimates at fixed total shot cost. The experiments also illustrate that enforcing spectral consistency can further reduce the energy estimation error.

</details>


### [92] [Dephasing-Powered Autonomous Quantum Engine with Time-Crystalline Work Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30747)
*Zhen-huan Yang,Dan-Bo Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明局域退相位（dephasing）可从"噪声"转变为量子引擎的动力源：它破坏相互作用自旋-1/2系综中的铁磁关联并注入能量，经LC谐振器传递给被动负载；无需外部周期驱动，系统可自组织成时间晶体式的工作循环，持续输出可复现功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自治量子引擎旨在把环境供能转化为有用功，但退相位通常被视为有害噪声。作者希望反转这一认识，考察退相位能否作为资源驱动引擎，并探讨在无外部周期驱动条件下系统能否自主形成持续循环。

Method: 构建"相互作用自旋-1/2系综 + LC谐振器 + 被动负载"的耦合模型。先在均匀平均场框架下分析自旋—谐振器耦合强度不同时的两类工作路径与负载反馈、谐振器反作用；再采用有限尺寸自旋扩散数值模拟，考察不间断运行时间随系统尺寸的标度行为。

Result: 识别出两条工作路径：弱耦合下，未加载工作介质的耗散时间晶体在加载后仍能存活并驱动接收端；强耦合下，谐振器反作用会破坏循环运行，但适当的负载阻尼可恢复复合系统稳定自洽的周期循环。有限尺寸模拟显示不间断运行时间随尺寸快速增长，且两种区制下尺寸依赖关系不同。

Conclusion: 自治量子引擎可通过相互作用与负载反馈自组织其集体动力学，将环境噪声（退相位）转化为持续的循环功输出，为噪声驱动的量子热机与时间晶体型功循环提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous quantum engines seek to convert energy supplied by environments into useful work, yet dephasing is usually regarded as detrimental noise. Here we show that local dephasing can instead power a quantum engine: by disrupting ferromagnetic correlations in an interacting spin-$1/2$ ensemble, it injects energy that can be transferred through an LC resonator to a passive load. Remarkably, without any external periodic driving, the coupled system can self-organize into a time-crystalline work cycle that delivers sustained, reproducible power. Within homogeneous mean-field dynamics, we identify two distinct routes to such operation. At weak spin--resonator coupling, the dissipative time crystal of the unloaded working medium survives loading and drives the receiver. At stronger coupling, resonator backaction can disrupt cyclic operation, whereas appropriate load damping restores a stable, self-consistent periodic cycle of the composite system. Finite-size spin-diffusion simulations further reveal rapidly increasing uninterrupted operating times with different size dependences in the two regimes. Our results demonstrate how an autonomous quantum engine can self-organize its collective dynamics through interactions and load feedback to harness environmental noise for sustained cyclic work output.

</details>


### [93] [Quantum resources constrain function approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30789)
*Oxana Shaya,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文用可训练门的累积资源能力，研究在固定编码与读出下，相干性、虚性、纠缠与魔法如何约束量子学习模型的函数逼近能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用函数逼近是机器学习的核心；量子学习模型虽然也具备通用性，但通用性并未说明逼近给定函数所需的具体资源，因此需要刻画资源对函数逼近的约束。

Method: 以可训练门的累积资源能力为度量，在固定编码—读出框架下分析：单量子比特相位编码模型中相干性约束模型函数的振荡、虚性约束其奇部；两量子比特模型中纠缠约束其与乘积函数的距离；并考察magic的作用，比较受限Clifford可训练门与无限制门在编码深度上的代价。

Result: 上述界在任意编码深度下均成立；相位编码本身即提供非Clifford操作；在远离零点的定义域上，Clifford可训练门足以实现一致逼近；用Clifford门替代无限制门，编码深度最多多项式增加（关于原深度、误差倒数与定义域到零点距离的倒数）；但在固定深度下，可训练门中的magic仍可提升精度。

Conclusion: 量子模型的资源必须与目标函数的结构相匹配，这为量子学习模型的资源—表达能力关系提供了理论刻画。

Abstract: Universal function approximation is central to machine learning. Quantum learning models are universal as well, but this does not specify the resources needed to approximate a given function. We study how coherence, imaginarity, entanglement and magic constrain function approximation for a fixed encoding and readout, using the cumulative resource power of the trainable gates. In a one-qubit model with phase encoding, coherence bounds the oscillation of the model function, while imaginarity bounds its odd part. In a two-qubit model, entanglement bounds the distance from product functions. These bounds hold at any encoding depth. For magic, the phase encoding itself supplies non-Clifford operations. On domains bounded away from zero, Clifford trainable gates suffice for uniform approximation. Replacing unrestricted trainable gates by Clifford gates increases the encoding depth at most polynomially in the original depth, the inverse error and the inverse distance of the domain from zero. Magic in the trainable gates can nevertheless improve accuracy at fixed depth. These results show how the resources of a quantum model must match the structure of the target function.

</details>


### [94] [Noise-Immune Attractors in Stochastically Driven Non-Hermitian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30811)
*Hanieh Najafzadeh,Radmehr Jafari,Abdollah Langari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明非厄米动力学可通过T对称破缺的Liouvillian吸引子实现投影型噪声不变性，使晚期缺陷密度对高斯时间噪声不敏感，并给出精确的几何分类，且可在光子波导中观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间随机涨落通常会破坏驱动量子体系的相干性并加速缺陷产生（anti-Kibble-Zurek效应），作者希望寻找一种能在噪声存在下稳定晚期态、抑制噪声诱导激发的机制。

Method: 针对受高斯涨落作用的非厄米系统建立精确的噪声主方程，揭示由T对称破缺Liouvillian吸引子支配的投影型噪声不变性机制；并在非平衡配对非厄米Kitaev模型与典型伪厄米体系中验证，同时提出耦合光子波导的实验方案。

Result: 噪声不变性机制产生稳健的缺陷密度平台，并得到精确的几何分类：噪声无关区、完全极化区与连续可调区；该行为超越了常规anti-Kibble-Zurek标度。

Conclusion: 非厄米吸引子动力学是控制噪声诱导激发的一种通用途径，为在噪声环境下稳定量子态提供了新范式。

Abstract: Temporal stochastic fluctuations generically destroy quantum coherence and accelerate defect generation in driven quantum systems. Here, we demonstrate that non-Hermitian dynamics can fundamentally reverse this trend by stabilizing noise-resilient late-time states. By developing an exact noise master equation for non-Hermitian systems subject to Gaussian fluctuations, we uncover a projective noise-invariance mechanism governed by T-symmetry-broken Liouvillian attractors. This mechanism produces robust defect-density plateaus and yields an exact geometric classification into noise-independent, fully polarized, and continuously tunable regimes. We substantiate these results in an imbalanced-pairing non-Hermitian Kitaev model, a prototypical pseudo-Hermitian system, and propose their experimental observation in coupled photonic waveguides. Our findings identify non-Hermitian attractor dynamics as a versatile route to controlling noise-induced excitations beyond conventional anti-Kibble-Zurek scaling.

</details>


### [95] [Exponential advantage in quantum observation with one extra qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30876)
*Zhenyu Du,Xiongfeng Ma,Vlatko Vedral,Jinzhao Sun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过仅增加一个辅助量子比特，利用其相干干涉动力学轨迹，可在探测量子多体动力学时实现指数级优势，同时高效估计指数多个多时刻关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术有望革新物理世界观测方式，但以最小开销获得显著优势仍是核心目标。现有无辅助比特方案在探测量子多体动力学、估计多时刻关联函数时演化次数多、效率低。

Method: 提出一种算法，为量子机器增加单个辅助量子比特，用其相干干涉动力学轨迹，从而并行估计指数多个多时刻关联函数，且演化次数远少于任意无辅助比特方案。

Result: 该指数优势在无辅助比特机器具有不受限相干时间和任意自适应控制时依然成立，甚至对单个关联函数也成立；并证明延长相干时间可指数增强观测能力，使协议在空间与时间上均达最优；数值上在多体系统中预测高阶探测信号，实验运行次数较既有方法少数个数量级。

Conclusion: 单辅助比特框架在量子学习、传感与模拟中具有广泛实用性，并在空间与时间资源上均达到最优。

Abstract: While quantum technologies promise to revolutionise how we observe the physical world, achieving substantial advantages with minimal overhead remains a central pursuit. Here, we show that augmenting a quantum machine with just one extra qubit yields an exponential advantage in probing quantum many-body dynamics. We propose an algorithm that uses this qubit to coherently interfere dynamical trajectories, simultaneously estimating exponentially many multi-time correlators with exponentially fewer evolutions than any ancilla-free scheme. Remarkably, this advantage holds even when the ancilla-free machine has unrestricted coherence time and arbitrary adaptive control, and persists even for a single correlator. We further prove that extending coherence time exponentially enhances observational power, establishing our protocol's optimality in both space and time. Finally, we numerically demonstrate our approach by predicting higher-order probe signals in a many-body system with orders-of-magnitude fewer experimental runs than previous methods. These results highlight the broad utility of our framework across quantum learning, sensing, and simulation.

</details>


### [96] [Optimal fidelity over zero-capacity erasure channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30902)
*Camilo Castro-Landolt,Rabsan Galib Ahmed,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了反可降解区量子擦除信道的最优渐近纠缠保真度，证明其等于 γ+(1-γ)/d（0<γ≤1/2，逻辑维度 d）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反可降解量子信道的可靠量子通信容量为零，但仍能以非平凡保真度传输量子信息，因此需要回答量子信息在此类信道中究竟能被多好地保持。

Method: 利用分数可扩展性（fractional extendibility）为任意纠错协议可达的纠缠保真度建立上界；并构造一族置换不变码（permutation-invariant codes）渐近饱和该上界。

Result: 对传输概率 0<γ≤1/2 与逻辑维度 d，整个反可降解区内量子擦除信道的最优渐近纠缠保真度恰为 γ+(1-γ)/d。

Conclusion: 给出了反可降解擦除信道纠缠保真度的精确解析刻画，且所构造的置换不变码可渐近达到该紧界。

Abstract: Antidegradable quantum channels have zero capacity for reliable quantum communication. However, they can nevertheless transmit quantum information with nontrivial fidelity. This raises the question: how well can quantum information be preserved through such channels? In this work, we exactly characterize the optimal asymptotic entanglement fidelity of quantum erasure channels throughout the antidegradable regime. Specifically, we show that it equals $γ+\frac{1-γ}{d}$ for transmission probabilities $0<γ\le \frac{1}{2}$ and logical dimension $d$. We use fractional extendibility to establish an upper bound on the entanglement fidelity achievable by arbitrary error-correcting protocols. Furthermore, we construct a family of permutation-invariant codes that asymptotically saturates this bound.

</details>


### [97] [Mitigating drift in spin-qubit control with calibration-insensitive pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30931)
*Mridul Pushp,Jonathan Hess,Seong Woo Oh,Vatsal K. Bandaru,Shize Che,Noah D. Johnson,Anthony J. Sigillito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 用BIR-4与CORPSE两种漂移鲁棒脉冲替代方波脉冲，在Si/SiGe量子点中实现免频繁重校准的高保真单比特门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅自旋量子比特虽具备容错级控制保真度且兼容商用代工，但器件校准漂移导致控制参数需每隔数小时重新调谐，成为量子点处理器规模化的关键瓶颈。

Method: 在Si/SiGe量子点器件中，以绝热BIR-4与复合CORPSE脉冲构建单比特门集，针对人为失校准（驱动功率偏差、频率偏置）进行基准测试，并自调谐好的脉冲出发，使用门集层析（GST）在十天内不重校准地跟踪误差。

Result: BIR-4在驱动功率变化2倍的范围内保持99% Clifford保真度；CORPSE在±0.9 MHz偏置下维持相近保真度；完全校准后CORPSE单门保真度99.97%，与校准方波相当。十天追踪中BIR-4旋转角偏差始终保持在0.3°以内，包括跨越1.3 MHz频率跳变。

Conclusion: 此类漂移鲁棒脉冲可在无需持续重校准的情况下维持高保真操作，缓解了量子点量子处理器规模化中的调谐瓶颈。

Abstract: Silicon spin qubits offer fault-tolerant control fidelities in a platform that is compatible with commercial foundries, but device calibration drifts and control parameters must be retuned every few hours. Here, we replace conventional square control pulses with two drift-resilient pulse families: the adiabatic BIR-4 and the composite CORPSE. In a Si/SiGe quantum-dot device, we benchmark single-qubit gate sets built from each pulse against deliberate miscalibration. We find that BIR-4 offers a 99% Clifford fidelity over a factor of 2 change in drive power and CORPSE maintains similar fidelity over +/-0.9MHz of offset. When fully calibrated CORPSE offers a 99.97% fidelity per gate, on par with a calibrated square pulse. Starting with tuned pulses, we then track errors using gate-set tomography for ten days without recalibration. BIR-4 holds its rotation angle to 0.3 degrees throughout, including across a 1.3MHz frequency jump. Our results demonstrate that these pulses can maintain high-fidelity operations without constant recalibration, mitigating the need for constant tuning, which is a key bottleneck in scaling quantum-dot-based quantum processors.

</details>


### [98] [Linear entanglement suffices for $f$-routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30947)
*Kevin Bogner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明对任意布尔函数 f，f-routing 只需 2n+2 个共享 EPR 对即可完成，显著改进了此前对所有函数成立的 2^{O(√(n log n))} 量子比特共享态上界，并在 Lean 中完成形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: f-routing 中共享纠缠量随 n 增长，但其下界与上界并不紧致；作者希望给出对所有函数统一成立、且更紧的共享纠缠上界，并厘清共享态与通信消息之间的资源权衡。

Method: 构造新的 f-routing 协议：将开销从共享态转移到消息。Alice 发送指数长的 2^{n+1}+1 量子比特消息，Bob 仅发送经典比特，二者共享 2n+2 个 EPR 对。结果以 Lean 形式化验证。

Result: 2n+2 个共享 EPR 对即对所有函数 f 足够，将共享态上界从 2^{O(√(n log n))} 量子比特降至线性；若把消息也计入资源，则此前的策略仍为已知最省。该结果同时表明：仅计共享态的下界不可能超过线性数量级 EPR 对。

Conclusion: 共享纠缠的最优代价至多为线性；仅计共享态的下界被限制在线性范围，资源权衡的关键在于是否将消息计入总量。

Abstract: In $f$-routing, Alice receives an $n$-bit string $x$ and an unknown qubit, and Bob receives an $n$-bit string $y$. They share a state prepared in advance and exchange one simultaneous message each. The party selected by the Boolean function $f(x,y)$ must then recover the unknown qubit without further communication. To perform $f$-routing successfully, Alice and Bob need to share entanglement that scales with $n$ for some functions $f$, but the lower and upper bounds for the entanglement are not tight. This paper shows that $2n+2$ shared EPR pairs suffice for every function $f$. The best previous bound that holds for all functions is a shared state of $2^{O(\sqrt{n\log n})}$ qubits. The new strategy moves the cost from the shared state to the messages: Alice sends an exponentially long message of $2^{n+1}+1$ qubits, while Bob sends only classical bits. When the messages are counted towards the resources as well, the previous strategy remains the cheapest known. The result also limits lower bounds that count only the shared state: they cannot exceed a linear number of EPR pairs. The result is formalized in Lean.

</details>


### [99] [Wigner negativity costs phase-space area: A conjectured refinement of the Lieb--Solovej inequality at Rényi order one half](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30969)
*Christos N. Gagatsos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一个猜想：任意单模态的Wigner对数负度被其Husimi函数Rényi-1/2熵超出相干态值的量所上界，且等号仅对相干态成立；作者证明该猜想在相差常数ln 2的意义下成立，并指出尖锐界需利用二模波函数的乘积结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb–Solovej（广义Wehrl）不等式给出了Husimi函数熵的下界，但缺少带资源单调性余项的精细刻画。作者希望用一个可实验观测（外差统计）的资源单调量来界定态的经典模拟代价（Wigner负度），从而细化该不等式。

Method: 1) 提出猜想：Wigner对数负度 ≤ Husimi函数Rényi-1/2熵减相干态值；2) 证明只需对纯态验证：负度凸、Husimi函数的L^{1/2}范数平方凹；3) 对所有态证明猜想至附加常数ln 2；4) 将纯态Wigner函数视为被动操作得到的二模态波函数进行分析，指出常数在一般二模波函数下已最优；5) 用真空与远距离相干态的显式叠加构造反例，检验Wehrl熵及高阶Rényi序。

Result: 猜想对所有单模态被证明至加性常数ln 2；对一般二模波函数该常数已最优，故尖锐界必须依赖二模态的乘积结构；显式叠加态表明Wehrl熵与高阶Rényi序的类比不等式失效。

Conclusion: 该猜想若成立可细化Lieb–Solovej不等式，并仅凭外差统计给出经典模拟代价的上界；作者给出相差ln 2的证明并定位因子2的损失来源；反例表明该猜想不能由Lieb–Solovej定理背后的majorization关系推出，需新的证明思路。

Abstract: We conjecture that the Wigner logarithmic negativity of any single-mode state is bounded by the amount by which the Rényi-$1/2$ entropy of its Husimi function exceeds the coherent-state value, with equality only for coherent states. If true, this refines the Lieb--Solovej (generalized Wehrl) inequality with a remainder term that is a resource monotone, and it implies that heterodyne statistics alone certify an upper bound on the classical-simulation cost of a state. We show that it suffices to prove the conjecture for pure states, since the negativity is convex and the squared $L^{1/2}$ norm of the Husimi function is concave. We prove the conjecture up to an additive constant $\ln 2$ for all states, and identify where the factor of two is lost: For pure states the Wigner function is the wavefunction of a two-mode state obtained by passive operations, and over generic two-mode wavefunctions our constant is already optimal, so that a proof of the sharp bound must exploit the product structure of that state. Finally, we show with an explicit superposition of the vacuum and a distant coherent state that the analogous inequality fails for the Wehrl entropy and for higher Rényi orders, meaning that the conjecture is not a consequence of the majorization relation underlying the Lieb--Solovej theorem.

</details>


### [100] [Solving the Traveling Salesman Problem Using Quantum Devices: QUBO and HOBO Formulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30972)
*G. V. Shuvalov,E. N. Krivtsova,A. V. Kapranov,M. A. Remnev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文系统研究TSP的QUBO与HOBO两种建模形式，并给出求解TSP的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSP是物流与优化任务的基础问题，但属NP-hard、计算开销大；量子计算有望加速或提升精度，而QUBO映射需量子比特数为二次量级，HOBO虽比特更少却含高阶项，二者权衡缺乏系统比较。

Method: 将TSP分别映射为QUBO与HOBO形式，结合VQE与QAOA等变分量子算法，对两种建模在比特资源、项结构等方面进行深入研究与细致对比。

Result: 给出QUBO与HOBO两种形式的全面对比分析，并提炼出求解TSP的实用配方（practical recipe）。

Conclusion: QUBO与HOBO各有资源与结构上的取舍，实践中应依据量子比特预算与高阶项处理能力选择建模方式。

Abstract: The traveling salesman problem (TSP) forms the foundation for many logistics and optimization tasks. At the same time, this problem is NP-hard and requires significant computational resources. Quantum computing promises to substantially accelerate computations or increase solution accuracy. The TSP can be conveniently mapped onto the quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) formulation, which in turn can be solved using variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) and quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) methods, but this mapping requires a quadratic number of qubits. The higher-order binary optimization (HOBO) formulation requires significantly fewer qubits, but it contains higher-order terms. In our work, we thoroughly investigate both formulations and provide a practical recipe for solving the TSP. We give a comprehensive study and compare in detail QUBO and HOBO formulations.

</details>


### [101] [Quantum Approximation and Integration in Sobolev Spaces -- Low Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31000)
*Stefan Heinrich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在基于信息的复杂性理论（IBC）的量子框架下研究Sobolev空间中逼近与积分问题的复杂性，去掉了以往结果所需的嵌入条件假设，并借助量子版本的随机离散化技术给出分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的量子Sobolev空间逼近与积分复杂性结果均依赖“嵌入条件”成立这一前提，适用范围受限。作者希望去除该假设，使结论覆盖更一般的参数范围（如嵌入不再紧或不再成立的Sobolev空间情形）。

Method: 采用基于信息的复杂性理论（IBC）中的量子计算模型；关键工具是此前发展的随机离散化技术的量子化版本，用于在无嵌入条件下构造离散化并分析量子算法的误差与信息复杂度。

Result: 在放弃嵌入条件后，得到了Sobolev空间逼近与积分问题量子复杂性的刻画（以单位精度误差及信息量/量子查询次数为度量）。

Conclusion: 嵌入条件并非获得量子复杂性上界与下界的必要条件；量子随机离散化可将经典结果推广到更一般的Sobolev空间设定，为无嵌入情形下的量子复杂性研究提供统一途径。

Abstract: We study the complexity of approximation and integration in Sobolev spaces in the quantum setting of information-based complexity theory (IBC). Previous results have been obtained under the assumption that the embedding condition holds. Here we drop this assumption and use a quantum version of a previously developed stochastic discretization technique.

</details>


### [102] [Resilience of Entangling Gates to Spatiotemporally Correlated Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31114)
*Xinru Tang,Sander Driessen,Ji Zou,Stefano Bosco,Jelena Klinovaja,Daniel Loss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文给出任意时空关联噪声下平均门失真度的通用解析式，并证明半导体自旋量子比特中CZ与√iSWAP门对关联噪声的鲁棒性显著优于SWAP门，从而将纠缠操作选择提升为一阶设计决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器规模化进程中，空间与时间关联噪声成为多比特门保真度的限制因素；需要判断在关联噪声环境下不同纠缠门的相对优劣，并给出可量化的误差预算方法。

Method: 推导适用于一般多比特体系及一般系统-环境耦合结构的平均门失真度通式，将失真度表示为噪声关联矩阵与门依赖滤波函数之间的谱重叠；据此分析半导体自旋量子比特的CZ、√iSWAP与SWAP门在低频主导噪声谱（含1/f与洛伦兹型）下的表现。

Result: CZ与√iSWAP门对关联噪声表现出明显鲁棒性，优于SWAP门；纠缠相互作用本身的关联涨落可将门性能退化至与局域噪声相当的水平；该层级关系源于噪声敏感通道被重新分布至远离零频处，且在低频主导谱下成立。

Conclusion: 门的相干动力学塑造了关联涨落进入的谱窗口；该框架可将实测噪声关联转化为定量误差预算，并为可扩展量子体系中的纠缠操作选择提供具体设计原则。

Abstract: As quantum processors scale, spatially and temporally correlated noise is emerging as a limiting factor in the fidelity of multiqubit gates. Here we show that this makes the choice of entangling operation a first-order design decision: for two-qubit gates in semiconductor spin qubits, the CZ and $\sqrt{\mathrm{iSWAP}}$ gates exhibit a pronounced resilience to correlated noise relative to the SWAP gate, even though correlated fluctuations of the entangling interaction can degrade gate performance to a level comparable to that of local noise. We trace this hierarchy to the redistribution of noise-sensitive channels away from zero frequency and show it to hold for noise spectra dominated by low-frequency weight, including $1/f$ and Lorentzian. These results follow from a general expression, derived here, for the average gate infidelity under arbitrary spatiotemporal noise correlations, valid for generic multiqubit systems and system-environment coupling structures. The infidelity takes the form of a spectral overlap between the noise correlation matrix and a gate-dependent filter function, in which the coherent dynamics of the gate itself shapes the spectral window through which correlated fluctuations enter. The framework converts measured noise correlations into quantitative error budgets and provides a concrete design principle for selecting entangling operations in scalable quantum architectures.

</details>


### [103] [A Quantum Model from the Riemann-Siegel Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31116)
*Germán Sierra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构建一个双组分量子模型，用破坏性干涉在连续谱中选出正则化zeta函数临界线零点，其能谱为实连续且统计符合GUE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将黎曼零点的临界线选取机制与量子力学中的干涉、PT对称哈密顿量联系起来，探索zeta零点作为物理谱的可实现性。

Method: 从精确Riemann–Siegel积分表示出发，减去原点极点贡献定义ζ_reg(s)，用剩余两个围道积分作为干涉振幅；引入Dirichlet热核滤波抑制高频，采用由素数幂伸缩生成的算术修饰；通过变换将伸缩生成元映射为具有正度量的物理Hilbert空间上的自伴PT对称哈密顿量。

Result: 哈密顿量谱为实且连续；计算得到的ζ_reg(s)下临界线零点接近相应的黎曼零点，其间距与对关联统计在所采样范围内与高斯幺正系综(GUE)一致。

Conclusion: 临界带下半部分每个解析零点是否都属于该物理谱族仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We construct a two-component quantum model in which destructive interference selects the critical-line zeros of a regularized zeta function within a continuous spectrum. Starting from the exact Riemann--Siegel integral representation, we define $ζ_{\rm reg}(s)$ by subtracting the contribution of the pole at the origin. The two remaining contour integrals supply the interfering amplitudes.
  A Dirichlet heat filter suppresses high spatial frequencies, and an arithmetic dressing has a profile generated by prime-power dilations. These transformations map the dilation generator to a self-adjoint, parity--time ($PT$) symmetric Hamiltonian on a physical Hilbert space with a positive metric. Its spectrum is real and continuous. The computed lower critical-line zeros of $ζ_{\rm reg}(s)$ lie close to the corresponding Riemann zeros and exhibit spacing and pair-correlation statistics consistent with the Gaussian unitary ensemble over the sampled range. Whether every analytic zero in the lower half of the critical strip belongs to the physical spectral family remains open.

</details>


### [104] [Reducing RSA-2048 Factoring Resources Below One Billion Toffolis via LLM-Driven Verifiable Quantum Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31158)
*Xiaodi Li,Yuqing Wang,Xue Chen,Yang-Yang Tang,Linghui Chen,Tao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用LLM驱动的搜索重设计近似RNS模幂流水线，构建确定性编译器生成RSA-2048电路的Pareto前沿，首次将RSA-2048分解所需Toffoli门降到十亿以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模幂运算主导了Shor算法分解2048位RSA整数的逻辑开销。基于近似剩余数系统（RNS）的方法虽大幅降低逻辑量子比特需求，但仍需数十亿Toffoli门。电路难以优化：共享辅助量子比特与基于测量的逆计算使各算术阶段紧耦合，且压缩寄存器中的稀有溢出概率过低，随机模拟难以检出。

Method: 采用大语言模型驱动的搜索跨阶段重新设计RNS流水线，并构建确定性编译器以生成RSA-2048电路的Pareto前沿；对稀有溢出概率进行解析界定，并在代表性电路上对所有组成RNS素数验证所生成的基本门序列；在与Gidney 2025基线相匹配的加法器计数口径下进行比较。

Result: 在匹配加法器计数的条件下，Pareto前沿将最小量子比特-Toffoli乘积降低70.1%；在累加器精度匹配时，期望分解Toffoli门数最多降低78.6%，使RSA-2048分解首次低于十亿Toffoli门。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的跨阶段电路搜索结合确定性编译与解析溢出界定，可有效优化高度耦合的RNS模幂电路，显著压缩RSA-2048分解的资源开销，推进实用规模量子因式分解的资源门槛。

Abstract: Modular exponentiation dominates the logical cost of factoring 2048-bit RSA integers (RSA-2048) with Shor's algorithm. Recent approaches based on approximate residue number systems (RNS) sharply reduce the required logical qubits, yet still demand billions of Toffoli gates. Optimizing these circuits is difficult because shared ancilla qubits and measurement-based uncomputation tightly couple the arithmetic stages, while rare overflows in compressed registers occur too infrequently for random simulation to detect. Here we use large language model (LLM)-driven search to redesign the RNS pipeline across stages and build a deterministic compiler that produces a Pareto frontier of RSA-2048 circuits. We bound rare overflow probabilities analytically and verify the resulting elementary gate sequences across all constituent RNS primes on representative circuits. Relative to Gidney's 2025 baseline under matched adder accounting, our frontier cuts the minimum qubit-Toffoli product by 70.1% and lowers expected factoring Toffolis by up to 78.6% at matched accumulator precision, bringing RSA-2048 factoring below one billion Toffolis for the first time.

</details>


### [105] [Floquet spectra and parameter sensitivity of entanglement in the quantum kicked top](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31179)
*Dyari Jarvis,Peter A. Rose,Arjendu K. Pattanayak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究四量子比特量子踢动顶中Floquet谱（本征相位简并）与纠缠景观对参数/初态敏感性的关系，发现敏感性极大值与本征相位简并或短周期量子复现相对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Floquet算符的谱结构（本征相位简并）是否能解释纠缠景观对参数与初态变化的敏感性，从而为小j量子踢动顶的动力学提供谱学判据。

Method: 以四量子比特量子踢动顶为对象，用特征多项式p与其导数∂_λp的结式（resultant）定位Floquet本征相位简并；并在自旋相干初态构成的球面上，定量映射纠缠景观对踢动强度变化的敏感性。

Result: 显著的敏感性极大值或与本征相位简并重合，或出现在呈现短周期量子复现的踢动强度处，部分点同时满足两个条件。

Conclusion: 建立了Floquet算符谱结构与纠缠景观敏感性之间的联系，表明结式是研究小j量子踢动顶动力学的有效工具；该谱结构对任意j均成立，故核心结论具有普遍性。

Abstract: We investigate the relationship between the Floquet spectrum and the sensitivity of the entanglement landscape to parameter and initial condition change in the quantum kicked top. We focus on the case of four qubits and on locating degeneracies in its Floquet eigenphases using the resultant of its characteristic polynomial $p$ and its derivative $\partial_λp$ and quantitatively map the sensitivity of the entanglement landscape to changes in kicking strength over the sphere of spin coherent initial states. We find that prominent sensitivity maxima coincide with either Floquet eigenphase degeneracies or kick strengths exhibiting short-period quantum revivals, with some points satisfying both conditions. These results establish a connection between the spectral structure of the Floquet operator and entanglement landscape sensitivity, demonstrating the utility of the resultant in studying the dynamics of small-$j$ quantum kicked tops. Since this structure extends to all values of $j$, the central lessons apply completely generally.

</details>


### [106] [Self-guided estimation of the stabilizer fidelity with single-copy Pauli measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31186)
*M. Torres,J. Gidi,L. Pereira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出自引导协议，仅用单拷贝Pauli测量估计任意态（纯或混）的稳定子保真度，避免对2^{O(n²)}个稳定子态做优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子保真度可量化纯态的非稳定子性（non-stabilizerness），但其估计需要对n量子比特的2^{O(n²)}个稳定子态进行优化，计算上难以实现。

Method: 基于相邻稳定子态构成的图进行自引导搜索；通过一次直接保真度估计（direct fidelity estimation）获得整个稳定子基的保真度；复用先前测量结果，并以其作为重启点缓解随机初值导致的贫瘠高原（barren plateau）；当估计保真度的置信下界超过cos²(π/8)时认证全局最优。

Result: 在最多14量子比特的数值模拟中，所有运行均达到最优；迭代次数保持较少，且随n仅轻微增长。

Conclusion: 该协议为稳定子保真度提供了一种仅需单拷贝Pauli测量的可扩展、可认证的估计方案，在大规模数值模拟中稳定收敛至全局最优。

Abstract: The stabilizer fidelity quantifies the non-stabilizerness of pure states, but it is hard to estimate because it requires optimizing over $2^{O(n^2)}$ stabilizer states for $n$ qubits. Here, we propose a self-guided estimation protocol for the stabilizer fidelity of an unknown state, pure or mixed, which requires only Pauli measurements on single copies. The protocol moves through the graph of neighboring stabilizer states, obtains the fidelities of a whole stabilizer basis from a single direct fidelity estimation, and reuses previous outcomes, which also provide a restart point that mitigates the barren plateau of a random start. In addition, it certifies the global optimum when the lower confidence bound of the estimated fidelity exceeds $\cos^2(π/8)$. In numerical simulations of up to 14 qubits, the protocol reached the optimum in all runs, with the number of iterations remaining small and increasing only slightly with $n$.

</details>


### [107] [Advancing the Theory of Pretty Good Quantum Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31207)
*Bikun Li,Guo Zheng,Liang Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文把"pretty good measurement"与"Petz recovery map"统一到一个带可调幂次的半定规划框架中，将其推广到更广泛的量子任务；证明了幂次在1到3之间（峰值在二次幂处）的更强性能下界，并指出对Pauli噪声下的稳定子码等结构化信道，大幂次会使恢复映射收敛到最大似然解码器（对最大混合输入而言）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优量子测量与最优恢复映射通常难以求解。Pretty good measurement与Petz map虽给出显式近似，并通过Barnum-Knill界保证至少达到最优性能的平方，但二者长期被视为相互独立的对象，且幂次固定的构造缺乏统一刻画与更紧的性能保证。

Method: 构造一个带可调幂次的半定规划（SDP）框架，将pretty good measurement与Petz map作为特例统一起来，并推广到更一般的量子任务（测量、恢复/信道近似）。在此框架下分析性能随幂次的变化，并针对结构化信道（如Pauli噪声下的稳定子码）考察大幂次极限行为。

Result: 证明了幂次大于1且不超过3时的更强性能界，该界在二次幂处达到最大（即存在一个"最优幂次"）；对Pauli噪声下的稳定子码等结构化信道，大幂次会使恢复映射趋于最大似然解码器（针对最大混合输入）。

Conclusion: Pretty good measurement与Petz map是同一SDP族的不同幂次实例；最优幂次并非单调外推的极端值，二次幂在保证与可计算性上尤为关键；幂次可作为连接"通用近似"与"结构化最优解码"的连续参数。

Abstract: Finding optimal quantum measurements or recovery maps is often demanding. Pretty good measurements and Petz maps offer explicit approximations achieving at least the square of optimal performance by the Barnum-Knill bound. We unify these operations in a semidefinite programming framework with a tunable power, extending them to broader quantum tasks. We prove a stronger bound for powers above one up to three that peaks at quadratic power. For structured channels such as stabilizer codes under Pauli noise, large powers steer recovery to a maximum likelihood decoder for maximally mixed inputs.

</details>


### [108] [Small-world networks yield tunable quantum codes at short circuit depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31224)
*Samuel Bevins,Gregory Bentsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过在Watts--Strogatz小世界网络上实现随机Clifford电路，可将非局域重连、测量率与电路深度作为三个调节旋钮，构造性质可调的量子纠错码；全重连网络只需对数深度的电路即可获得有限码率与广延的平均连续距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监测随机量子电路天然产生量子纠错码这一现象启发，作者希望探究电路的连通性（网络拓扑）能否作为一个独立设计自由度，用来调控所生成量子码的码率、距离以及纯化相变的性质，从而突破局域网络中编码需要多项式深度的限制。

Method: 在Watts--Strogatz小世界网络上实现随机Clifford电路，以三个旋钮调节码性质：非局域图重连概率、测量率与电路深度；通过数值模拟比较完全重连网络与局域网络所生成码的码率、平均连续距离及失败概率，并考察重连对纯化相变临界测量率与临界指数的影响。

Result: 在完全重连网络上，对数深度电路即可产生具有有限码率和广延平均连续距离的码，其失败概率显著低于局域电路产生的码；此类小世界码所需电路深度随系统规模按ln n增长，而局域网络需要多项式深度。重连还将纯化相变推移到更高的测量率，并改变其临界指数。

Conclusion: 连通性是设计量子纠错码的一个有效调控旋钮：非局域重连可显著降低编码所需电路深度并改善码参数，同时改变监测电路的相变行为，为可调量子码的构造提供了新途径。

Abstract: Monitored random quantum circuits naturally generate quantum error-correcting codes. We generate quantum codes with tunable properties by implementing random Clifford circuits on Watts--Strogatz `small-world' networks and tailor code properties via three knobs: nonlocal graph rewiring, measurement rate, and circuit depth. On fully rewired networks, circuits of logarithmic depth produce codes with a finite rate and an extensive mean contiguous distance with failure probability substantially lower than codes produced from local circuits. These small-world codes form at a circuit depth growing as $\ln n$ compared to polynomial depths required for codes produced from local networks. Rewiring also moves the purification transition to higher measurement rates and changes its critical exponents. Connectivity is thus a knob for designing codes.

</details>


### [109] [Simple design of adaptive protocols for quantum metrology with limited data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31230)
*Ricard Ravell Rodríguez,Jesús Rubio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于Personick最小均方损失形式主义的简单贝叶斯设计原则，将测量优化纳入自适应精度增益框架，实现对探针与测量的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有传感实验采用贝叶斯精度增益来调整固定测量下的控制参数，但无法优化测量本身。作者希望把测量优化也纳入该过程，为每个探针确定最优测量，并以依赖先验的精度增益优化探针，从而在有限数据下实现更优的量子度量学方案设计。

Method: 以Personick最小均方损失形式主义为基础，构造Fisher信息协议设计的贝叶斯对应物：其代数结构与基于Fisher信息的设计类似，但完整纳入先验信息。将该原则应用于单模高斯探针的自适应相位估计，进行探针与测量的联合优化。

Result: 联合优化优于自适应零差探测以及针对初始先验优化的固定探针策略；相对零差探测，平均后验方差最多降低32%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当测量基可被控制时，简单的贝叶斯设计在有限数据的量子度量学中具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Recent sensing experiments have used a Bayesian notion of precision gain to adapt control parameters for fixed measurements. Here, we advocate a simple design principle based on Personick's minimum mean square loss formalism to extend this procedure in order to include measurement optimisation, determining the optimal measurement for each probe and providing a prior-dependent precision gain to optimise the probe itself. This offers a Bayesian counterpart to protocol design based on the Fisher information, with a similar algebraic structure but incorporating the full prior. We apply this principle to adaptive phase estimation with single-mode Gaussian probes, showing that joint optimisation outperforms both adaptive homodyne detection and a fixed-probe strategy optimised for the initial prior, with a maximum reduction of 32% in mean posterior variance relative to homodyne detection. Our results illustrate the potential of simple Bayesian design for quantum metrology with limited data when measurement bases can be controlled.

</details>


### [110] [Two messages suffice for quantum interactive proof systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31236)
*Yupan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明任意量子交互证明系统可并行化到两条消息，用"量子公币"(EPR对的一半)加stoquastic验证即可，得到 qq-StoqAM = qq-QAM = QIP(2) = QIP = PSPACE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子交互证明可并行化到三条消息(Kitaev–Watrous)，是与经典情形的显著区别；本文试图进一步压缩到两条消息，并完整刻画两消息量子交互证明的能力。

Method: verifier先发送EPR对的一半作量子公币，收到prover响应后仅做stoquastic验证；核心技术是将Shamir/Shen的sum-check协议归约为CIUniform问题(自然属于qq-QAM的stoquastic版本)，证明其PSPACE-难：构造高效采样器，当量化布尔公式为真时其像分布对某输入分布恰为均匀。

Result: 证明 qq-StoqAM = qq-QAM = QIP(2) = QIP = PSPACE，强化了Jain–Ji–Upadhyay–Watrous的 QIP(3)=QIP=PSPACE。

Conclusion: 两消息量子交互证明已足以刻画PSPACE，且stoquastic验证不损失能力，给出两消息量子交互证明系统的完整刻画。

Abstract: Quantum interactive proof systems can be parallelized to three messages, as shown by Kitaev and Watrous (STOC 2000), perhaps their most striking distinction from classical counterparts. In this work, we establish that any quantum interactive proof system can further be parallelized to two messages, with the verifier sending halves of EPR pairs (``quantum public coins'') and, after the prover's response, using only stoquastic verification. Technically, we strengthen ${\sf QIP}(3) = {\sf QIP} = {\sf PSPACE}$ of Jain, Ji, Upadhyay, and Watrous (JACM, 2011) and fully characterize two-message quantum interactive proof systems by proving \[ {\rm qq}\text{-}{\sf StoqAM} = {\rm qq}\text{-}{\sf QAM} = {\sf QIP}(2) = {\sf QIP} = {\sf PSPACE}. \] Our main technical contributions show that Close Image to Uniform (CIUniform), which is naturally in the stoquastic version of ${\rm qq}\text{-}{\sf QAM}$, is $\sf PSPACE$-hard via transforming the sum-check protocol of Shamir (JACM, 1992) and Shen (JACM, 1992) into a CIUniform instance consisting of an efficient sampler whose image distribution is exactly uniform for some input distribution when the underlying quantified Boolean formula is true.

</details>

### [111] [Multiorbital driving of quantum geometric nonlinear transport in oxide interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28213)
*Irene Gaiardoni,Mattia Trama,Roberta Citro,Annika Johansson*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 理论上研究SrTiO₃基二维电子气中量子几何对非线性电导的贡献，比较多轨道紧束缚下(001)与(111)界面取向的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何是决定低维电子系统本征非线性输运性质的关键因素，但氧化物界面二维电子气中多带电子结构与晶向如何塑造量子几何非线性响应尚不清楚，需对比原型(001)与(111)取向。

Method: 采用多轨道紧束缚模型，计算能带归一化量子度规在动量空间的分布及对应的非线性电导，考察t₂g轨道在避免交叉点附近的杂化效应，并对比(001)与(111)两种晶体界面取向。

Result: 多带电子结构对量子几何非线性输运起决定性作用；t₂g轨道杂化塑造量子度规的动量分布与非线性电导；(001)界面在Lifshitz转变附近呈现尖锐的峰-谷特征，而(111)界面的三方对称性促进强轨道混合，响应更为展宽。

Conclusion: 轨道杂化与晶体取向的相互作用导致氧化物界面中不同的量子几何非线性输运响应，为界面取向调控非线性输运提供理论依据。

Abstract: Quantum geometry plays a relevant role in determining the intrinsic non-linear transport properties of low-dimensional electron systems. Here, we theoretically investigate the quantum geometric contribution to the non-linear conductivity in $\text{SrTiO}_3$-based two-dimensional electron gases, comparing the prototypical (001) and (111) crystallographic interface orientations. Using a multi-orbital tight-binding approach, we uncover the crucial role of the multiband electronic structure in determining the quantum-geometric contribution to nonlinear transport. In particular, we show how the hybridization of $t_{2g}$ orbitals near avoided band crossings shapes the momentum-space distribution of the band-normalized quantum metric and the resulting nonlinear conductivity. Furthermore, we reveal a strong dependence on crystal orientation: while the (001) interface exhibits sharp peak-and-dip features confined around Lifshitz transitions, the trigonal symmetry of the (111) interface promotes strong orbital mixing. Our results highlight how the interplay between orbital hybridization and crystallographic orientation gives rise to distinct quantum-geometric nonlinear transport responses in oxide interfaces.

</details>


### [112] [Wireless readout and control of cavity magnon-polaritons via photonic modes delocalised across a gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28347)
*Alban Joseph,Ryan D McKenzie,Amy Eden,Morven Last,Shannon Howard,Alzbeta Oravcova,Mawgan A Smith,Robert L Stamps,Rair Macedo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用两个空间分离、无线耦合的环形谐振器，实现了腔磁子极化激元的远程激发与读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔磁子学中光子-磁子杂化通常依赖腔自身反射/透射谱的共振位移来探测，该读读方式在二维谐振器（利于片上集成）中仍未改变，限制了探测灵活性，需要一种远程激励与读取的途径。

Method: 采用两个空间分离的环形谐振器，通过电感耦合实现无线能量传输；用一个谐振器激发磁子体系，用另一个谐振器测量其杂化模式谱。

Result: 在毫米至厘米量级的间距下实现了腔磁子极化激元的无线读取，并在远程测得的谱中识别出其特征响应。

Conclusion: 无线谐振器耦合可作为混合光子-磁子系统远程探测的有效途径。

Abstract: In cavity magnonics, photon-magnon hybridisation is typically detected by measuring the cavity response (i.e. measuring cavity resonance shifts through its reflection or transmission spectra). While a large body of work in the field has focused on three-dimensional cavities, a variety of two-dimensional resonators have also been explored as hosts for photonic modes due to their compatibility with on-chip integration. Nevertheless, the readout approach remains largely unchanged. Here, we demonstrate remote excitation and readout of cavity magnon-polaritons using two spatially separated, wirelessly coupled ring resonators. Through inductive coupling, energy is transferred between the resonators, enabling the magnonic system to be excited by one resonator while its hybrid-mode spectrum is detected through measurements of the other. We demonstrate wireless readout of cavity magnon-polaritons at separations ranging from millimetres to centimetres, identifying their characteristic response in the remotely measured spectrum. This establishes wireless resonator coupling as a route to remote interrogation of hybrid photon-magnon systems.

</details>


### [113] [Spherical HgTe nanocrystal: a multi-band effective mass study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28689)
*Rui Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文解析求解球形HgTe纳米晶中的多带有效质量方程，给出尺寸量子化能级、本征态与光学选择定则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HgTe纳米晶具有强自旋轨道耦合与能带反转特性，需在球对称近似下计入Γ6与Γ8能带耦合，以准确描述量子限域下的电子结构与光学跃迁规律。

Method: 采用球对称近似下的六带Kane模型（含Γ6与Γ8能带），解析求解多带有效质量方程；按总角动量与宇称对本征态进行分类，并推导光学选择定则。

Result: 能级随纳米晶半径变化时，不仅电子与空穴能级之间出现交叉，空穴能级自身之间也存在交叉；每个自旋子空间中最低的两个电子态为表面态，分别具有偶宇称与奇宇称；光学选择定则包含宇称选择与角动量选择两部分。

Conclusion: 六带Kane模型的解析解可系统刻画HgTe球形纳米晶的能级结构、表面态特征与光学跃迁规则，为理解其尺寸依赖的光电性质提供理论基础。

Abstract: The size-quantized energies, eigenstates, and optical selection rules in a spherical HgTe nanocrystal are studied by solving the multi-band effective-mass equations analytically. The six-band Kane model, involving both the $Γ_{6}$ and $Γ_{8}$ bands within the spherical approximation, is employed in the derivation. The eigenstates can be classified by both the total angular momentum and the parity. As the nanocrystal radius varies, crossings occur not only between electron and hole energy levels but also among the hole levels themselves. The lowest two electron states (in each spin subspace) are surface states, one with even parity and the other with odd parity. The optical selection rules consist of two parts, namely, the parity selection and the angular momentum selection.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Hall Effect in a 3D Cross-Dimensional Quantum Anomalous Hall System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28751)
*Liang-Liang Cheng,Ao-Long Li,Zhi-Qiang Zhang,Hua Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过耦合两个Weyl半金属哈密顿量构建三维量子反常霍尔体系，系统研究跨维度输运与量子霍尔效应，揭示磁场下陈数、手征模式与霍尔响应演化及体-边对应失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维量子反常霍尔体系中的跨维度输运及磁场驱动的拓扑演化机制尚不清晰，尤其陈数与手征模式、霍尔响应之间的关系缺乏系统理解。

Method: 以耦合两个Weyl半金属哈密顿量构造三维量子反常霍尔模型，分析沿三个高对称方向施加磁场时陈数、手征模式与霍尔响应的演化，并结合多终端霍尔输运数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 体系具有高度各向异性的表面态与手征模式，产生丰富的量子霍尔响应；不同方向的手征模式源自不同机制，呈现多样的磁调制特征；磁场下基于陈数的常规体-边对应失效，陈数无法完全刻画手征态演化，该反常行为归因于表面能带独特的动量空间分布。

Conclusion: 研究拓展了对三维拓扑体系中磁场驱动机制的认识，深化了三维系统中量子化霍尔效应与陈数相互作用的理解。

Abstract: We systematically investigate cross-dimensional transport and the resulting quantum Hall effect in 3D quantum anomalous Hall effect systems, which are constructed by coupling two Weyl-semimetal Hamiltonians. Our study unveils that the system hosts highly anisotropic surface states and chiral modes, leading to rich quantum Hall responses. Crucially, we clarify how magnetic fields applied along three high-symmetry directions dictate the evolution of the Chern numbers, chiral modes, and Hall responses. We discover that chiral modes along different directions can originate from distinct mechanisms, giving rise to various magnetic modulation characteristics. Furthermore, we unveil the breakdown of the conventional Chern-number-based bulk-boundary correspondence under magnetic fields, where the Chern numbers alone fail to fully characterize the evolution of the chiral states. This anomalous behavior is attributed to the unique momentum-space distributions of surface bands. These insights are further validated numerically through multi-terminal Hall transport simulations. Our findings expand the understanding of magnetic-field-driven mechanisms in 3D topological systems and provide deeper insights into the interplay between quantized Hall effects and Chern numbers in 3D systems.

</details>


### [115] [Local Pressure Induced Quantum Dot to Ring Transition and Aharonov Bohm Effect in Reconfigurable MoTe2 Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28786)
*Ibsal Assi,A. Belayadi,Hocine Bahlouli,Udo Schwingenschlögl,J. P. F. LeBlanc*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 局部压力可在MoTe_2同质双层中实现可重构量子限制，限制几何可由量子点连续调控为量子环，并呈现可调谐的Aharonov–Bohm干涉效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现二维材料中无需光刻的可重构量子限制结构，并探索压力作为一种调控自由度来塑造限域势与量子环几何。

Method: 对AB堆叠MoTe_2同质双层施加平滑的空间变化压力分布，计算压力诱导的电子参数空间调制与有效限制势；分析垂直磁场下的能谱与持续电流；将压力诱导量子环与源漏电极耦合，计算电导与实空间电流分布。

Result: 限制几何可从常规量子点连续过渡为量子环；环形限制源于有效限制势的偏心极小值，使基态概率密度偏离中心；量子环区呈现通量周期性能谱与持续电流；耦合电极后出现显著Aharonov–Bohm电导振荡，整数与半整数磁通量子处分别呈相长与相消干涉的电流图样。

Conclusion: 局部加压MoTe_2双层可作为免光刻的可重构量子限制平台，并实现压力可调的Aharonov–Bohm干涉测量。

Abstract: We demonstrate that local pressure provides an effective means of engineering reconfigurable quantum confinement in MoTe2 homobilayers. By applying a smooth, spatially varying pressure profile, we show that the confinement geometry can be continuously tuned from a conventional quantum dot to a quantum ring, with the emergence of annular confinement being a distinctive feature of AB-stacked bilayers. This crossover originates from the pressure-induced spatial modulation in the electronic parameters, which produces an off-center minimum in the effective confinement potential, thereby redistributing the ground-state probability density away from the center and giving rise to an annular confinement geometry. In the quantum-ring regime, a perpendicular magnetic field gives rise to a flux-periodic energy spectrum and persistent currents, providing characteristic signatures of the Aharonov-Bohm effect. By coupling the pressure-induced quantum ring to source and drain leads, we further demonstrate pronounced Aharonov-Bohm conductance oscillations, accompanied by real-space current patterns exhibiting constructive and destructive interference at integer and half-integer magnetic flux quanta, respectively. These findings establish locally pressurized MoTe2 bilayers as a lithography-free platform for reconfigurable quantum confinement and realizing pressure-tunable Aharonov-Bohm interferometry in two dimensional materials.

</details>


### [116] [Floquet-Induced Valley Polarization and Topological Phases in Twisted Bilayer Graphene Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28797)
*A. Belayadi,I. A. Assi,A. Johansson,C. I. Osuala,H. Bahlouli,Udo Schwingenschlögl,J. P. F. LeBlanc,A. Abbout*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在反演对称破缺的扭转双层石墨烯异质结中施加圆偏振光，通过Floquet工程实现能谷简并解除、陈数相变与完全谷极化的畴壁通道，并借助二维铁磁近邻效应实现自旋-谷过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在石墨烯基异质结构中可调地操控谷自由度与自旋自由度，并实现可切换的拓扑相与自旋-谷选择性输运。

Method: 理论计算：对扭转双层石墨烯（tTBG）施加圆偏振光进行Floquet驱动，同时用面外电场与绝缘衬底破坏反演对称性；再通过邻近二维铁磁接触引入磁交换不对称。

Result: 识别出谷简并解除的区间：一个谷保持无能隙线性狄拉克色散，另一谷打开有限能隙并变为电子非活跃；Floquet驱动与反演破缺的协同作用诱导陈数 C = -2、-1、0 的拓扑相变，出现完全谷极化的畴壁通道；铁磁近邻产生磁不对称，实现自旋-谷过滤。

Conclusion: 石墨烯基异质结构是可高度调控的平台，可同时控制自旋与谷自由度，为谷电子学与拓扑Floquet器件提供理论基础。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate twisted bilayer graphene (tTBG) heterostructures subjected to circularly polarized light. Inversion symmetry is broken through the combined application of an out-of-plane electric field and the incorporation of insulating substrates, enabling precise control of the valley degrees of freedom and associated topological phases. We identify a regime in which the valley degeneracy is lifted: one valley retains a gapless, linear Dirac-like dispersion, whereas the other develops a finite band gap and becomes electronically inactive. The interplay between Floquet driving and inversion-symmetry breaking induces topological phase transitions characterized by Chern numbers C = -2, C = -1, and C = 0, together with the emergence of perfectly valley-polarized domain-wall channels. Furthermore, magnetic asymmetry arising from proximity to adjacent two-dimensional ferromagnetic contacts enables spin-valley filtering. These findings establish the potential of graphene-based heterostructures as highly tunable systems for controlling spin and valley degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [117] [Interface invariants of rhombohedral graphene with layer-dependent potentials and couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29093)
*Yuelong Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文确定菱形堆垛（ABC）石墨烯在任意单调层间势与键依赖层间耦合下可实现哪些体-差不变量（即畴壁处谷分辨界面模式的带符号计数），并将分类归结为零动量能级占据角的排序问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABC石墨烯在垂直偏压下，畴壁束缚谷分辨界面模式，其带符号数目由体-差不变量固定；但实际屏蔽产生的非线性和单调层间势，以及层间耦合沿键变化，可能改变可达到的不变量取值。需要判定这些情况下不变量集合是否扩大、能隙结构是否改变。

Method: 采用单粒子连续模型：证明m层堆垛的不变量等于零动量能级占据角权重之差，从而把分类约化为能级排序。先对等耦合情形比较非线性单调势与线性势的不变量集合（并附加共同能隙要求）；再对键依赖耦合枚举全部占据构型；用反射对称堆垛构造具备保证共同能隙的大的子族，并用单体能隙下界补全5、6层的完整不变量集合；最后以精确转移矩阵计算界面谱验证预测的带符号交叉计数，并用范数界给出实现给定能隙所需层间耦合的最小离散度。

Result: 等耦合下，非线性单调势实现的不变量集合与线性势完全相同（屏蔽仅移动相边界，不产生新不变量值），加共同能隙要求亦然。键依赖耦合下可达到的不变量范围不变，但范围内能隙从5层起被填满。反射对称堆垛给出保证共同能隙的大子族；结合单体能隙下界，得到5、6层具共同全局能隙的完整不变量集合。示例包括一个完全由栅极定义的8层畴壁，随栅极强度增强每谷共传播通道数由8切换到16。转移矩阵结果与全部预测的带符号交叉计数一致。

Conclusion: 体-差不变量的可达集合对势的非线性和单调性以及耦合的键依赖具有鲁棒性：单调屏蔽不扩展不变量取值，键依赖耦合只填充现有范围内的能隙而不扩大范围。能级排序给出统一分类框架，栅控多层畴壁可实现通道数可切换的谷分辨界面模式。

Abstract: Domain walls in the perpendicular bias of rhombohedral (ABC-stacked) graphene bind valley-resolved interface modes whose signed number is fixed by a bulk-difference invariant. We determine which values of this invariant are reachable when the layer potentials are arbitrary monotone profiles, as produced by screening, and when the interlayer couplings vary from bond to bond. Within the single-particle continuum model, the invariant of an $m$-layer stack equals the difference of occupied angular weights of the zero-momentum levels, which reduces the classification to the ordering of those levels. At equal coupling, nonlinear monotone potentials reproduce exactly the set of invariants of linear potentials, also under the requirement of a common energy gap: screening moves phase boundaries but creates no new invariant values. With bond-dependent coupling, we characterize all occupied configurations. The range of attainable invariants is unchanged, but the gaps inside it are filled, beginning at five layers. Reflection-symmetric stacks realize a large subfamily with a guaranteed common gap, and lower bounds on single-bulk gaps complete the set of invariants with a common global gap for five and six layers. Among the examples is an eight-layer domain wall defined purely by gates, whose number of co-propagating channels per valley switches from 8 to 16 as the gate strength increases. Exact transfer-matrix calculations of the interface spectrum reproduce every predicted signed crossing count. A norm bound gives the minimum spread of interlayer coupling compatible with a prescribed gap.

</details>


### [118] [Population of spin orbit-split-electron branches in semimetallic HgTe quantum wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29487)
*G. M. Minkov,O. E. Rut,A. A. Sherstobitov,A. V. Germanenko,S. A. Dvoretski,N. N. Mikhailov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在栅控 HgTe 基无隙半导体量子阱中，通过磁阻、霍尔效应与 Shubnikov-de Haas 振荡测量发现：当阱宽对应半金属型载流子谱时，电子并不按理想化模型只占据最低的自旋-轨道分裂支，而是同时填充两支自旋-轨道分裂能带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想化模型通常假设在半金属型谱中电子仅占据能量较低的单一自旋-轨道分裂支，需通过实验检验这一图像是否成立。

Method: 在栅控 HgTe 基无隙半导体量子阱异质结构中进行磁阻、霍尔效应及 Shubnikov-de Haas 振荡的实验测量，并可调节费米能级位置。

Result: 无论费米能级仅位于导带内，还是处于导带-价带交叠区，电子均同时填充两支自旋-轨道分裂分支，与理想化模型的单支占据预期不符。

Conclusion: 半金属型 HgTe 量子阱中的实际电子占据情况偏离理想化单支图像，双支占据是普遍现象，相关能带描述与数据分析需考虑两支配对占据。
