Abstract: We present the results of experimental studies on magnetoresistance, the Hall effect, and Shubnikov-de Haas oscillations performed in gated heterostructures with HgTe-based gapless semiconductor quantum wells. We show that in quantum wells with widths corresponding to a semimetallic charge carrier spectrum, electrons, in contrast to the idealized model, populate both spin-orbit-split branches of the spectrum. This occurs regardless of whether the Fermi level lies exclusively in the conduction band or within the conduction-valence band overlap region.

</details>


### [119] [FlakeForge: automated lithography-ready device layout from optical images of exfoliated van der Waals flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29509)
*Or Berman,Ilay Mangel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: FlakeForge：从标记芯片的光学图像到可直接光刻的多层设计文件的开源自动化流程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机械剥离范德华薄片制备器件时，需在显微镜下人工寻找合适薄片并手工设计光刻版图，耗时且依赖专家经验。

Method: 流水线包括：对薄片进行器件可用性评分；利用芯片标记对每张图像进行对齐与尺度标定；将用户器件设计拟合到所选薄片轮廓；为每个接触电极布线至键合焊盘，且绕开此前所有器件以避免重叠。可全程无人值守或暂停供用户审查；适配新材料、新标记版式或器件尺寸仅需配置，无需训练数据；新增器件类型仅需其几何定义。

Result: 在霍尔条与Little-Parks环上演示，约半小时处理四块芯片，相当于专家数天的工作量。

Conclusion: FlakeForge 提供了开源、免训练、易配置的端到端自动化版图生成方案，显著提升二维材料器件制备效率。

Abstract: Fabricating a device from an exfoliated van der Waals flake requires finding a suitable flake under a microscope and designing its lithography layout by hand. We present FlakeForge, an open-source pipeline from raw optical images of a marker-patterned chip to a lithography-ready, multilayer design file. It scores flakes for device usability, aligns and scales each image from its markers, fits the user's device design to each selected flake's contour and connects every contact to a bond pad by a trace routed around all earlier devices, so no two overlap. Demonstrated on Hall bars and Little-Parks rings, it processed four chips in about half an hour, work that took an expert days. It runs fully unattended or pauses for user review. Adapting it to a new material, marker design or device dimensions requires only configuration, with no training data; a new device type requires only its geometry definition.

</details>


### [120] [Kinetic inductance of two-dimensional viscous electron fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29645)
*A. A. Grigorev,A. N. Afanasiev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了窄沟道几何中二维电子流体的线性无功交流响应，发现非局域粘性耗散导致非线性尺寸效应：动生电感因非均匀流分布而增强，并借粘性趋肤效应在碰撞区即主导阻抗，起始频率落在微波频段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高迁移率二维电子系统足够纯净，可进入由动量守恒电子-电子碰撞主导的流体动力学输运区，此时稳态输运系统性偏离德鲁德模型；但对其交流（尤其无功）响应的理解仍不足，需明确粘性在窄沟道中的角色。

Method: 理论分析：在窄沟道几何下求解二维电子流体的线性无功交流响应，考虑动量守恒碰撞与粘性耗散，分析非均匀流分布对动生电感及粘性趋肤效应的影响，并估算其频率尺度。

Result: 动生电感（代表电子流体集体惯性）因非均匀流分布而增强；与扩散输运不同，借助粘性趋肤效应，它在碰撞区即已主导电阻抗；无功行为的起始位于微波频段。

Conclusion: 二维流体动力学导体在升温条件下有望用于高频电子学，其粘性诱导的尺寸效应与动生电感为交流阻抗设计提供新机制。

Abstract: Modern high-mobility two-dimensional electron systems are pure enough to manifest hydrodynamic regime of electron transport similar to dynamics of classical fluids. With the bulk scattering controlled by momentum-conserving electron-electron collisions, these novel hydrodynamic materials demonstrate systematic violation of conventional metallic behavior due to the Drude model in stationary electric transport. In this letter, we study the linear reactive AC response of 2D electron fluid in a narrow channel geometry and show that the essentially non-local viscous dissipation induces non-trivial size effect in it. In particular, kinetic inductance, which represents collective inertia of the entire electron fluid, is enhanced by non-uniform flow profile and, in contrast to diffusive transport, dominates the electrical impedance already in collisional regime by virtue of viscous skin effect. The onset of reactive behavior falls within the microwave frequency domain in the state-of-the-art 2D hydrodynamic conductors which makes them promising material system for applications in high-frequency electronics at elevated temperatures.

</details>


### [121] [Sondheimer oscillations in Weyl semimetal films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29875)
*Lucas Méchet,Louis-Thomas Gendron,Ion Garate*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将Sondheimer振荡的半经典Boltzmann描述推广至I型Weyl半金属薄膜，计入Berry曲率、轨道磁矩与Zeeman耦合对费米波矢和群速度的修正，发现Berry曲率与轨道磁矩会在振荡中产生拍频与啁啾，并给出厚膜与薄膜两种极限下纵向电导率的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sondheimer振荡（外磁场下薄膜中电子在表面间运动导致的周期性电导振荡）已在传统金属中研究，但其对拓扑材料Berry曲率的依赖仍鲜有探索，因此需要将这一尺寸效应引入拓扑半金属体系以理解能带拓扑与边界散射在磁输运中的相互作用。

Method: 基于半经典Boltzmann输运理论，将Sondheimer振荡描述推广到I型Weyl半金属薄膜；在电子色散与动力学中引入Berry曲率、轨道磁矩，以及Zeeman耦合对费米波矢和电子群速度的修正；分别在厚膜与薄膜极限下解析计算纵向电导率。

Result: Berry曲率与轨道磁矩使Sondheimer振荡呈现出拍频（beating pattern）和啁啾（chirping）特征，为拓扑能带结构在磁电导振荡中的可观测印记；并得到厚膜与薄膜两类区间下纵向电导率的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 拓扑能带效应与边界散射共同决定Weyl半金属薄膜的磁输运，Sondheimer振荡可作为探测Berry曲率与轨道磁矩的实验手段，所给解析结果有助于量化能带拓扑在磁输运中的作用。

Abstract: In conducting films subject to an out-of-plane magnetic field, electron motion between the surfaces gives rise to conductance oscillations periodic in magnetic field, known as Sondheimer oscillations. While this size effect has been investigated in conventional metals, its dependence on the Berry curvature of topological materials remains less explored. In this work, we extend the semiclassical Boltzmann description of Sondheimer oscillations to type-I Weyl semimetal films. We account for the Berry curvature, the orbital magnetic moment and the effect of the Zeeman coupling on the Fermi wavevector and the electronic group velocity. The Berry curvature and the orbital magnetic moment generate a beating pattern and chirping in the Sondheimer oscillations. In the thick-film and thin-film regimes, we provide analytical expressions for the longitudinal conductivity, which highlight the interplay between electronic band topology and boundary-scattering in magnetotransport.

</details>


### [122] [Geometric Polarization-Virial Theorems for Field-Induced Dipolar Interactions and Force Reversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30039)
*Shuze Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文提出"类熵"几何结构：立方加权自由能 r³F(r) 在物理标度变换下单调不减，由此导出两个精确的极化位力定理，统一刻画远距偶极体系能量零点与力零点的角向精细结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电场可使远距原子间的吸引变为排斥，但支配这一反转的完整角向力学结构此前未知；能量零点与力零点之间的关系、以及"魔角"的确切角色均缺乏严格的几何与定理性刻画。

Method: 引入立方加权相互作用自由能 r³F(r) 的单调性（类熵几何结构），证明对任意稳定准静态偶极集合与完全延迟偶极对成立的极化位力定理；结合量子谐振子对模型解析求解角向精细结构，并辅以标度律推导与有限球体数值计算。

Result: r³F(r) 非减性统一两个精确位力定理；自由能至多一个零点且能量抵消先于力学抵消——力零点处能量严格为正，能量零点处力仍为吸引；解析得到任意角度的相互作用能与径向力并复现量子微扰结果；传统偶极魔角分裂为严格有序的能量零点角与力零点角，后者唯一界定径向排斥扇区并带正能垒。

Conclusion: 吸引—排斥反转由单一几何单调性原理支配，能量与力的抵消存在严格层级顺序；所得标度律与有限球体结果为从微观偶极到宏观可极化体的实验检验提供可测特征。

Abstract: Electric fields can turn attraction into repulsion between far-separated atoms, yet the complete angular mechanical structure governing this reversal has remained unknown. Here, we uncover an entropy-like geometric structure: the cubically weighted interaction free energy, r^3 F(r), is nondecreasing under physical dilation. This structure unifies two exact polarization-virial theorems for arbitrary stable quasistatic dipole assemblies and fully retarded dipole pairs under stated conditions. Consequently, the free energy has at most one zero and energetic cancellation precedes mechanical cancellation. A force zero retains strictly positive energy, whereas an energy zero retains an attractive force. The resulting analytical angular fine structure is resolved by a quantum harmonic oscillator pair, yielding the complete arbitrary-angle interaction energy and radial force that recover quantum-perturbative results. The conventional dipolar magic angle splits into strictly ordered energy- and force-zero angles; the latter uniquely bounds the radially repulsive sector and carries a positive energy barrier. Derived scaling laws and finite-sphere calculations provide experimentally testable signatures from microscopic dipoles to macroscopic polarizable bodies.

</details>


### [123] [Domain advantage in two-dimensional quantum-device design with multi-modal agentic harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30043)
*Shenhao Miao,Yizhou Li,Liqiao Xia,Ruihao Liu,Dingke Wang,Pablo Jarillo-Herrero,Eli Zeldov,Jiaqi Cai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出 qlaybench 基准与多模态智能体框架 qlaybot，将显微图像理解与器件版图绘制工具连接起来，使语言模型智能体能为二维量子器件自动生成贴合样品的版图，任务通过率达 81.5%（传统工具仅 24.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械剥离制备的二维量子器件形状与位置不规则，光刻版图需针对每个样品定制，目前主要依赖人工绘制，效率低且难以自动化。现有智能体在给定材料轮廓时尚可构建器件，但从显微图像中识别轮廓的能力不足。

Method: 1) 构建 qlaybench 基准，测试语言模型智能体能否将显微图像与实验需求转化为贴合样品的器件版图；2) 仿照人类工作流程设计多模态智能体框架 qlaybot，串联图像解读与图像配准、器件结构绘制、版图检查等工具；3) 依据实际设计经验制定评估检查项，判断版图是否适配样品并将目标部件正确连接。

Result: 在增加计算投入的条件下，使用 qlaybot 的智能体通过 81.5% 的基准任务，而使用传统工具的智能体仅通过 24.1%，体现出显著领域优势。

Conclusion: 该领域优势表明，将样品的科学解读与几何设计相连接至关重要；该路线有望减少二维量子器件制备中的人工绘图工作量。

Abstract: High-quality two-dimensional quantum devices are commonly fabricated from mechanically exfoliated crystals, whose irregular shapes and placement require a lithographic layout tailored to each specimen. Drawing these layouts remains largely manual. We introduce qlaybench, a benchmark that tests whether language-model agents can turn microscope images and experimental requirements into device layouts that fit the specimen. Agents with conventional tools often construct devices when material outlines are supplied, but struggle to identify those outlines from images. Modelled on the human workflow, qlaybot, a multimodal agentic harness, connects image interpretation to the tools used to align images, draw device features and inspect the resulting layout. We evaluate each layout with checks drawn from practical design experience, asking whether it fits the specimen and connects its intended components. With greater computational effort, an agent using qlaybot passes 81.5% of the benchmark tasks, compared with 24.1% when it uses conventional tools. This domain advantage highlights the importance of connecting scientific interpretation of a specimen to geometric design and provides a route towards reducing manual effort in two-dimensional quantum-device fabrication.

</details>


### [124] [A Unified Energy-Density Framework for Moiré Interface Energetics and Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30152)
*Zhangke Lei,Weidong Yan,Zhicheng Feng,Wengen Ouyang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文建立倒空间能量密度框架，统一描述刚性范德华界面（含摩尔条纹）的微观配准、有限接触尺寸与边界几何，给出圆盘与正多边形薄片的能量表达式及边界标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔界面的力学响应取决于原子配准与接触尺寸、边界几何的耦合，现有解析描述难以同时保留离散晶格信息并把微观原子间相互作用与有限接触的能量学联系起来。

Method: 将配准依赖的对势与原子密度在倒空间展开，构建统一的能量密度框架，可兼容不同晶格基与原子种类；推导圆形及正多边形薄片的显式能量表达式，分离面积项与边界依赖项，并把微观褶皱系数与能垒、转动力矩、层间剪切刚度相联系；以直接求和作为基准验证，并以石墨烯上二维冰为分子晶格算例。

Result: 边界分析复现圆盘能垒包络的四分之一次方面积标度；多边形接触依边取向呈现有界振荡或平方根面积标度；给出以摩尔波长、薄片尺寸和边数表示的多边形—圆盘转变无量纲判据；直接求和基准验证有限域表达式，二维冰算例再现相应边界标度趋势；并应用于层间剪切与石墨压缩。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了微观配准、摩尔周期性与有限域几何之间的解析联系，为理解和调控范德华接触的能量学与力学提供了基础。

Abstract: The mechanical response of moiré interfaces depends on how atomic registry combines with contact size and boundary geometry. An analytical description must retain this discrete lattice information while connecting microscopic interactions to the energetics of finite contacts. Here, we develop a unified energy-density framework for rigid van der Waals interfaces by expressing registry-dependent pair potentials and atomic densities in reciprocal space. The resulting formulation accommodates different lattice bases and atomic species, linking single-atom and molecular adsorption to infinite bilayers and finite crystalline contacts. Explicit expressions for circular and regular polygonal flakes separate the area contribution from boundary-dependent terms and connect microscopic corrugation coefficients to energy barriers, rotational torques, and interlayer shear stiffnesses. The boundary analysis recovers the quarter-power area scaling of the barrier envelope for circular flakes and distinguishes bounded oscillations from square-root area scaling for polygonal contacts according to edge orientation. A dimensionless criterion characterizes the polygon-to-circle crossover in terms of moiré wavelength, flake size, and side number. Direct-summation benchmarks support the finite-domain expressions, while calculations for two-dimensional ice on graphene demonstrate the corresponding boundary-scaling trends in a molecular lattice. Applications to interlayer shear and graphite compression connect the same microscopic interaction description to tangential and normal mechanical responses. The framework establishes an analytical connection between microscopic registry, moiré periodicity, and finite-domain geometry, providing a basis for understanding and tailoring the energetics and mechanics of van der Waals contacts.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum Geometry Effects in Lattice Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30210)
*Haoshu Li,Hao Chen,Qian Niu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在晶格动力学中超越绝热近似，分离出Berry曲率与量子度规对电子-晶格响应的贡献；第一性原理计算表明，磁性拓扑半金属EuB$_6$中同时存在由Berry曲率导致的声子手性劈裂与纯粹由量子度规导致、且Berry曲率贡献被晶体对称性禁止的非手性劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何效应（Berry曲率、量子度规）在电子输运中已被充分研究，晶格动力学中的Berry曲率也已证明可使简并光学声子劈裂为手性伙伴；但量子度规如何影响声子尚不清楚，需突破绝热晶格动力学框架加以厘清。

Method: 在绝热晶格动力学基础上引入非绝热修正，将电子对晶格响应中的Berry曲率贡献与量子度规贡献分离开来，并在磁性拓扑半金属EuB$_6$中开展第一性原理计算进行验证。

Result: 在EuB$_6$中发现两类声子模式共存：具有手性劈裂的模式源于Berry曲率；具有非手性劈裂的模式则完全来自量子度规，而其Berry曲率贡献因晶体对称性而被禁止。

Conclusion: 量子度规可与Berry曲率并列，作为独立机制驱动声子劈裂，并可通过声子谱对其实现实验层面的探测；研究将量子几何效应从电子输运拓展至晶格动力学。

Abstract: Quantum geometric effects such as the Berry curvature and the quantum metric are well established in electronic transport, whereas in lattice dynamics the Berry curvature also plays a key role: adiabatically following the lattice, electrons generate a molecular Berry curvature that splits degenerate optical phonons into chiral partners. To determine how the quantum metric affects phonons, we go beyond adiabatic lattice dynamics, identifying Berry curvature and quantum metric contributions to the electronic response of the lattice. Using first-principles calculations in the magnetic topological semimetal EuB$_6$, we find that phonon modes with chiral splitting and those with nonchiral splitting coexist. Unlike the former, which originate from the Berry curvature, the latter arise from the pure quantum metric, while the Berry curvature contribution is forbidden by crystal symmetry.

</details>


### [126] [Anatomy of orbital and spin responses in GaAs and Si: Hall, inverse Faraday, and magneto-optical Kerr effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30297)
*Sanaz Alikhah,Daegeun Jo,Peter M. Oppeneer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在电子掺杂GaAs和Si中，轨道霍尔电导在低掺杂下超过自旋，光致磁化的轨道贡献在宽光子能量范围内占主导；单位轨道矩的Kerr旋转比单位自旋矩大5倍(GaAs,1.5 eV)和13倍(Si,3.0 eV)，结合漂移-扩散模型预测边缘信号以轨道为主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非磁性半导体中的磁光信号在角动量产生与磁光探测两个环节都混杂了自旋与轨道贡献，若仅以自旋极化解读光致磁化与Kerr/Hall信号会产生误判，因此需定量区分二者的相对权重。

Method: 以密度泛函理论、线性响应理论，以及针对逆法拉第效应的二阶密度矩阵处理，计算电子掺杂GaAs与Si的自旋/轨道分辨光学霍尔电导与极向Kerr系数；并将计算结果与唯象漂移-扩散模型耦合，评估边缘信号的成分。

Result: 小电子掺杂下本征轨道霍尔电导在量值上超过自旋对应量；轨道对光致磁化的贡献在较宽光子能量区间占优。轨道与自旋分辨的光学霍尔与极向Kerr系数对轨道矩的敏感度显著高于等量自旋矩，源于轨道运动可直接电偶极耦合、而自旋须经自旋-轨道耦合间接耦合；故Si尽管自旋-轨道作用弱，仍保持可观的轨道磁光响应。单位轨道矩的Kerr旋转在GaAs 1.5 eV处约为单位自旋矩的5倍，Si 3.0 eV处约13倍。在给定扩散常数与弛豫长度下，边缘信号以轨道为主。

Conclusion: 掺杂半导体（如GaAs、Si）中光致磁化的光学探测可由轨道信号主导，不能仅用自旋极化来解释；轨道自由度的直接电偶极耦合使轨道磁光响应在弱自旋-轨道体系中依然显著。

Abstract: Magneto-optical signals in nonmagnetic semiconductors entangle spin and orbital contributions, both in angular-momentum generation and magneto-optical detection. We calculate these contributions for electron-doped GaAs and Si using density-functional theory, linear-response theory, and a second-order density-matrix treatment of the inverse Faraday effect. At small electron doping, the intrinsic orbital Hall conductivity exceeds its spin counterpart in magnitude, while the orbital contribution to light-induced magnetization dominates over broad photon-energy ranges. We show that orbital- and spin-resolved optical Hall and polar Kerr coefficients exhibit stronger sensitivity to orbital moments than to equal spin moments. This contrast reflects direct electric-dipole coupling to orbital motion versus spin-orbit-mediated coupling to spin polarization. Consequently, Si maintains a substantial orbital magneto-optical response despite its weak spin-orbit interaction. At probe energies of 1.5 eV in GaAs and 3.0 eV in Si, the magnitude of the Kerr rotation per unit orbital moment is approximately 5 and 13 times larger than that per unit spin moment, respectively. Combining the calculated Hall conductivities and Kerr coefficients with a phenomenological drift-diffusion model yields predominantly orbital edge signals for the assumed diffusion constants and relaxation lengths. These results indicate that optical detection of induced magnetization in doped semiconductors such as GaAs and Si can be dominated by orbital signals, and therefore cannot be interpreted solely in terms of spin polarization.

</details>


### [127] [Edge-asymmetric magnetism in semi-Dirac quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30343)
*Mustafa Polat*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究三角/六角量子点从Dirac极限到半Dirac极限及更远处的电子与磁性质，发现半Dirac费米子受限时低能态不对称地局域于三角形量子点的某一特定边缘，电子相互作用使其形成边缘不对称磁基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究半Dirac费米子受限体系（尤其从Dirac极限过渡到半Dirac极限）中电子关联与几何限域共同作用下的磁性行为，并寻找边缘不对称磁有序的可能性及其在自旋电子学中的应用价值。

Method: 在三角（zigzag、armchair）与六角量子点中限域无相互作用半Dirac费米子，计算费米能级附近电子态的空间分布；再引入电子相互作用，比较不同体系尺寸下基态总自旋与磁矩分布，判定铁磁/反铁磁基态。

Result: 无相互作用时，三角量子点费米能级附近态在三条边间不对称局域；含相互作用时，zigzag三角量子点磁矩仅局域于单一边缘，形成边缘不对称铁磁基态，且在半Dirac极限之外基态总自旋普遍增加一个单位（ΔS_z=+1），与体系尺寸无关；armchair三角量子点其余两边呈反铁磁耦合，为边缘不对称反铁磁基态；六角量子点同样存在反铁磁基态，说明该不对称构型不限于三角形。

Conclusion: 半Dirac限域与电子相互作用可稳定产生边缘不对称磁基态（铁磁或因边缘而异的反铁磁），具有鲁棒的尺寸无关自旋增量特征，为自旋电子学提供潜在设计思路。

Abstract: In this study, we investigated the electronic and magnetic properties of quantum dots (QDs) throughout the transition from the Dirac limit to the semi-Dirac limit and beyond. When non-interacting semi-Dirac fermions are confined in triangular QDs, the resulting electronic states near the Fermi level are localized asymmetrically among the three edges of these systems. In the presence of electron interactions, we show that these low-energy states favor the formation of edge-asymmetric magnetism in triangular QDs. In particular, the magnetic moments were localized only along a single edge of the zigzag triangular QDs, corresponding to an edge-asymmetric ferromagnetic ground state. For various system sizes, these zigzag triangular QDs exhibit a one-unit increase in the ground-state total spin ($ΔS_z = +1$) beyond the semi-Dirac limit, revealing a striking result of this transition. In contrast, the remaining two edges are antiferromagnetically coupled in the armchair triangular QDs, indicating an edge-asymmetric antiferromagnetic (AFM) ground state. We further identified the AFM ground states in hexagonal QDs, indicating that these types of asymmetric configurations are not unique to triangular QDs. Collectively, edge-asymmetric magnetism could create potential opportunities for future spintronics.

</details>


### [128] [Scanning nanowire quantum dot with radio-frequency read-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30427)
*Aurèle Kamber,Luca Forrer,Valentina Zannier,Fabio Beltram,Lucia Sorba,Martino Poggio,Floris Braakman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出将纳米线量子点单电子晶体管集成于非接触原子力显微镜探针尖端，实现形貌成像与静电势测量的同步，并通过LC谐振腔实现射频反射读出，为高带宽扫描静电测量提供平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描单电子晶体管（SET）是灵敏的电荷与电势局域探针，但其与扫描探针显微镜的高度控制集成困难；且读出带宽仅数十kHz，难以表征更快的电荷动力学。

Method: 将纳米线量子点（NW QD）形式的扫描SET集成在非接触原子力显微镜（NC-AFM）悬臂尖端；用其映射一组纳米尺度偏压栅电极产生的静电势，并与静电仿真对比；将量子点嵌入LC谐振腔，实现射频反射测量读出。

Result: 演示了NC-AFM形貌成像与局域静电测量能力，测得的电势分布与静电仿真一致；成功实现射频反射读出。

Conclusion: 该平台为兼具形貌对比的高带宽扫描静电测量开辟了途径。

Abstract: Scanning single-electron transistors (SETs) are highly sensitive local probes of charge and electric potential, but their integration with robust height control for scanning probe microscopy remains challenging. Furthermore, readout bandwidths are limited to tens of kHz, while characterizing faster charge dynamics is often of interest. Here, we present a scanning SET in the form of a nanowire (NW) quantum dot (QD) integrated at the apex of a non-contact atomic force microscopy (NC-AFM) cantilever. We demonstrate NC-AFM topographic imaging and local electrometry capability by mapping the electrostatic potential generated by a set of nanoscale voltage-biased gate electrodes. Comparison with electrostatic simulations shows agreement with the measured potential profiles. We demonstrate radio-frequency (rf) reflectometry read-out of the sensor, by embedding the QD in an LC-resonator. This platform establishes a route towards high-bandwidth scanning electrometry with simultaneous topographic contrast.

</details>


### [129] [All-in-one Spintronic Sensory Neurons for Magnetic Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30740)
*Jiajia Jian,Bin Rong,Jiaxin Liu,Fangyi Zhao,Kai Li,Tao Huang,Xue Zhou,Xin Yue,Zeyu Ma,Xuefeng Zheng,Weidong Zhang,Jian Fu Zhang,Yumeng Yang,Zheng Chai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于40 nm MRAM工艺的STT-MTJ器件实现"全合一"自旋电子感觉神经元（SSN），利用磁场调制随机翻转将磁刺激直接转化为二值脉冲序列，实现可电调灵敏窗口的概率式磁传感与神经编码的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场可穿透非磁性材料实现非接触感知，是边缘智能（自主识别、导航、控制）的理想信号源；但将磁刺激直接转换为神经脉冲以实现本地神经形态处理仍具挑战。此外，传统隧穿磁阻（TMR）传感的灵敏度—量程固定，缺乏可调性。

Method: 采用40 nm MRAM工艺制备STT-MTJ，构建单器件级SSN：利用磁场调制的随机翻转实现概率式传感，通过电学偏置调节敏感磁场窗口；同一随机翻转过程直接将磁刺激转译为二值脉冲串，在单个纳米器件内集成磁传感、非线性速率编码与脉冲发放。

Result: 获得±40 mT的可及感知范围，平均概率灵敏度2.91%/mT；器件呈现S型（sigmoidal）脉冲速率响应，并基于神经工程框架（NEF）实验验证了磁场编码；基于实测器件响应的10×10 SSN阵列仿真在优化编码下实现90.7%的非接触磁轨迹识别准确率，接近软件基线。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了与MRAM工艺兼容的可扩展方案，将可编程磁传感与神经编码统一于单一器件，为"传感器原生"的边缘智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Magnetic fields enable non-contact sensing through nonmagnetic materials, offering opportunities for magnetic edge intelligence in autonomous recognition, navigation, and control. However, directly converting magnetic stimuli into neural spikes for local neuromorphic processing remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate an all-in-one spintronic sensory neuron (SSN) based on spin-transfer-torque magnetic tunnel junctions (STT-MTJs) fabricated using a 40-nm magnetoresistive random-access memory (MRAM) process. By exploiting magnetic-field-modulated stochastic switching, the SSN enables probability-based sensing with an electrically tunable sensitive field window, allowing adaptive operation across different magnetic-field ranges without structural modification. This tunability alleviates the fixed sensitivity-range constraint of conventional tunneling magnetoresistance sensing, providing an accessible sensing range of +/-40 mT with an average probability sensitivity of 2.91%/mT. Importantly, the same stochastic switching process directly transduces magnetic stimuli into binary spike trains, integrating magnetic sensing, nonlinear rate encoding, and spike generation within a single nanoscale device. The resulting sigmoidal spike-rate response enables magnetic-field encoding, experimentally validated using the Neural Engineering Framework. Using experimentally characterized device responses, a simulated 10 x 10 SSN array achieves 90.7% accuracy in contactless magnetic-trajectory recognition with optimized encoding, approaching the software baseline. These results establish a scalable, MRAM-compatible approach to unifying programmable magnetic sensing and neural encoding for sensor-native edge intelligence.

</details>


### [130] [Two-dimensional Thyristor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30748)
*Purbasha Ray,Rupali Verma,Biswajeet Nayak,Suman Kumar Chakraborty,Mayank Shrivastava,Prasana Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 首次在原子级厚度的二维平台实现基于侧向WSe2-MoSe2-WSe2-MoSe2 PNPN异质结的可重构电光晶闸管，展示正向阻断、突变回跳、双稳态与电学锁存等非线性开关行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维半导体被视为后3 nm节点电子学的可行平台，但晶闸管等关键非线性电路元件在原子级薄平台上尚未被探索，亟需填补多结器件空白。

Method: 采用顺序边缘外延直接生长侧向WSe2-MoSe2-WSe2-MoSe2 PNPN多结异质结；通过开栅条件测试、栅脉冲调制与变温测量，分析雪崩辅助载流子倍增与再生反馈机制。

Result: 正向阻断电压183 V，对应临界电场1 MV/cm；开栅下正向电压陡降175 V并伴随急剧电流涌升与超低维持电压；栅脉冲调制与变温测量证实雪崩辅助晶闸管工作与锁存行为。

Conclusion: 该二维侧向异质结为非线性双稳态开关、锁存、超低待机功耗与自适应逻辑提供新路径，可用于可重构二维电子学与光子集成电路。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) semiconductors offer a viable platform for next-generation electronics beyond sub-3 nm nodes, yet essential nonlinear circuit elements, such as thyristors, remain unexplored on atomically thin platforms. Here, we report an atomically thin reconfigurable electro-optical thyristor based on a lateral WSe2-MoSe2-WSe2-MoSe2 PNPN heterostructure, directly grown via sequential edge epitaxy. Unlike single-junction 2D diodes, this multijunction architecture exhibits thyristor-like nonlinear switching, including forward blocking, abrupt snapback, bistability, and electrical latching. The device exhibits a forward-blocking voltage of 183 V, corresponding to a critical electric field of 1 MV/cm. Under open-gate conditions, avalanche-assisted carrier multiplication and regenerative feedback are associated with a sharp forward-voltage drop of 175 V, a pronounced current surge, and an ultralow holding voltage. Gate-pulse modulation and temperature-dependent measurements indicate avalanche-assisted thyristor operation and latching behavior. This 2D lateral heterostructure enables a new route to nonlinear and bistable switching, latching, ultralow standby power, and adaptive logic, opening the way for reconfigurable 2D electronics and photonic integrated circuits.

</details>


### [131] [Aharonov-Bohm oscillations in transport through interacting quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30753)
*Oliver Oing,Alexander Hahn,Jürgen König,Alfred Hucht*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究含有限内点库仑作用的两量子点双端Aharonov-Bohm干涉仪的电子输运，用图解法在线性隧穿微扰下给出占据数、电流与干涉可见度的解析表达，并以相干度度量（约化密度矩阵四个非对角元之和）数值求解主方程，揭示对称性破缺导致相消干涉迅速被抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点AB干涉仪中相干输运与库仑相互作用的竞争是核心问题；现有工作多集中于无相互作用或特殊参数情形，缺乏对有限内点库仑相互作用如何定量影响干涉可见度的系统分析，以及清晰的相干性度量与解析表达。

Method: 考虑双量子点双端AB干涉仪、有限内点库仑相互作用U；采用图解（diagrammatic）技术，在隧穿耦合强度的线性微扰理论框架下推导占据数、电流与干涉可见度的解析表达式，后者以包含约化密度矩阵四个非对角元之和的相干度量表示；对任意参数通过主方程数值求解该密度矩阵元；在若干对称构型下给出线性电导的闭式解；并以TraSPI方法等替代方法交叉验证。

Result: 得到占据数、电流与干涉可见度的一般解析表达式及相干度量的数值解；对特定对称构型获得线性电导闭式结果；系统分析有限库仑相互作用对干涉可见度的影响，发现一旦破坏体系对称性，相消干涉被迅速抑制；结果得到包括TraSPI在内的其他方法支持。

Conclusion: 有限内点库仑相互作用与体系对称性共同决定AB干涉仪的相干输运性质；相干度量为量化干涉可见度提供了统一框架，对称性破缺下相消干涉的快速抑制是鲁棒的可观测效应，且经多方法验证可靠。

Abstract: We investigate electronic transport through a two-terminal Aharonov-Bohm interferometer with two quantum dots in the presence of finite intra-dot Coulomb interactions. With the help of a diagrammatic technique, we derive, within linear perturbation theory in the tunnelling-coupling strength, analytic expressions for the occupations, current, and interferometric visibility in terms of a coherence measure that involves the sum of four off-diagonal elements of the reduced density matrix. The latter are calculated numerically from a master equation for arbitrary settings of the system parameters. For some symmetric setups, we obtain closed-form expressions for the linear conductance. We systematically analyse the impact of a finite Coulomb interaction on the interferometric visibility, and reveal a rapid suppression of destructive interference upon breaking of system symmetries. Our results are supported by alternative methods, including the TraSPI method.

</details>


### [132] [Interaction-driven ordering of multiple pseudospins and anomalous Hall metals in Bernal-stacked graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30906)
*Jeonghyeon Suh,Kyungjin Shin,Hongki Min*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在Hartree-Fock近似下研究Bernal堆叠四层石墨烯中相互作用驱动的赝自旋与自旋-谷极化序，给出由位移场和载流子密度调制的丰富相图，并识别出σ_xy≈6e²/h的拓扑态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在Bernal堆叠四层石墨烯中观测到反常霍尔态，引发一个基本问题：电子-电子相互作用如何在每个自旋-谷味内组织多个低能赝自旋扇区。作者希望厘清多个赝自旋二重态在相互作用驱动相中的作用。

Method: 采用Hartree-Fock近似，系统分析相互作用驱动的赝自旋序与自旋-谷极化随位移场和载流子密度的演化，并考察两个赝自旋二重态之间的相互竞争与耦合。

Result: 得到由两个赝自旋二重态相互作用塑造的丰富相图：在电荷中性点出现混合味序（mixed flavor orders），在掺杂时出现反常霍尔金属；并识别出σ_xy≈6e²/h的拓扑态，与近期实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多个赝自旋二重态在偶数层Bernal多层石墨烯的相互作用驱动相中起核心作用，为理解实验观测到的反常霍尔态提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Recent observations of anomalous Hall states in Bernal-stacked tetralayer graphene raise a fundamental question: How do electron-electron interactions organize multiple low-energy pseudospin sectors within each spin-valley flavor? Within the Hartree-Fock approximation, we analyze interaction-driven pseudospin ordering and spin-valley polarization as functions of displacement field and carrier density. We find a rich phase diagram shaped by the interplay between the two pseudospin doublets, including mixed flavor orders at charge neutrality and anomalous Hall metals upon doping. In particular, we identify topological states with $σ_{xy} \approx 6e^{2}/h$, consistent with recent experimental observations. Our results highlight the central role of multiple pseudospin doublets in the interaction-driven phases of even-layer Bernal multilayers.

</details>


### [133] [Molecular Pawns on a Surface Chessboard: Computational Investigation of Nanographenes with Atomistic Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30917)
*Antonio Lorenzo Mariano,Jacopo Lupi,Emilio Lorini,Matteo Briganti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 综述了原子级精确纳米石墨烯作为分子量子技术候选平台的多尺度理论框架，涵盖磁性计算方法层级、衬底—分子界面、表面合成动力学与超分子自组装，并强调DFT、自动反应路径发现与自旋感知机器学习原子间势如何连接静态电子结构与介观动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米石墨烯兼具可调控电子拓扑与局域相干自旋态，是分子量子技术的候选平台，但其从电子结构到介观动力学、从合成到组装的跨尺度描述尚缺系统整合，作者因此梳理统一的多尺度理论框架，以期在合成前预测磁交换、光激发与自旋输运。

Method: 综述性工作，构建分层理论框架：按方法层级梳理磁性描述手段；在全第一性原理密度泛函理论之外，引入自动反应路径发现以刻画表面合成动力学，引入自旋感知的机器学习原子间势以桥接静态电子结构与介观尺度动力学，并结合衬底—分子界面模型与超分子自组装的建模策略。

Result: 综述表明，DFT、自动反应路径搜索与自旋感知机器学习势可形成互补链条，将静态电子结构信息传递至介观动力学；据此可在合成之前对磁交换耦合、光学激发与自旋输运性质给出理论预测，并把衬底效应、表面反应动力学与自组装行为纳入同一描述体系。

Conclusion: 原子级精确纳米石墨烯的研究正从单一电子结构计算转向覆盖合成—界面—组装—器件性质的多尺度理论范式，机器学习势与自动化路径发现是其中的关键连接环节，为分子量子技术平台的前瞻性设计提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Atomically precise nanographenes combine tunable electronic topology with localized, coherent spin states, making them candidate platforms for molecular quantum technologies. This review examines the multiscale theoretical framework governing these systems: the hierarchy of computational methods for describing magnetic properties, the substrate-molecule interface, on-surface synthetic kinetics, and supramolecular self-assembly. We highlight how density functional theory, automated reaction-path discovery, and spin-aware machine-learned interatomic potentials connect static electronic structure to mesoscale dynamics, and how magnetic exchange, optical excitations, and spin transport can be predicted before synthesis.

</details>


### [134] [Dirac-like bands and weak-field transverse transport in ABA trilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30924)
*Agustin Montemurro,Fujia Lu,Ellis R. Huntley,Vladimir Fal'ko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在ABA三层石墨烯中，垂直位移场可依据SWM参数取值触发能带边缘反转，并伴随贝里曲率翻转与巨大拓扑磁矩，从而在弱垂直磁场下产生谷劈裂与独特反常电/热电响应，可用于探测能带反转并约束Δ′、γ2、γ5等紧束缚参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABA三层石墨烯低能电子结构由轻、重电子支构成，且可被面外位移场调控；作者希望明确位移场导致能带边缘反转的条件，并寻找可实验观测的判据，同时约束刻画不足的紧束缚（SWM）参数。

Method: 基于Slonczewski-Weiss-McClure（SWM）紧束缚模型分析ABA三层石墨烯低能能带随面外位移场的演化，推导能带边缘反转的解析条件，并计算能带边缘的贝里曲率、拓扑磁矩以及在弱垂直磁场下的谷劈裂与横向输运响应。

Result: 发现能带边缘反转是否发生依赖于SWM参数取值，并给出相应反转条件；反转伴随贝里曲率反号及能带边缘处巨大拓扑磁矩；弱垂直磁场下拓扑磁矩引起谷劈裂，产生可区分的反常横向电导与热电响应。

Conclusion: 位移场诱导的能带反转及其贝里曲率/拓扑磁矩特征提供了探测能带反转的实验途径，并可对Δ′、γ2、γ5等最不确定的紧束缚参数施加约束。

Abstract: The low-energy electronic structure of ABA trilayer graphene is composed of light and heavy electron branches, which can be altered by an out-of-plane displacement field. Here, we show that, depending on the values of Slonczewski-Weiss-McClure parameters, a displacement field can inflict a band edge inversion, for which we identify the inversion conditions. This inversion is accompanied by a reversal of Berry curvature and giant topological magnetic moments at the band edges. In a weak perpendicular magnetic field, those topological magnetic moments produce valley splitting, giving rise to distinct anomalous transverse electrical and thermoelectric responses. These features offer a potential route to detecting the band inversion and constraining several of the least established tight-binding parameters, such as the on-site energy shift for the dimer pair of carbons $Δ^\prime$ and the next neighbour hopping parameters $γ_2$ and $γ_5$.

</details>


### [135] [LeIFF: A localized-electron and ion force field for coupled nuclear and electronic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31029)
*Anthony Mannino,Isidro Losada,Simon Divilov,Michelle Fritz,Younos Hashem,Ashraf Mrabet,Paula Mori Sánchez,Javier Junquera,Mariví Fernández-Serra,Jose M. Soler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出局域电子—离子力场（LeIFF），以自旋标记的球形电荷分布（含动态中心与宽度）表征价电子，构建兼顾基态成键与非绝热电子动力学的紧凑物理模型，可同时描述分子多样成键性质与固体硅的介电屏蔽及集体电子响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与机器学习势以有效势能面替代电子结构，虽可大尺度模拟却排除了电子动力学、电子激发及电子—离子能量交换；第一性原理电子动力学虽能描述这些过程，但计算成本严重限制体系尺寸与模拟时长。已有电子力场精度、稳健性与可迁移性不足，亟需介于两者之间的中间层次描述。

Method: 提出LeIFF模型：价电子用带自旋标记的球对称电荷分布表示，其中心与宽度均为动力学变量；能量由解析的电子动能项、库仑项与色散项，加上经大规模分子数据集拟合的样条插值短程相互作用组成。模型未使用任何固态数据训练。

Result: 所得势能面可刻画多种成键环境与电荷态下的基态性质，并对远离平衡的电子行为给出定性描述；相较早期电子力场，稳健性、精度与可迁移性均有提升。无需固态训练即能描述硅中的介电屏蔽与集体电子响应。

Conclusion: LeIFF为分子与固体的非绝热过程大尺度模拟提供了新途径，在同一物理框架内统一化学成键与非平衡电子运动。

Abstract: Classical and machine-learned interatomic potentials enable large-scale simulations by replacing electronic structure with an effective energy surface for nuclear motion. This excludes electronic dynamics, electronic excitation and energy exchange between electrons and ions. First-principles electron dynamics can describe these processes, but its computational cost restricts system sizes and simulation times. Electron force fields offer an intermediate description by treating electrons as semiclassical dynamical particles interacting with ions. We introduce the localized-electron and ion force field (LeIFF), a compact physical model for both ground state bonding and excited electron dynamics. Valence electrons are represented by spin-labeled spherical charge distributions with dynamical centers and widths. The energy combines analytic electronic kinetic, Coulomb, and dispersion terms with spline-interpolated short-range interactions fitted to a large molecular data set. The resulting energy surface describes a variety of ground state properties across diverse bonding environments and charge states. It also provides a qualitative description of electronic behavior away from equilibrium. It shows improved robustness, accuracy, and transferability relative to earlier electron force-fields. Without any solid-state training, the same model describes dielectric screening and collective electronic response in silicon. LeIFF thus opens a route to large-scale simulations of nonadiabatic processes in molecules and solids, combining chemical bonding and nonequilibrium electronic motion within a common physical framework.

</details>


### [136] [Protected Charge Readout Scheme Utilizing Nonlinear Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31144)
*Andrea Cicovic,Harald Havir,Pierre Glidic,Samuel Andersson,Sebastian Lehmann,Kimberly A. Dick,Claes Thelander,Ville F. Maisi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 非线性谐振器通过频率失谐将传感器与测量信号解耦，从而在保持高读出对比度的同时降低测量反作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米量子器件的电荷检测通常依赖耗散型传感器，信号的吸收会不可避免地引入测量反作用，限制测量精度与量子态保真度。

Method: 梳理了非线性谐振器近期的近单位读出特性，即通过诱导频移产生强信号；并借助数值模拟分析谐振器激发随失谐的变化，验证传感器保护机制。

Result: 频移使谐振器偏离测量信号频率而实现失谐，谐振器激发降低，但增强的读出信号仍得以维持，数值模拟与实验观察一致。

Conclusion: 非线性谐振器能够同时实现强读出对比度与降低的测量诱导反作用，为低反作用量子器件测量提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Charge detection of nanoscale quantum devices commonly relies on dissipative sensors, where signal absorption causes measurement backaction. Recently, nonlinear resonators showed a near-unity readout signal through an induced frequency shift. We show that this frequency shift protects the sensor, as it detunes the resonator from the measurement signal. The resonator excitation reduces while an enhanced readout signal is maintained. Our results, supported by numerical modeling, show that nonlinear resonators can combine strong readout contrast with reduced measurement-induced backaction.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Observation of collective flatbands in hybrid Mie-grating THz metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28246)
*Jie Ji,Thanh Xuan Hoang,Ayan Nussupbekov,Siyan Zhou,Kehan Wang,Yuri Kivshar,Jaime Gómez Rivas*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该论文提出将Mie谐振器与高折射率光栅杂化，构建具有零群速度平带的杂化超表面，并在太赫兹波段实验验证了长寿命磁偶极共振、Q因子调控及Purcell增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超表面在实现兼具零群速度、强局域化和高Q因子的平带方面存在局限，需要新的结构设计策略来光谱调控高Q平带并定制局域电磁相互作用。

Method: 通过Mie谐振器与高折射率光栅杂化构建功能性超表面；制备自支撑THz超表面，利用介质互连形成的栅格介导多极杂化，用偶极激发的近场测量（含空间映射）表征共振响应，并连续改变栅宽调谐磁偶极共振，辅以Bloch分辨的Purcell计算。

Result: 观察到与准平磁偶极共振模式相关的长寿命相干振荡，空间映射解析了介质互连上的共振电磁响应；连续调节栅宽可调谐杂化磁偶极共振并显著提升Q因子，同时保持近平带色散；Purcell因子随Q因子增大而显著增强，随后趋于饱和，体现辐射限制与局域发射体-模式耦合的相互作用。

Conclusion: 周期性晶格中介质互连的工程化设计可作为光谱调谐高Q平带的新策略，用于定制功能光学超表面中的局域电磁相互作用。

Abstract: We propose a novel type of functional metasurfaces created by a hybridization of Mie resonators with high-index optical gratings. We demonstrate that such hybrid metasurfaces possess flatbands characterized by zero group velocity and strong energy localization. We experimentally observe the flatbands in free-standing terahertz (THz) metasurfaces, where dielectric interconnections between Mie resonators, forming the grating, mediate multipolar hybridization and provide a geometric control over collective resonances. Our THz near-field measurements with a dipole excitation uncover long-lived coherent oscillations associated with the quasi-flat magnetic-dipolar resonant modes, while a spatial mapping resolves the resonant electromagnetic response across the dielectric interconnections. We vary the grating width continuously to tune the hybrid magnetic-dipolar resonances and substantially enhance the quality factor (Q factor), while preserving the quasi-!atband dispersion. Complementary Bloch-resolved Purcell calculations reveal the substantial Purcell enhancement with increasing Q factor, followed by a trend towards saturation and highlighting the interplay between radiative confinement and local emitter-mode coupling. We believe our findings establish the engineering of dielectric interconnections in periodic lattices as a novel strategy for spectrally tuning high-Q flatbands in these metastructures, tailoring localized electromagnetic interactions in functional optical metasurfaces.

</details>


### [138] [Picojoule-level self-referencing with dual dispersive waves in thin-film lithium niobate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28304)
*Kiyoung Ko,Ian Christen,Kamila Kunes,Clayton Cheung,Chun-Ho Lee,Kiwon Kwon,Mihir Chaudhari,Ran Yin,Tushar Sanjay Karnik,Mengjie Yu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在单片薄膜铌酸锂(TFLN)波导中结合克尔光谱展宽与准相位匹配(QPM)二次谐波产生，实现电信波段脉冲能量极低的f-2f自参考，16.8 pJ下获得32 dB信噪比的fceo拍频，30 pJ下完成Er光纤激光器fceo锁定，为电信波段已报道的最低脉冲能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统f-2f自参考需要较高脉冲能量以实现足够的倍频程光谱展宽，在电信波段尤其受限。作者希望借助TFLN平台的色散工程与QPM非线性，降低对光谱展宽的能量需求，实现低脉冲能量的fceo探测与锁定。

Method: 采用单片TFLN波导，同时利用克尔效应展宽光谱与QPM SHG提供倍频；通过色散工程在1 μm与2 μm附近对称产生双色散波(DW)，缩短所需展宽跨度；优化设计包括模式交叉型DW、啁啾极化周期，以及将极化区对齐DW产生起点以抑制时域走离；并与未极化的相同波导作对照。

Result: 片上脉冲能量仅16.8 pJ时即探测到SNR为32 dB的fceo拍频信号；在30 pJ下实现Er光纤激光器fceo稳定，为电信波段已报道最低脉冲能量。相较未极化波导，QPM段使拍频SNR提升43 dB，所需脉冲能量降低4倍。

Conclusion: TFLN平台中QPM-SHG与色散工程化双DW的结合可显著降低f-2f自参考的能量门槛；模式交叉DW、啁啾极化周期及极化区与DW产生起点对齐是性能提升的关键设计要素，为低能耗集成化fceo稳定与光频梳提供可行路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate pulse-energy-efficient f-2f self-referencing of telecom-band pulses in a monolithic thin-film lithium niobate (TFLN) waveguide that combines Kerr spectral broadening with quasi-phase-matched (QPM) second-harmonic generation (SHG). Dispersion engineering generates dual dispersive waves (DWs) near 1 and 2 um, placed symmetrically around the pump to reduce the required spectral broadening. With an on-chip pulse energy of only 16.8 pJ, we detect a carrier-envelope-offset frequency (fceo) beatnote with a 32 dB signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and we stabilize the fceo of an Er-fiber laser at 30 pJ, which is, to our knowledge, the lowest pulse energy reported for fceo stabilization at telecom wavelengths. Compared with an identical waveguide without poling, the QPM section enhances the beatnote SNR by 43 dB and reduces the required pulse energy fourfold. We further show that a mode-crossing DW, a chirped poling period, and a poled section aligned with the onset of DW generation to mitigate temporal walk-off are key to this performance.

</details>


### [139] [Cryogenic Luminescence Thermometry of Upconversion Micro- and Nanoparticles via Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28311)
*Pablo José Coria Ledo,Lina Wölcken,Lucas R. Mendicino,Santiago Gliosca,M. Claudia Marchi,Marcos Illescas,Beatriz Barja,Christian Tomás Schmiegelow*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文用一维卷积神经网络(1D-CNN)从NaYF4:Yb3+,Er3+微米/纳米颗粒的单条发射谱直接预测温度（-150至105 °C），在低温敏感度丧失、光学失配等恶劣条件下均显著优于传统荧光强度比(LIR)方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单颗粒或光镊捕获颗粒的发光测温存在信号弱、随对准/收集效率/激发条件波动的缺陷；对Er3+探针而言，低温下LIR失去灵敏度，限制了单谱测温的准确性。

Method: 构建一维卷积神经网络(1D-CNN)，以单条发射谱为输入直接回归温度，训练与测试覆盖-150至105 °C；与标准LIR对比，并人为复现颗粒聚集体的光学失配条件进行鲁棒性评估；同时考察窄温区精度及跨泵浦功率/波长的单一标定能力。

Result: -150至30 °C区间，1D-CNN将单谱平均绝对误差由5.4 °C降至1.0 °C，且在低温下温度不确定度基本恒定（Stark分量特征仍随温度变化）；人为光学失配下，其误差仅由0.58升至0.88 °C，而LIR由1.4升至3.5 °C；窄温区内精度达0.21 °C，且单一标定可跨泵浦功率与波长使用。

Conclusion: 1D-CNN可作为稳健的单次激发测温器，为单颗粒与捕获颗粒的快速测温提供可行途径。

Abstract: Luminescence thermometry with single or optically trapped particles is hampered by weak signals that fluctuate with alignment, collection efficiency, and excitation conditions and, for $\mathrm{Er}^{3+}$-based probes, by the loss of sensitivity of the luminescence intensity ratio (LIR) at cryogenic temperatures. Here we use a one-dimensional convolutional neural network (1D-CNN) to predict the temperature directly from single emission spectra of $\mathrm{NaYF}_4{:}\mathrm{Yb}^{3+},\mathrm{Er}^{3+}$ micro- and nanoparticles between $-150\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$ and $105\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$, and compare it with the standard LIR under adverse conditions deliberately reproduced with particle ensembles. From $-150\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$ to $30\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$, the 1D-CNN reduces the single-spectrum mean absolute error from $5.4\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$ to $1.0\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$ and keeps its temperature uncertainty nearly constant down to the lowest temperatures, where Stark-component features remain temperature dependent while the LIR loses its sensitivity. Under deliberate optical misalignment, its error increases only from $0.58\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$ to $0.88\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$, whereas that of the LIR rises from $1.4\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$ to $3.5\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$. The same approach reaches sub-degree accuracy ($0.21\,^\circ\mathrm{C}$) over a narrow temperature window and a single calibration valid across different pump powers and wavelengths. These results establish 1D-CNNs as robust single-shot thermometers and a step toward fast thermometry of single and trapped particles.

</details>


### [140] [Inverse design of polarization-analyzing gratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28364)
*Kexin Bian,Alexander R. Fiani,Lisa W. Li,Noah A. Rubin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖近轴近似的可微拓扑优化框架，用于设计超表面偏振分析光栅，并在大角度下获得优于传统Jones-傅里叶方法的设计，同时给出初步实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超表面偏振分析光栅的设计依赖Jones矩阵与傅里叶光学结合的形式化方法，该方法仅在近轴条件下有效，限制了器件在大角度场景下的性能。

Method: 构建可微拓扑优化（differentiable topology optimization）框架，对超表面偏振分析光栅进行端到端优化，摆脱近轴近似约束。

Result: 优化设计在大角度下性能优于传统方法所得设计，并获得初步实验验证支持。

Conclusion: 可微拓扑优化为超表面偏振分析光栅提供了一种突破近轴限制的通用设计途径，具备拓展至大角度偏振管理与传感应用的潜力。

Abstract: Metasurface polarization analyzing gratings have attracted increasing interest for polarization management and sensing in optical systems. Traditional strategies for the design of these gratings are based on a formalism combining the Jones calculus with Fourier optics. While this approach has achieved notable success in numerous applications, its validity is restricted to the paraxial regime. Here we introduce a differentiable topology optimization framework for these gratings which is free of this constraint. We show that this framework can yield designs which outperform the traditional approach, especially at high angles. Additionally, we provide preliminary experimental validation to corroborate these results.

</details>


### [141] [Excited-state lifetime and decay mechanisms in erbium-doped ZBLAN using Variable Excited-State dual-Pump Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28398)
*Clement Chan,Thomas J. de Prinse,Jillian E. Moffatt,Nigel A. Spooner,David J. Ottaway,Ori Henderson-Sapir*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于双光学参量振荡器（双OPO）的可变延迟激发方案，通过重构延迟荧光强度谱测量稀土离子激发态寿命，尤其适用于短寿命、低光子产额的能级（如Er³⁺:ZBLAN的⁴I₉/₂），测得准确寿命并揭示新的去布居机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规时间分辨激光荧光光谱（TRLFS）对寿命极短或光子产额低的激发态测量困难，因为其去激发以非辐射过程为主，直接记录的荧光衰减信号弱；因此需要一种间接、可重构衰减曲线的寿命测量技术。

Method: 采用两台光学参量振荡器（OPO）产生脉冲对，两脉冲间引入可变延迟；不直接记录荧光时间衰减，而是测量一系列不同延迟下的延迟荧光强度谱，由此重构激发态衰减曲线。以Er³⁺掺杂ZBLAN中低光子产额的⁴I₉/₂能级为对象进行演示，并以⁴F₉/₂态与传统TRLFS对比验证准确性。

Result: 成功测得Er³⁺:ZBLAN中⁴I₉/₂能级的寿命，并据此提出该能级在ZBLAN玻璃中一种新的去布居机制；⁴F₉/₂态寿命测量结果与传统TRLFS高度一致，验证了该方法准确性。技术适用寿命范围为亚微秒至毫秒量级。

Conclusion: 双OPO延迟重构谱方法为稀土掺杂材料中激发态动力学与能量传递过程研究提供了通用平台，尤其适合传统方法难以测量的短寿命、低量子产额能级。

Abstract: This work presents a new technique for measuring excited-state lifetimes of rare-earth ions. This technique is particularly powerful for states that are very short lived or have a low photon yield because their de-excitation pathways are dominated by non-radiative processes. The technique uses dual optical parametric oscillators, whose pulses are separated by a variable delay. It reconstructs the excited-state decay curve from a series of delayed fluorescence intensity spectra rather than directly recording the fluorescence decay as done in conventional time-resolved laser fluorescence spectroscopy. The power of the technique is demonstrated by measuring the lifetime of the short-lived $^4I_{9/2}$ level in Er$^{3+}$-doped ZBLAN, which is well-known for its low photon yield. The technique enables accurate measurement of its lifetime and suggests a new depopulating mechanism involving the $^4I_{9/2}$ level in ZBLAN glass. Its accuracy is validated by measuring the lifetime of the $^4F_{9/2}$ state achieving excellent agreement with conventional time-resolved fluorescence spectroscopy measurements. The approach is well suited for measuring lifetimes ranging from the sub-microsecond to the millisecond range. It provides a versatile platform for investigating excited-state dynamics and energy-transfer processes in RE-doped materials.

</details>


### [142] [Anisoplanatic Blind Deconvolution using Phase Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28458)
*Shubhankar Jape,Seungman Choi,Peter Menart,Andrew Schramka,Pratyush Uppuluri,Vaneet Aggarwal,Zubin Jacob*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出编码非等晕盲反卷积（CA-BD），用瞳孔面SLM对每次测量施加已知相位编码，使短采集内即获得足够多样性，从而在强湍流与快速成像下优于MFBD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气湍流在超过等晕角的视场内造成随方向变化、随时间演化的空间变化模糊，单一波前无法描述；多帧盲反卷积（MFBD）依赖长时间采集的多样性，在快速成像时多样性不足而失效。

Method: 在瞳孔面放置空间光调制器（SLM），对每次测量施加不同的已知相位编码，无需波前传感器或变形镜，从而在短采集内引入多样性；采用逐像素单层估计器，从编码测量中联合恢复目标与空间变化相位；并用Cramér-Rao界分析比较编码成像与直接成像的相位精度。

Result: CRB分析表明，强湍流下直接成像达到同等相位精度所需光子数约为编码成像的41倍；在较强湍流下，CA-BD在各突发长度上平均优于调优的MFBD，相位MSE在稳态湍流下降低3.5倍、时变湍流下降低2–3倍。

Conclusion: 已知编码SLM图案在数个相干时间内的测量中收益最大，因此更快SLM是其充分发挥时变湍流优势的关键。

Abstract: Atmospheric turbulence distorts the wavefront of light from a distant scene and blurs its image. Over a field of view wider than the isoplanatic angle, the distortion varies with direction and evolves in time, so no single wavefront describes the blur. Multiframe blind deconvolution (MFBD) can recover the scene from measurements spread over a long acquisition, but falls short during fast imaging because of limited diversity across measurements. We present coded anisoplanatic blind deconvolution (CA-BD), in which a pupil-plane spatial light modulator (SLM) applies a different known phase code to every measurement, with no wavefront sensor or deformable mirror, so that the measurements carry diversity even within a short acquisition. A per-pixel single-layer estimator recovers the object and spatially varying phases from the coded measurements. In simulation, a Cramér-Rao bound analysis shows that under anisoplanatism direct imaging needs about 41 times the photons of coded imaging for equal phase precision in strong turbulence. At the stronger of two turbulence strengths, CA-BD outperforms tuned MFBD on average at every burst length. It reduces the phase mean squared error 3.5x for stationary turbulence and 2-3x for time-varying turbulence. Measurements taken within a few coherence times benefit most from the known coded SLM patterns, making faster SLMs the key to its full advantage in time-varying turbulence.

</details>


### [143] [MCP-based EUV ptychography with physics-simulation-trained residual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28912)
*Qijun You,Nuoxuan Yang,Fangrui Quan,Xu Han,Jie Li,Seunghwoi Han,Wei Cao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MCP探测器的EUV叠层成像（ptychography）系统，将硬件与"物理仿真训练"的残差学习网络结合，在无需实验配对数据的情况下恢复衍射图样，实现接近衍射极限的单谐波成像（边缘响应宽度1.08 μm，@57 nm）及波长复用成像（44.4与57 nm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP探测器在极紫外（EUV）实验中具有高增益优势，但存在空间模糊与非线性响应，制约定量衍射成像的精度；同时科学级EUV CCD成本高昂，需要一种低成本的替代方案。

Method: 构建MCP-based EUV叠层成像系统；训练一个残差学习（residual-learning）神经网络，其训练数据来自对探测器退化过程的物理仿真，从而无需实验采集的配对数据即可对衍射图样进行复原（计算补偿预处理），并与未校正数据在相同重建设置下对比。

Result: 经网络复原预处理后，图像质量较未校正数据重建显著提升；57 nm单谐波成像的有效边缘响应宽度为1.08 μm，接近成像几何的标称衍射极限尺度；系统进一步实现44.4 nm与57 nm的波长复用成像。

Conclusion: 通过将高增益MCP探测与计算补偿相结合，该方案实现了高分辨率、多谐波EUV叠层成像，为科学级EUV CCD探测器提供了一种高性价比的替代途径。

Abstract: Microchannel-plate (MCP) detectors offer high gain for extreme-ultraviolet (EUV) experiments, but spatial blur and nonlinear response hinder quantitative diffraction imaging. Here, we present an MCP-based EUV ptychography system that combines detector hardware with physics-simulation-trained computational restoration. A residual-learning neural network trained on simulated detector degradation restores diffraction patterns without experimentally acquired training pairs. This preprocessing substantially improves image quality compared with reconstructions from uncorrected data under identical reconstruction settings. Single-harmonic imaging at $57$~nm yields an effective edge-response width of $1.08~μ$m, approaching the nominal diffraction-limited scale of the imaging geometry. The approach further enables wavelength-multiplexed imaging at $44.4$ and $57$~nm. By coupling high-gain detection with computational compensation, this system enables high-resolution, multi-harmonic EUV ptychography and offers a cost-effective alternative to scientific-grade EUV CCD detectors

</details>


### [144] [Multiscale wave-coupled neural networks for the inverse-design of broadband diffractive optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.28937)
*Peyton Brown,Riccardo Franchi,Roberto Riganti,Jonathan Schuster,Luca Dal Negro*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出受PINNs启发的多尺度波耦合神经网络（MSWNets）逆向设计框架，融合全连接网络与Rayleigh-Sommerfeld衍射理论，用于宽带衍射光学器件设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有衍射光学器件逆向设计需兼顾物理严格性与宽带（多频）性能，希望借助深度学习实现高聚焦效率的宽带器件自动设计。

Method: MSWNets由多个子网络组成，分别学习最优设计解的不同频率分量；网络以描述衍射结构高度剖面的空间输入/输出函数训练，并将Rayleigh-Sommerfeld衍射理论嵌入网络作为物理约束。先在解析轴棱镜的完整复场传播上验证重构能力，再以最大化聚焦效率为目标用于新器件设计。

Result: MSWNets可准确重构解析轴棱镜的复场；用于中波红外（3–5 μm）、焦距f=500 μm的消色差透镜与双合透镜设计，展示出良好聚焦性能。

Conclusion: 该逆设计框架对下一代宽带衍射光学器件具有应用潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce an inverse-design model called multiscale wave-coupled neural networks (MSWNets) inspired by physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) that integrate the deep-learning capability of fully-connected neural networks with rigorous Rayleigh-Sommerfeld diffraction theory. MSWNets are trained using spatial input and output functions describing the height profiles of diffractive structures. Importantly, a MSWNet is composed of multiple sub-networks that provide learning across multiple frequency components of the optimal design solution. First, we validate and analyze the inverse design capabilities of the MSWNets' architecture through training on the complete complex field propagated from the analytical design of an axilens, demonstrating accurate reconstruction. Finally, we employ MSWNets, trained to maximize focusing efficiency, for the inverse design of novel diffractive devices. In particular, we design and display the performance of several achromatic lenses and doublets across the mid-wave infrared region ($3-5\rm{\;μ} m$) with focal distance $f=500\rm{\;μm}$, demonstrating the potential of our inverse-design framework for the next-generation of broadband diffractive optical devices.

</details>


### [145] [A Candidate twin-spine photon model in MacCullagh's medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29001)
*B. Hou,D. A. Sinclair*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将真空建模为旋转弹性连续介质，从MacCullagh单参数作用量导出无源Maxwell方程，并证明横向局域、以c刚性传播的场需线缺陷：最小构型为双脊光子，可解释双折射宏观力矩与电子发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电磁理论中电场与磁场需分别假设，光子与电子发射缺乏统一几何图像；作者希望以单一弹性连续介质（GR可变形度规的低能电磁近似）统一导出Maxwell方程并给出场的拓扑载体。

Method: 采用MacCullagh单参数作用量（对扭转的阻抗）替代分设电磁场；通过拓扑分析证明横向局域刚性传播解必须含线缺陷，构造双脊光子（两个反向位移电流核心）；将电子设为带π扭转相位条件的束缚拓扑缺陷，用共动弓形波剪切与波动方程导向双脊几何，相位条件提供量子化边界条件；辅以SymPy可执行研究记录区分公设拓扑与导出力学。

Result: 无源Maxwell方程可由扭转阻抗导出；双脊光子无损耗、无横向应力传播，具线/圆几何，角动量为0或±ħ；在双折射晶体中差分波速重塑共享脊轨迹，产生连续宏观力矩而无需叠加相位延迟平面波；电子横向加速时残余偶极应变被导向双脊几何，相位条件给出量子化。

Conclusion: 真空弹性连续介质框架以单一作用量与拓扑缺陷统一描述光子与电子发射，提供无需分设场量的几何解释；但其公设性拓扑与推导力学需明确区分，实证检验仍待开展。

Abstract: We model the vacuum as a rotationally elastic continuum, a low-energy electromagnetic approximation to the deformable spacetime metric of General Relativity. MacCullagh's single-parameter action yields the source-free Maxwell equations from resistance to twist, without separately postulating electric and magnetic fields. We show that a transversely localized field propagating rigidly at $c$ requires line defects; the minimal finite configuration is a twin-spine photon with two opposing displacement-current cores. It propagates without loss or transverse stress and admits linear and circular geometries with angular momentum $0$ or $\pm\hbar$. In a birefringent crystal, differential wave speeds reshape the shared spine trajectory, producing a continuous macroscopic torque without invoking superposed phase-delayed plane waves. For emission, we model the electron as a bound topological defect with a $π$-twist phase condition. Transverse acceleration shears its co-moving bow wave; the vacuum wave equation directs the residual dipole strain into twin-spine geometry, while the phase condition supplies a quantizing boundary condition. An accompanying SymPy Executable Research Record distinguishes postulated topology from derived mechanics.

</details>


### [146] [Parallel Two-Photon Lithography: From Optical Nonlinearities to Energy Limits, Dose Control and Throughput Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29010)
*Michalis Stavrou,Panagiotis A. Loukakos,David Gray,Maria Farsari*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 综述双光子光刻的并行化提速方案，并用基于非线性吸收系数的能量沉积框架定量比较各类并行写写的体素尺寸、单个体素能量与体素速率上限，指出高速度下的保真度瓶颈并提出最小报告标准与研究优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TPL可实现亚衍射极限的三维聚合物结构，但逐体素串行写入严重限制吞吐量，需要系统评估并行化路径的性能边界与代价。

Method: 综述固定多焦点阵列、基于空间光调制器与数字微镜器件的可重构全息、混合超透镜阵列、整形光束、时间聚焦投影与双步吸收等并行方案；建立基于实测非线性吸收系数的能量沉积框架，推导吸收阶数与系数对体素尺寸、单个体素能量和可达体素速率的影响，并量化非均匀焦点、焦点间相干串扰、整形光束旁瓣损耗与投影中轴向约束减弱等代价。

Result: 扫描焦点阵列峰值速率超过10^8 voxel/s；在细体素定义下灰度投影可达1.7×10^9 voxel/s；并行特征尺寸小至20–55 nm。速率提升同时带来剂量均匀性、邻近效应、热、拼接与数据带宽等保真度限制。

Conclusion: 提出包含单个体素能量与可持续零件级速率的最小报告标准，并建议领域优先发展共享基准样件、预测性剂量控制与面向并行写入的光刻胶设计。

Abstract: Two-photon lithography (TPL) prints three-dimensional polymer architectures with sub-diffraction features, but writing them voxel by voxel limits its throughput. This review examines how parallelization overcomes this limit with fixed multi-focus arrays, reconfigurable holography using spatial light modulators and digital micromirror devices, hybrid metalens arrays, shaped beams, temporally focused projection and two-step absorption. An energy-deposition framework built on measured nonlinear absorption coefficients shows how the absorption order and coefficients of a resist determine its voxel size, energy per voxel and attainable voxel rate. Applied to parallel writing, the framework quantifies the costs of non-uniform foci, coherent crosstalk between foci, side-lobe losses in shaped beams and weaker axial confinement in projection. Published systems reach peak rates above $10^{8}$ voxels s$^{-1}$ with scanned focus arrays and up to $1.7\times10^{9}$ voxels s$^{-1}$ with grayscale projection under a fine-voxel definition, and parallel features as small as 20-55 nm. As rates rise, however, dose uniformity, proximity effects, heat, stitching and data bandwidth increasingly limit fidelity. A minimum reporting standard that includes the energy per voxel and sustained part-level rates is therefore proposed, together with priorities for the field: shared benchmark parts, predictive dose control and resists designed for parallel writing.

</details>


### [147] [Efficient phase-only wavefront shaping at 250 kHz for multimode fiber endomicroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29015)
*Pau Riera Rodríguez,Antonio M. Caravaca-Aguirre,Omer Tzang,Alexander Payne,Stephen Hamann,Rafael Piestun*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出定制的相位型MEMS调制器（32×256=8192单元、7位相位、250 kHz全帧刷新），在MMF上实现同时具备高速率、高模态维度与高光效的波前整形，并用于体成像荧光内窥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMF波前整形可实现毛发级探针的衍射极限荧光成像，但其速度与光预算受波前调制器限制；现有调制器只能同时满足"高刷新率、高模态维度、高光效"三者中的至多两项。

Method: 采用定制的纯相位MEMS调制器（8192单元、32×256阵列、7位相位控制、250 kHz全帧刷新率）；采用轴上直接相位调制，避免离轴编码的光学损耗与全息图计算；对实测传输矩阵做奇异值分解以确定系统有效自由度；通过多深度焦点扫描实现体成像。

Result: 轴上相位调制将70±5%的入射光耦合进调制光场，并消除离轴编码损耗与全息计算开销；光纤远端生成的焦点保持各向同性且保真度高；SVD分析给出了系统的有效自由度；多深度扫描演示了体荧光成像。

Conclusion: 该调制器同时兼顾高刷新率、高模态维度与高光效，为自适应在体多模光纤内窥成像开辟了新的工作区间。

Abstract: Wavefront shaping through multimode fibers (MMFs) enables diffraction-limited fluorescence imaging with hair-thin probes, at a speed and light budget bounded by the wavefront modulator. Existing modulators provide at most two of three key characteristics: high refresh rate, (high) modal dimensionality, and light efficiency. Here, we demonstrate wavefront control through an MMF using a tailored phase-only microelectromechanical modulator that combines all three, with 8,192 elements arranged in a 32 x 256 array, 7-bit phase control, and a 250 kHz full-frame refresh rate. Direct on-axis phase modulation delivers 70+/-5% of the incident light into the modulated field and eliminates both off-axis encoding optical losses and hologram computation. The generated foci at the distal end of the fiber remain isotropic with high fidelity. We use singular-value decomposition analysis of the measured transmission to identify the effective degrees of freedom of our system. By scanning the focus at multiple depths, we demonstrate volumetric fluorescence imaging. These results open a new operating regime for adaptive in vivo multimode-fiber endoscopy.

</details>


### [148] [Optical Motion Sensing for Piezoelectric-Resonator-Based Power Converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29035)
*Liyang Jin,Zhiyang Cao,Jun Ji,Liyan Zhu,Linbo Shao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于光学干涉的压电谐振器机械位移实时传感方法，可为高频压电功率变换器提供闭环控制所需的幅值与相位信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压电谐振器在高频dc-dc变换器中效率高、功率密度大，但其最优工作点对温度波动与负载变化敏感，需鲁棒闭环控制；而电气式动生电流检测会破坏谐振器机械特性所固有的电气隔离与EMI抗扰性。

Method: 构建由激光器、光纤与光电探测器组成的光学传感系统，利用光学干涉实时监测机械位移，输出含机械运动幅值与相位的光信号；以45 kHz压电谐振器（GaN半桥驱动、输入电压至45 V）进行实验表征与验证，并在99 kHz隔离型压电变换器上演示闭环控制。

Result: 实验完成了光学运动传感方法的表征与验证，并在99 kHz隔离压电变换器上实现了基于该光学传感的闭环控制。

Conclusion: 初步结果证明了压电功率变换器光学闭环控制的可行性途径。

Abstract: Piezoelectric components present a promising alternative to conventional magnetics for miniaturized, high-frequency power converters. While high-Q piezoelectric resonators have demonstrated high efficiencies and large power densities in prototype high-frequency dc-dc converters, their optimal operating conditions are sensitive to temperature fluctuations and load variations. Therefore, robust closed-loop control is essential for practical deployment. Electrical approaches for sensing motional current may compromise the electrical isolation and electromagnetic interference (EMI) immunity inherently provided by the mechanical nature of these resonators. Here, we present an optical motion sensing approach for piezoelectric power converters. The optical sensing system comprises a laser, fiber optics, and a photodetector. The proposed approach uses optical interference to monitor the mechanical displacement in real time. The resulting optical signal carries both the amplitude and phase of the mechanical motion, which are necessary for closed-loop control. We experimentally characterize and validate this optical motion sensing approach using a 45-kHz piezoelectric resonator driven by a GaN half bridge with input voltages up to 45 V. We further experimentally demonstrate closed-loop control using the proposed optical motion sensing for a 99-kHz isolated piezoelectric converter. These initial results establish a viable pathway toward optically closed-loop control of piezoelectric power converters.

</details>


### [149] [Uniguided and unicoupled resonances in an asymmetric acoustic metasurface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29078)
*Andrey S. Konchakov,Daniil S. Buzin,Dmitrii N. Maksimov,Mingzhao Song,Y. Li,Mikhail I. Petrov,Chao Peng,Andrey A. Bogdanov,Pavel S. Pankin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出由反向传播的单向辐射(UGR)与单向耦合(UCR)共振对构成的声学超表面，在斜入射下实现吸收的终极非对称性，并兼具全相位调控与完美吸声能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在斜入射条件下同时实现完全的相位调控与单向完美吸声，寻找一种可赋予超表面"吸收终极非对称性"的物理机制。

Method: 理论分析一对反向传播的UGR与UCR共振：UGR仅向单侧辐射，UCR向两侧辐射但仅能从单侧激发；分别在无损耗与含热黏性损耗两种情形下考察透射相位、透射率、共振强度调制与临界耦合条件。

Result: 无损耗时，透射相位在UCR与UGR处各缠绕2π，而透射率在任意设定的非零水平上保持平坦；引入热黏性损耗后产生共振强度调制并满足临界耦合，仅当声波从激发UCR的通道入射时实现完美吸声。

Conclusion: UCR与UGR共振对为构造非对称超表面提供了统一途径，可同时获得全相位控制与完美声吸收。

Abstract: We theoretically study an acoustic metasurface that exhibits ultimate asymmetry in absorption under oblique incidence. This behavior arises from a counter-propagating pair of uniguided (UGR) and unicoupled (UCR) resonances. The UGR radiates to only one side, while the UCR radiates to both sides but can be excited from only one. In the lossless regime, the transmission phase winds by $2π$ across both UCR and UGR while the transmittance remains flat at any prescribed nonzero level. Thermoviscous losses introduce resonant intensity modulation and enable critical coupling, yielding perfect sound absorption exclusively for incidence through the channel that excites the UCR. Therefore, asymmetric metasurfaces with UCR and UGR offer a route to full phase control and perfect sound absorption.

</details>


### [150] [Acoustic bound states in the continuum in a labyrinthine meta-atom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29091)
*Aleksandra S. Zheleznova,Iuliia A. Timankova,ZiWei Ye,Yunmeng Wang,Jihao Tang,Mikhail V. Kuzmin,Nikolay S. Solodovchenko,Mihail I. Petrov,Andrey A. Bogdanov,Pavel S. Pankin,Yong Li*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文理论结合实验研究嵌入声波导中的迷宫型超原子所支持的声学连续域束缚态（BIC）：超原子位于轴线上时形成对称保护BIC，偏离轴线破坏对称性后转为可辐射的准BIC，表现为透射谱中的窄谐振；用3D打印迷宫超原子在阻抗管中验证，并用双模时间耦合模理论（TCMT）解释可调谐响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用对称破缺调控BIC的辐射泄漏，为可调吸声器、传感器和调制器提供平台。

Method: 在声波导中嵌入迷宫式超原子；理论分析对称保护BIC及其向准BIC的转变；3D打印样品于阻抗管中测量透射谱，与数值模拟对比，并采用双模TCMT进行解析建模。

Result: 无损耗极限下超原子居于波导轴线上时存在对称保护BIC；偏离轴心引起对称破缺，BIC转变为辐射型准BIC，透射谱出现窄谐振峰；实测透射谱与数值模拟一致，证实存在热黏性损耗时仍可实现BIC–准BIC转变；谐振频率、对比度与线宽可通过改变超原子位置调控，TCMT给出与之一致的解析描述。

Conclusion: 在吸收存在条件下可调谐辐射泄漏的声学BIC平台，具有发展可调吸声器、传感器与调制器的潜力。

Abstract: In this Letter, we theoretically and experimentally study acoustic bound states in the continuum (BICs) emerging in labyrinth meta-atom embedded in an acoustic waveguide. A symmetry-protected BIC is supported in the lossless limit when the meta-atom is positioned on the waveguide axis. Breaking the symmetry by shifting the meta-atom away from the center of the waveguide transforms the BIC into a radiative quasi-BIC, manifested by the emergence of a narrow resonant feature in the transmittance spectrum. The proposed concept is experimentally tested using a 3D-printed labyrinthine meta-atom placed inside an impedance tube. The measured transmittance spectra are consistent with numerical simulations and support the BIC-to-quasi-BIC transition induced by symmetry breaking in the presence of thermoviscous losses. The resonance frequency, contrast, and linewidth can be controlled by varying the meta-atom position. The observed behavior is interpreted within a two-mode temporal coupled-mode theory (TCMT), providing an analytical description of the resonant response. The ability to tune radiative leakage in the presence of absorption makes the proposed acoustic BIC platform promising for the development of tunable absorbers, sensors, and modulators.

</details>


### [151] [Reconfigurable Microwave Photonic All-Pass Filters based on Optical Kerr Microcombs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29115)
*David J. Moss*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出基于微梳驱动横向滤波器系统的微波光子全通滤波器新方案，实现高阶可重构、陡峭相位跃变与低损耗，实验验证一/二阶APF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波光子全通滤波器可在不改变信号幅度的情况下实现相位调控，对高带宽微波信号处理的相位工程至关重要；但传统方法难以在滤波器阶数和特性参数上实现高度可重构。

Method: 基于微梳（microcomb）驱动的横向滤波器架构构建MWP APF，通过编程抽头系数（tap coefficients）而无需更改任何硬件，实现滤波器阶数与特性参数的独立及联合调谐。

Result: 实验实现一阶与二阶APF：一阶最大幅度变化（MAV）1.95 dB、最大群延迟（MGD）580 ps；二阶MAV 2.01 dB、MGD 1740 ps；并演示了特性参数的独立与联合调谐。

Conclusion: 该方案可同时实现高性能与高可重构性的MWP APF，适用于需要动态、多样相位控制的实用场景。

Abstract: Microwave photonic (MWP) all pass filters (APFs), which enable phase manipulation without altering signal amplitudes, are essential for phase engineering in high bandwidth microwave signal processing. However, achieving a high degree of reconfigurability in the filter order and characteristic parameters of APFs remains challenging for conventional methods. Here, we propose and experimentally demonstrate a new strategy for realizing MWP APFs based on a microcomb driven transversal filter system, enabling high reconfigurability, sharp phase transitions with high group delays, and low loss. We experimentally demonstrate both 1st and 2nd order APFs, achieving a maximum amplitude variation (MAV) of 1.95 dB and a maximum group delay (MGD) of 580 ps for the former, and a MAV of 2.01 dB and an MGD of 1740 ps for the latter. In addition, both independent and joint tuning of APF characteristic parameters are demonstrated by simply programming the tap coefficients without changing any hardware, enabling highly reconfigurable filter response for diverse phase engineering functionalities. These results highlight the potential of our approach for implementing MWP APFs with both high performance and reconfigurability for practical applications requiring dynamic and versatile phase control.

</details>


### [152] [2D MXene integrated optical polarizers based on TiO2 with high performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29121)
*David J. Moss*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过将二维MXene薄膜集成到硅光子器件上并原位转化为二维TiO2，制备出兼具高偏振品质因子（FOM=17.5）、强功率耐受（>2 W）、宽带（>140 nm）与高良率（98%）的混合偏振器，并实现最高12 dB消光比的微环偏振器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料具有宽波段强各向异性吸收，可实现远超体材料的偏振器工作带宽；但现有二维材料偏振器难以同时兼顾高FOM、功率耐受性与可规模化制备，限制其走出实验室。

Method: 将二维MXene薄膜集成于硅光子器件，并原位转化为二维TiO2，利用转化后显著提升的各向异性吸收与热/化学稳定性构建混合偏振器。

Result: 偏振器FOM达17.5（二维材料涂覆器件中最高），在>2 W耦合平均功率下稳定工作，工作带宽>140 nm，制备良率约98%；微环谐振偏振器消光比高达12 dB。

Conclusion: 该方案在综合性能与大规模制造能力上均具优势，推动二维材料光偏振器向产业化落地迈出实质性一步。

Abstract: Polarization control is essential for modern optical systems. Recently, two dimensional (2D) materials featuring highly anisotropic absorption across broad wavelength regions have emerged as a promising solution for realizing optical polarizers with operation bandwidths far exceeding those achievable using bulk materials. Despite this, existing 2D material based optical polarizers face critical limitations in simultaneously delivering high polarizer figures of merit (FOMs), robust power endurance, and scalable fabrication, hindering their practical deployment beyond laboratory. Here, we overcome this bottleneck by demonstrating optical polarizers through integrating 2D MXene films onto silicon photonic devices and subsequently converting them in situ into 2D titanium dioxide (TiO2). The 2D TiO2 exhibits significantly improved anisotropic absorption and thermal / chemical stability compared with MXene, enabling the hybrid polarizers to achieve a record high polarizer FOM of 17.5 (among 2D material-coated devices) and excellent power tolerance (stable under > 2 W coupled average power), together with a broad operation bandwidth (> 140 nm) and a high fabrication yield ( 98%). We also demonstrate microring resonator polarizers achieving high polarization extinction ratios up to 12 dB. With outstanding overall performance and strong capability for large-scale manufacturing, these polarizers represent a solid step towards industry ready implementations of 2D material based optical polarizers.

</details>


### [153] [Polarization-independent self-hybridized strong coupling by Brillouin zone folding induced quasi-bound states in the continuum in bulk WS2 metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29204)
*Xiongwei Guo,Fu Liu,Jinhai Si,Xun Hou,Kuidong Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作在同一块体WS2超表面中实现布里渊区折叠诱导准连续域束缚态(BZF-QBIC)模与A激子的自杂化强耦合，Rabi劈裂达165.9 meV且与入射光偏振无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高折射率介质超表面与TMDC强耦合体系通常依赖特定线偏振光才能获得最大耦合强度，源于光学模式的偏振依赖性；作者希望摆脱偏振限制，实现鲁棒、偏振无关的极化激元。

Method: 构建单一材料（bulk WS2）超表面，利用布里渊区折叠将准BIC模式与材料自身A激子在同一结构中耦合（自杂化），并通过改变超表面厚度调控失谐量，测量透射/反射谱的反对交叉行为；同时比较线偏振与圆偏振激发下的耦合强度。

Result: 观察到典型反对交叉，Rabi劈裂为165.9 meV；将入射光由线偏振改为圆偏振时劈裂几乎不变，验证了偏振无关的自杂化强耦合。

Conclusion: 自杂化BZF-QBIC方案为块体TMDC超表面中实现偏振无关极化激元提供了新途径，可拓展任意偏振极化激元器件应用。

Abstract: High-refractive-index dielectric metasurfaces supporting optical modes are ideal candidates for achieving strong coupling with transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs) materials. However, optical illumination with a specific polarization is always required to estimate the maximal coupling strength in such coupled systems due to the polarization-dependent optical modes. Here, we propose a self-hybridized strong coupling between Brillouin zone folding induced quasi-bound states in the continuum (BZF-QBICs) mode and A excitons in the same bulk WS2 metasurface. By changing the thickness of the bulk WS2 metasurfaces, a typical anticrossing behavior with Rabi splitting of 165.9 meV is observed. Such Rabi splitting is unchanged when changing the polarization of the incident light from linearly to circularly polarizations, demonstrating the polarization-independent self-hybridized strong coupling. This work provides a novel method for realizing robust polarization-independent polaritons in bulk TMDCs metasurface and offers new insights for expanding the applications of polaritonic devices with arbitrary polarizations.

</details>


### [154] [Giant thermal transduction via feedback-phase engineering in a thin-film lithium-niobate opto-electro-mechanical oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29263)
*Ran Yin,Yue Yu,Ian Christen,Mengjie Yu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过将悬浮薄膜铌酸锂机械谐振器嵌入光-电-机械(OEM)反馈环路，利用激光-腔失谐控制环路相移，实现有效TCF达+1.24×10⁴ ppm/K（较本征-124 ppm/K增强100倍且符号反转），并支持2:1次谐波注入锁定获得亚6 Hz线宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谐振式MEMS测温依赖机械谐振的本征频率温度系数(TCF)，其数值受限、难以大幅提升，因而需要一种可后处理、普适的方法来放大热换能灵敏度。

Method: 将悬浮薄膜铌酸锂(LiNbO₃)机械谐振器嵌入光-电-机械(OEM)反馈环路；利用激光-腔失谐调节环路往返相位，由Barkhausen判据选择振荡频率；通过温度相关测量与开环相位测量分离反馈相位选择效应与本征谐振器响应，并用闭环振荡频率及失谐相关延迟模型加以验证；同时研究2:1次谐波注入锁定。

Result: 实现有效TCF为+1.24×10⁴ ppm/K，即相对本征TCF(-124 ppm/K)的100倍增强与符号反转；实验证实该巨响应源于反馈相位选择而非无源谐振器本身；次谐波注入锁定获得亚6 Hz线宽、参考限制的频率稳定度，以及76.71–76.82 MHz的可调工作范围。

Conclusion: 反馈相位工程是一种普适的、制造后(post-fabrication)调控手段，可同时实现巨热换能与低噪声且可调谐的机械振荡。

Abstract: Conventional resonant MEMS thermometry typically relies on the intrinsic temperature coefficient of frequency (TCF) of mechanical resonances. Here we show that embedding a suspended thin-film lithium-niobate mechanical resonator in an opto-electro-mechanical (OEM) feedback loop enhances the intrinsic coefficient by two orders of magnitude. By using laser--cavity detuning to control the loop's round-trip phase, and thus the Barkhausen-selected oscillation frequency, we engineer an effective TCF of $+1.24\times10^{4}$~ppm/K: a 100-fold enhancement and a sign reversal relative to its intrinsic TCF of $-124$~ppm/K. Temperature-dependent and open-loop phase measurements confirm that this giant response originates from feedback phase selection rather than from the passive resonator itself. This interpretation is further supported by the independently measured closed-loop oscillation frequencies and a detuning-dependent delay model. The same architecture also supports 2:1 subharmonic injection locking, yielding sub-6~Hz linewidths, reference-limited frequency stability, and tunable operation from $76.71$ to $76.82$~MHz. These results establish feedback-phase engineering as a general, post-fabrication route to both giant thermal transduction and low-noise yet tunable mechanical oscillation.

</details>


### [155] [Ultrabroadband, high-sensitivity time-resolved terahertz spectroscopy enabled by a Yb-based laser system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29295)
*Hong-Yuan Wu,Si-Jia Li,Jing-Long Ma,Guo-Qian Liao,Alexander Holm Ohrt,Long Cheng,Peter Uhd Jepsen,Bin-Bin Zhou,Si-Jie Zhang,Yu-Tong Li*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文研制了基于Yb激光的时间分辨太赫兹光谱（TRTS）平台，单次扫描即可获得至25 THz、频谱分辨率0.1 THz的相位分辨连续光谱，并实现72 dB信噪比，可统一表征量子材料的低频电荷动力学与高能共振激发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快光场在量子材料中诱导出大量新奇的“光致态”，要识别这些态并阐明其形成机制，需要在相关能量尺度上对非平衡电动力学进行全面的定量测量，而现有手段难以在共同时间基准下同时覆盖低频电荷动力学与更高能量的共振激发。

Method: 构建Yb激光驱动的TRTS平台，集成多程池脉冲压缩、自旋电子学太赫兹产生与空气偏置相干检测（ABCD）；单次时域波形扫描给出相位分辨的连续光谱覆盖，并保留大部分激光脉冲能量用于可调激发。

Result: 单次扫描覆盖至25 THz（~100 meV），傅里叶分辨率0.1 THz（~0.4 meV），单次时域信噪比72 dB且波形长期稳定；在本征GaAs上分辨出23 THz以上的极弱声子特征，并定量反演平衡态与瞬态复光学性质。

Conclusion: 该平台可在统一时间参考下同时获取低频电荷动力学与高能共振激发的定量信息，为量子材料中光致态提供更完整的微观图像。

Abstract: Ultrafast optical fields have introduced numerous novel light-induced states in quantum materials. Identifying these states and elucidating their formation mechanisms require comprehensive, quantitative access to nonequilibrium electrodynamics across relevant energy scales. Here, we develop a Yb-laser-based time-resolved terahertz spectroscopy (TRTS) platform combining multi-pass-cell pulse compression, spintronic terahertz generation, and air-biased coherent detection. A single time-domain waveform scan provides continuous phase-resolved spectral coverage to 25 THz ($\sim$100 meV) with a Fourier resolution of 0.1 THz ($\sim$0.4 meV). This capability enables the characterization of both low-frequency charge dynamics and higher-energy resonant excitations in quantum materials under a common temporal reference. The system achieves a single-scan time-domain signal-to-noise ratio of 72 dB with long-term waveform stability, while preserving most of the laser pulse energy for tunable excitation. Measurements on intrinsic GaAs resolve extremely weak phonon features above 23 THz and demonstrate quantitative retrieval of equilibrium and transient complex optical properties. The resulting platform can provide a more complete microscopic picture of light-induced states in quantum materials.

</details>


### [156] [Turbulence Strength Estimation from Multiplexed Orbital Angular Momentum Beams Using Multimode Fibre Speckle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29382)
*Warwick G. C. Brown,Mitchell A. Cox*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出利用多模光纤将复用OAM光束转化为散斑，并借助极限学习机（ELM）对相机像素加权求和、多帧平均，无需已知模式成分即可直接估计Fried参数（湍流强度）。在实验室模拟湍流下，11个OAM模式叠加的估计精度与高斯探测光束相当；除弱湍流外，还优于理想Strehl计与理想tip/tilt传感器，为模分复用系统提供高效湍流强度估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流强度决定自由空间光链路中模式混合程度，但现有测量依赖独立仪器或高斯探测光束，未利用结构光链路本身已承载的光束，造成额外硬件开销与系统复杂度。

Method: 将复用OAM光束经无源多模光纤散射成相机上的散斑图样；用训练好的极限学习机（ELM，即相机像素的加权求和）对几十帧散斑平均后输出Fried参数估计；且无需知悉所传输的具体模式成分。实验采用模拟湍流，测试最多11个OAM模式叠加的情形。

Result: 估计精度与同一光纤下高斯探测光束相当；在除弱湍流以外的条件下，精度优于理想Strehl计和理想tip/tilt传感器。

Conclusion: 该方案可直接复用链路中已有的OAM模式实现湍流强度在线估计，无需独立仪器或额外探测光，为模分复用自由空间光系统提供高效、低成本的湍流强度监测手段。

Abstract: The strength of atmospheric turbulence governs how much the modes of a free-space optical link mix together. It is usually measured with a separate instrument or with a Gaussian probe beam, neither of which uses the beams that a structured-light link already carries. Here we show that the turbulence strength, expressed as the Fried parameter, can be estimated directly from multiplexed orbital angular momentum (OAM) beams without knowing which modes are present. The beams pass through a passive multimode fibre that turns them into speckle on a camera, and a trained weighted sum of the camera pixels (known as an extreme learning machine), averaged over a few tens of frames, gives the Fried parameter. In the laboratory with emulated turbulence, superpositions of up to eleven OAM modes give an estimate as accurate as that from a Gaussian probe beam sent through the same fibre. In all but weak turbulence the estimate is also more accurate than an ideal Strehl meter or an ideal tip/tilt sensor, providing an efficient way to estimate turbulence strength in mode-division multiplexed systems.

</details>


### [157] [Polarization-cloaked spatiotemporal topological knots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29498)
*Yaning Zhou,Nianjia Zhang,Zhao Zhang,Chunhao Liang,Qian Cao,Houan Teng,Qiwen Zhan,Sergey A. Ponomarenko,Xu Guo,Yangjian Cai,Xin Liu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 将反向缠绕的环形涡旋模式与正交圆偏振态在高阶庞加莱球上组合，构建矢量电磁时空波包，使拓扑连通性编码于偏振分量间的相对相位中，实现可重构的三维偏振结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑结直接由标量复场识别，且依赖贯穿三维空间的纯空间本征模式；用偏振自由度对超快局域波包"打结"难以实现，限制了时空拓扑的控制维度。

Method: 在高阶庞加莱球上将两束相反缠绕的环形涡旋模式与正交圆偏振态叠加，合成矢量电磁时空波包；通过斯托克斯参量分析（S_0 保持公共环形包络，S_1、S_2 呈现依赖缠绕的链环/结轨迹），并以数值模拟验证轨迹闭合，用时间切片离轴干涉术实验重建三维 S_2 织构。

Result: S_0 保持共同的环形包络，而相位敏感的 S_1、S_2 显示出与缠绕数相关的链环与结轨迹；数值仿真证实其闭合性，实验重建出相应的三维 S_2 纹理。

Conclusion: 将拓扑连通性与标量强度包络解耦，把时空拓扑调控拓展至偏振自由度，为多维结构光中拓扑信息访问方式提供通用框架。

Abstract: Spatiotemporally localized topological knots encode global connectivity within a moving space-time optical wavepacket, circumventing the requirement for spatial-only eigenmodes that extend throughout three-dimensional space-filling. In conventional modalities, this connectivity is identified directly from a scalar complex field. However, knotting optical knots with the polarization state of light proves difficult to realize for ultrafast localized wavepackets. In this work, we combine oppositely wound toroidal vortex modes with orthogonal circular polarization states on the higher-order Poincaré sphere to construct vectorial electromagnetic spatiotemporal wavepackets, in which the topological connectivity is encoded in the relative phase between the polarization components. Stokes analysis shows that S_0 retains a common toroidal envelope, whereas the phase sensitive Stokes parameters S_1 and S_2 reveal winding-dependent linked and knotted trajectories; numerical simulations verify their closure, and time-sliced off-axis interferometry experimentally reconstructs the corresponding three-dimensional S_2 textures. This separation of topological connectivity from the scalar intensity envelope extends the control of spatiotemporal topology to the polarization degree of freedom and provides a versatile framework for controlling how topological information is accessed in multidimensional structured light.

</details>


### [158] [Pseudo-Altermagnetism in Non-Hermitian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.29976)
*Chang-Yin Ji,Runze Li,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: physics.optics
