TL;DR: 该文在非厄米系统中引入"赝交错磁性"(pseudo-altermagnetism)，以平衡增益/损耗模拟交错磁自旋，并以满足 O k ≠ k 的反幺正对称 O 联系增益与损耗子晶格；以 C4z·T 对称的二维系统为例揭示了动量依赖的增益损耗分布及可移动但不可移除的节点结构，并在非厄米光子晶体中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁性(altermagnetism)受到广泛关注，其类比已在多种经典平台被探索；作者希望将这一概念推广到非厄米系统，用平衡增益与损耗替换交错磁自旋，从而建立非厄米物理与交错磁性之间的对称性桥梁，并寻找超越 PT 对称的新型厄米谱机制。

Method: 提出"赝交错磁性"概念：子晶格对应相反增益与损耗，由反幺正对称 O 联系，且对一般动量 k 有 O k ≠ k。以具 O = C4z·T 对称的二维非厄米系统为代表性模型，分析能带的对称配对与节点结构；并进一步在非厄米光子晶体中演示。

Result: 赝交错磁性产生类似交错磁动量依赖自旋劈裂的动量依赖增益损耗分布，并具内禀非厄米特性。C4z·T 不变系统的能带要么孤立、要么由 C4z·T 成对，两种情况均保证存在可移动但不可移除的节点结构（由两个不同量之零点刻画）。孤立能带具有两条可移动但不可移除的厄米谱线，其一般点不在 C4z·T 下不变——与通常仅在反幺正对称固定点处强制厄米谱的情形形成鲜明对比；成对能带则形成由实部与虚部简并线之并集构成的可移动但不可移除的节点网络，其交点一般为奇异点(exceptional points)。

Conclusion: 结果建立了非厄米物理与交错磁性之间基于对称性的桥梁，并提供了超越 PT 对称的厄米谱新机制。

Abstract: Altermagnetism has attracted growing attention, and its analogs have been actively explored across classical platforms. Here, we introduce pseudo-altermagnetism in non-Hermitian systems, where balanced gain and loss plays the role of the altermagnetic spin, and the opposite gain and loss sublattices are related by an antiunitary symmetry $O$ with $O\bm k\neq \bm k$ for generic momentum $\bm k$. Using a two-dimensional non-Hermitian system with $O=C_{4z}\mathcal{T}$ symmetry as a representative example, we find that pseudo-altermagnetism not only leads to a momentum-dependent gain and loss distribution analogous to the momentum-dependent spin splitting of altermagnets, but also exhibits intrinsic non-Hermitian properties. The bands of $C_{4z}\mathcal{T}$-invariant non-Hermitian systems are either isolated or paired by $C_{4z}\mathcal{T}$, and both cases are guaranteed to host movable but unremovable nodal structures, characterized by the zeros of two distinct quantities. An isolated band exhibits two movable but unremovable Hermitian spectral lines, whose generic points are not invariant under $C_{4z}\mathcal{T}$, in sharp contrast to the conventional Hermitian spectra enforced only at the fixed points of an antiunitary symmetry. The paired bands, in turn, feature a movable but unremovable nodal network formed by the union of real-part and imaginary-part degeneracy lines, whose intersections are generally exceptional points. We further demonstrate these findings in a non-Hermitian photonic crystal. Our results establish a symmetry-based bridge between non-Hermitian physics and altermagnetism, and provide novel mechanisms of Hermitian spectra beyond $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry.

</details>


### [159] [Designing Incoherent Multispectral Diffractive Optical Networks for Satellite Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30079)
*Xilin Yang,Md Sadman Sakib Rahman,Ryan Lee,Aydogan Ozcan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种以计算高效的代理模型引导设计非相干多光谱衍射光学网络的方法，使网络在星上直接输出类别概率，无需传输完整多光谱数据立方体；六通道三分类卫星场景识别盲测精度79.8%，接近代理模型的81.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多光谱卫星成像需将高维图像立方体传回地面做电子学分析，通信与后处理负担重；光学神经网络与衍射光学计算具备高并行、轻量电子后处理的优势，适合星上任务特定推理，但缺乏高效、可迁移的设计框架。

Method: 1）先训练物理兼容的代理模型——非负全连接模型，兼容广义探测器读出，得到任务特定的非负多光谱强度变换；2）在空间非相干照明下，通过无数据的点扩散函数优化合成其衍射对应物，将强度变换迁移至光学处理器。

Result: 六光谱通道衍射分类器实现人工地表、水体、植被三类卫星场景识别，盲测精度79.8%，接近代理模型的81.8%；更紧凑的三通道设计盲测精度72.7%，对应代理模型为75.1%。

Conclusion: 该代理模型引导策略为资源受限遥感推理提供了设计紧凑、任务特定非相干多光谱光学处理器的可行途径。

Abstract: Optical neural networks and diffractive optical computing provide a promising route for performing task-specific inference with high parallelism and lightweight electronic post-processing. These advantages are especially attractive for multispectral satellite imaging, where conventional workflows rely on transmitting high-dimensional image cubes to the ground for downstream electronic analysis. Here, we introduce a computationally efficient surrogate model-guided framework for designing incoherent multispectral diffractive optical networks that directly output class probabilities onboard, eliminating the need to capture and transfer the full multispectral data cube for ground-side inference. We first train a physically compatible surrogate model, represented by a non-negative fully connected model that accommodates a generalized detector readout, yielding a task-specific non-negative multispectral intensity transformation that can be transferred to an optical processor. We then synthesize its diffractive counterpart through data-free point-spread-function-based optimization under spatially incoherent illumination. Using this approach, we numerically demonstrate a six-spectral-channel diffractive classifier for tri-class satellite-scene recognition, achieving a blind-testing accuracy of 79.8% for artificial surface, water body, and vegetated land images, closely approaching the 81.8% accuracy of its surrogate. We further demonstrate a more compact three-spectral-channel design that numerically achieves 72.7% blind-testing accuracy, compared with 75.1% for its corresponding surrogate model. This surrogate model-guided strategy provides a practical pathway for designing compact, task-specific incoherent multispectral optical processors for resource-constrained remote-sensing inference.

</details>


### [160] [Strain-driven terahertz phase modulation in piezoelectric and nonlinear optical crystals: mechanisms, material developments, and modulation strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30194)
*Aarifa Muhammed Ashraf,Rajaboopathi Mani*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 综述太赫兹调制技术的最新进展，重点讨论二维半导体、超材料在外场（光泵浦、热场、电场）刺激下的响应，以及非中心对称压电材料中应变驱动的压电共振型THz调制，并总结关键性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: THz技术在传感、光谱、成像与无线通信领域的发展提升了对高性能THz调制器的需求，而现有外场调控策略各有局限，亟需系统梳理不同材料体系及新型调控机制（如应变驱动）的潜力。

Method: 文献综述方法：分类梳理基于二维半导体、超材料等材料的THz调制器在外场刺激下的响应；分析有机/半有机压电晶体在压电共振下实现THz波与晶格振动强耦合的应变驱动调制；评述LiNbO3等非线性光学晶体的应变驱动调制报道及候选材料；归纳可调材料集成调制器的工作原理与性能指标。

Result: 总结了光泵浦、热场、电场等外场调控下各类材料THz调制的响应特性；揭示了压电共振条件下THz波与晶格振动强耦合是实现应变驱动调制的物理基础；识别出若干具压电共振峰、适用于应变驱动的非线性光学晶体候选体系；并给出调制速度、带宽、调制深度与插入损耗等关键指标的比较框架。

Conclusion: 应变驱动压电共振调制为THz调制提供了一条区别于传统外场调控的新路径，结合二维材料与超材料有望进一步提升调制性能；未来需在调制速度、带宽、深度与插入损耗之间取得平衡，并拓展高效可调材料体系。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz) technology has witnessed notable developments in sensing, spectroscopy, imaging, and wireless communication which have increased the demand for high-performance THz modulators. Among the various approaches for controlling the THz waves, the application of external stimuli such as optical pumping and thermal or electric fields is widely employed as a promising strategy for modulation. This review focuses on recent advances in THz modulation based on different materials including 2D semiconductors and metamaterials with attention to their response under external stimuli. In addition, strain-driven THz modulation in non-centrosymmetric piezoelectric materials including organic and semi-organic crystals is examined with an emphasis on their ability to establish strong coupling between propagating THz waves and lattice vibrations under piezoelectric resonance conditions. We further highlight earlier reports on strain-driven THz modulation in lithium niobate (LiNbO3) and critically assess the potential of several other nonlinear optical crystals exhibiting piezoelectric resonance peaks suitable for strain-driven THz modulation. An overview of THz modulators integrated with tunable materials is presented highlighting their operating principles and key performance metrics comprising modulation speed, bandwidth, modulation depth and insertion loss.

</details>


### [161] [Polarization switching of double azimuthal backflow in two transverse orbital angular momentum superpositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30213)
*Zhen-Lai Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 两束等强度、不同拓扑电荷的旋转波结构光干涉可产生方位向光学回流；该回流同时使坡印廷动量与正则动量的方位分量反转，且仅由两束光的偏振态开关，无需调节振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学回流（backflow）通常指局域动量与波矢方向相反的反常现象，已有研究多集中于线向或单动量（坡印廷或正则动量）。本文动机在于探究结构光干涉能否在方位向同时产生两种动量的回流，并寻找一个简洁、无需振幅调制的开关机制。

Method: 理论上分析两束具有不同横向拓扑电荷、等强度的旋转波结构光的干涉：分别计算干涉场中坡印廷动量与正则动量的方位分量，考察其在角向扇区内的符号反转（回流）；引入单一“偏振重叠”因子，分析其仅进入两种动量的干涉项而不进入单束项，从而以偏振匹配/失配实现开关；并解析角向周期性及其与拓扑电荷差的关系。

Result: 干涉使坡印廷动量与正则动量的方位分量同时反转；虽然二者一般不相互绑定，但在此处于共同角向扇区同步获得方位向回流，而径向间隔可以不同。双回流完全由两束光的偏振切换：偏振匹配时开启，失配时关闭。双回流沿方位向周期分布，其角周期由拓扑电荷差锁定，与各自电荷的绝对值无关。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一种偏振门控方位向双回流的矢量机制，为偏振可切换的光学操控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Optical backflow in the azimuthal direction can be generated by the interference of two rotating-wave structured light beams with distinct transverse topological charges and equal intensity. Here we show that the interference reverses both azimuthal components of the Poynting and the canonical momenta. Although these two momenta are not generally tied, they reverse jointly here: both acquire azimuthal backflow in a common angular sector, while their radial intervals can differ. The double backflow is switched solely by the polarizations of the two beams, without amplitude tuning. A single polarization overlap enters the interference terms of both momenta but not the single-beam terms of either, so that matched polarizations switch the double backflow on whereas mismatched ones switch it off. The double backflow is periodic in the azimuthal direction, with its angular period locked to the topological charge difference and independent of the individual charges. Our results establish a vectorial mechanism for polarization-gated azimuthal backflow, with potential applications in polarization-switchable optical manipulation.

</details>


### [162] [Computation in flight through precision-processed optical fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30266)
*Kailai Liu,Zhongyao Luo,Xiangpeng Xiao,Zhen Wang,Weiliang Zhao,Mingming Zhang,Hao Wu,Zhijun Yan,Ming Tang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出FBG-CA，一种通过自动透涂写制弱光纤布拉格光栅构成的被动计算模块，在同一光传播事件中同时完成数据传输与卷积，并在ML分类与信号恢复任务上验证精度、吞吐与波长可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式感知与计算系统中，光电转换、缓存与调度使数据搬移成为瓶颈，需要在不打断传输的前提下于光域内直接完成计算。

Method: 以自动化透涂写制工艺在光纤中刻写空间分布的弱FBG阵列，使后向反射信号对时域样本进行延迟累加实现卷积，前向信号保留原始数据流送达远端接收端；针对有限反射率分辨率与制造偏差，将量化感知训练与锐度感知最小化（SAM）联合优化；分别实现单波长（1440 m计算光纤）与4波长WDM（144 m）版本。

Result: 单波长下卷积相关系数0.987，MNIST 87.90%、MIT-BIH心律失常79.07%，估计吞吐288 GOPS；约20%前向功率损耗（0.97 dB）下，恢复MNIST图像平均SSIM 0.9930，恢复心律波形MSE 0.005826；4波长WDM下Fashion-MNIST四分类92.25%，吞吐115.2 GOPS；架构级投影显示8波长、100 GBaud/波长时本征吞吐密度达1.6 POPS/m。

Conclusion: FBG-CA验证了光纤中被动、波长可扩展的"飞行中计算"（computation-in-flight），可在传输同时完成卷积，兼具高精度与高吞吐潜力。

Abstract: In distributed sensing and computing systems, optoelectronic conversion, buffering, and scheduling can make data movement a bottleneck. We propose FBG-CA, a passive computing module fabricated by automated through-coating inscription of weak fiber Bragg gratings (FBGs). The spatially distributed FBGs reflect, delay, and accumulate temporal samples to produce a convolution output in the backward direction, while the forward signal carries the original data stream to a remote receiver. This enables transmission and convolution within the same optical propagation event. To mitigate finite reflectivity resolution and fabrication deviations, we combine quantization-aware training with sharpness-aware minimization. Over 1440 m of computing fiber, the single-wavelength implementation achieves a convolution correlation coefficient of 0.987, classification accuracies of 87.90% on MNIST and 79.07% on MIT-BIH Arrhythmia, and an estimated throughput of 288 GOPS. With approximately 20% forward-power reduction (0.97 dB) across the FBG sections, the recovered MNIST images achieve a mean structural similarity index of 0.9930, and the recovered MIT-BIH heartbeat waveforms have a mean squared error of 0.005826. Using four wavelength-division-multiplexed channels over 144 m of computing fiber, FBG-CA achieves 92.25% accuracy on four-class Fashion-MNIST and an estimated throughput of 115.2 GOPS. An architecture-level projection indicates an intrinsic throughput density of 1.6 POPS/m for eight wavelengths at 100 GBaud per wavelength. These results demonstrate passive, wavelength-scalable computation-in-flight in optical fiber.

</details>


### [163] [SPECTRA-RCWA: A Spectral Propagation Engine for Coupled-Wave Theory with Recursive Admittance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30305)
*Hyoseok Park*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出 SPECTRA-RCWA，一种不依赖特征值分解的严格耦合波分析（RCWA）正演与伴随求解器；通过在单精度下批量求解并把目标的所有求解一次成批执行，在大幅加速的同时获得稳定、精确的梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCWA 是周期超表面标准全波求解器，但每一层都要对稠密非对称矩阵做对角化；逆设计需对每个波长、角度与偏振重复该步骤，在 GPU 上成为瓶颈，且当特征值简并或衍射级到达截止时导数失效。因此需要避免对角化且梯度稳定的求解方案。

Method: 不再对角化层矩阵：每层步用三个矩阵整函数（仅靠矩阵乘法求值）作用于半尺寸层矩阵；用导纳递推，或对厚层采用散射矩阵倍化（scattering-matrix doubling）以保持倏逝级稳定；一次伴随场即给出全部设计参数的梯度，无需计算图；一个目标的所有求解合并为单个批次。整体支持单精度。

Result: 144 个 578×578 层矩阵求解的数值与梯度在单张 GPU 上耗时 0.25 s，比可微特征分解代码快 218 倍；相比截断级数的 TorchRDIT，在数微米级厚度层上无需切片即保持单精度精度，匹配精度下快 47–65 倍；截止处单精度梯度与双精度参考之差在 4e-5 以内，而特征分解梯度误差为一阶量级。

Conclusion: SPECTRA-RCWA 回避对角化，兼顾速度、厚层稳定性与梯度精度；在台式 CPU 上 3.4 min 完成自由形状超光栅设计，双偏振 65° 偏折，带平均效率 75%。

Abstract: Rigorous coupled-wave analysis (RCWA) is the standard full-wave solver for periodic metasurfaces, but every solve diagonalises a dense nonsymmetric matrix for each layer. Inverse design repeats this step for every wavelength, angle and polarisation of an objective. On a graphics processor it is the slow step, and its derivative fails where eigenvalues coincide or a diffraction order reaches cutoff. Here we introduce SPECTRA-RCWA, an RCWA forward and adjoint solver that never diagonalises the layer matrix. Each layer step uses three entire functions of the half-size layer matrix, evaluated by matrix products alone, and an admittance recursion, or scattering-matrix doubling for thick layers, keeps the evanescent orders stable. One adjoint field gives the gradient for every design value without a computational graph, and all solves of an objective run as one batch. For 144 solves with 578 x 578 layer matrices, the value and the gradient take 0.25 s on one graphics card, 218 times less than with a differentiable eigendecomposition code. The truncated-series solver TorchRDIT loses accuracy beyond a few tenths of a micrometre unless the user slices the layer; SPECTRA-RCWA keeps single-precision accuracy in layers several wavelengths thick with no setting changed and is 47 to 65 times faster at matched accuracy. Its single-precision gradient stays within 4e-5 of a double-precision reference at a cutoff, where eigendecomposition gradients are wrong by order one. A freeform metagrating designed in 3.4 min on a desktop processor deflects both polarisations by 65 degrees with a band-averaged efficiency of 75%.

</details>


### [164] [Dark and bright solitons in a multimodal lithium tantalate microresonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30313)
*Huizong Zhu,Yuyao Guo,Xiangfei Cheng,Yiqun Wu,Juanjuan Lu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过在LiTaO₃微环中利用两个TE类横向模式族（TE₀₀与TE₁₀）实现波长寻址，使同一谐振腔在固定输入偏振下分别产生暗脉冲与亮孤子微梳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亮孤子与暗脉冲微梳输出互补，若集成于单一谐振腔可拓展集成相干光源的工作能力；但二者需相反的群速度色散（GVD）通道，同时还须兼顾暗脉冲形成所需的局域模式间相互作用与独立亮孤子泵浦所需的频谱隔离，二者存在矛盾。

Method: 在LiTaO₃微环中采用两个TE类横向模式族：高阶TE₁₀族在不同光谱区承担双重角色。在局域避免模式交叉（avoided-mode-crossing）区域内，借助模式分辨光谱表明TE₁₀扰动正常GVD的TE₀₀共振序列；远离这些区域时TE₁₀保持反常GVD且频谱隔离，作为可独立泵浦的亮孤子通道。在代表性泵浦共振处，非寻址模式族最近共振相距75–192个加载腔线宽。保持输入偏振不变，分别泵浦TE₀₀与TE₁₀共振产生暗脉冲与亮孤子微梳。

Result: 在集成LiTaO₃微环中首次实验实现暗脉冲微梳；同时在同一器件中经由模式寻址获得亮孤子微梳，非寻址模式族隔离度达75–192线宽。

Conclusion: 横向模式族工程是在单一微谐振腔中兼顾局域模式间相互作用与独立频谱寻址的可行途径，为在一个集成相干光源中统一暗脉冲与亮孤子两类微梳提供了方案。

Abstract: Bright-soliton and dark-pulse microcombs provide complementary outputs. Integrating both states in a single resonator would expand the operating capabilities of an integrated coherent source, but requires opposite group-velocity-dispersion (GVD) channels while reconciling localized interfamily interaction for dark-pulse formation with spectral isolation for independent bright-soliton pumping. Wavelength-addressed access to both states is achieved in a LiTaO$_3$ microring through two TE-like transverse mode families. The higher-order TE$_{10}$ family fulfills two distinct roles in different spectral regions. Within localized avoided-mode-crossing regions, mode-resolved spectroscopy implicates TE$_{10}$ in perturbing the normal-GVD TE$_{00}$ resonance sequence. Away from these regions, TE$_{10}$ retains anomalous GVD and remains spectrally isolated as an independently pumpable bright-soliton channel; at representative pump resonances, the closest resonance of the non-addressed family is separated by 75--192 loaded cavity linewidths. With the input polarization held fixed, pumping TE$_{00}$ and TE$_{10}$ resonances generates dark-pulse and bright-soliton microcombs, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first experimental realization of a dark-pulse microcomb in an integrated LiTaO$_3$ microring. These results establish transverse-mode-family engineering as a route to combining localized modal interaction with independent spectral addressing in a single microresonator.

</details>


### [165] [Equivalence and origin of discrepancies between transfer matrix method and multiple reflection model of surface induced second harmonic generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30448)
*Deependra Singh Gaur,Jakub Lukeš,Karel Žídek*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文指出，计算薄膜表面/界面二次谐波产生（SHG）的传输矩阵法与多重反射模型虽理论上等价，但在数值实现不一致时会给出矛盾的SHG强度；作者明确了实现等价所需的条件及应施加的物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHG光谱是研究薄膜表面与界面的灵敏手段，但正确解释层边界与实测数据依赖于能描述薄膜中SHG产生的理论模型。传输矩阵法与多重反射模型是计算表面诱导SHG的两种常用数值技术，实践中二者的等价性并未得到保证，可能导致对同一输入性质与二阶极化率张量产生争议性结果。

Method: 对传输矩阵法与多重反射模型进行对比分析，考察二者在相同输入性质与二阶极化率张量下的数值实现；定位SHG强度差异的来源，指出差异源于界面SHG数值实现的不一致性，并推导两模型实现等价所需的条件，识别数值实现中必须施加的物理约束。

Result: 尽管两种方法在理论上应当等价，但对同一输入参数和二阶极化率张量，二者可给出相互矛盾的SHG强度结果。差异归因于界面SHG的数值实现不一致；作者给出了实现两模型等价的条件，并识别出为获得两方法一致结果所必须施加的物理约束。

Conclusion: 传输矩阵法与多重反射模型的等价性并非自动成立，而是依赖于界面SHG数值实现的一致性及特定物理约束的施加；这为薄膜SHG建模的正确实现与结果解读提供了明确准则。

Abstract: Second harmonic generation (SHG) spectroscopy is a sensitive tool to study surfaces and interfaces in thin films. To understand the layer boundaries and interpret the measured data, it is essential to use a theoretical model describing the SHG generation in the thin film. The transfer matrix method and multiple reflection model are two numerical techniques which have been commonly utilized in the calculation of surface-induced SHG. Here, we demonstrate that, despite the expected equivalence of the approaches, the two methods can lead to controversial results for the same input properties and second-order susceptibility tensors. The discrepancies resulting in different SHG intensity from both methods are caused by inconsistent numerical implementation of the interface SHG. We describe the conditions for the implementation to achieve the equivalence between the two models. Moreover, we identify the physical constraint that has to be imposed in numerical implementation to get identical results from both methods.

</details>


### [166] [1.2 dB Insertion Loss in a Monolithically Integrated Silicon Mach-Zehnder Optical Isolator Using Stoichiometric Ce:YIG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30501)
*Zhenyuan Ren,Di Wu,Tianchi Zhang,Yizhi Chen,Junxian Wang,Zixuan Wei,Jun Qin,Lei Bi*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过调控Ce:YIG薄膜化学计量比消除纳米团簇缺陷，将磁光薄膜FoM提升至72.7 deg/dB，并实现插损仅1.2 dB、隔离度27.6 dB的SiN基MZI型集成光隔离器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成磁光隔离器是光子集成回路（PIC）中稳定光信号的关键器件，但Si上沉积的Ce:YIG磁光薄膜FoM偏低，导致集成MO隔离器插损过高，制约其实用化。

Method: 研究薄膜化学计量比对Ce:YIG的FoM的调控作用；在非化学计量薄膜中观察到富铁与富铈的石榴石相纳米团簇；采用缺铁靶材在Si(100)上制备近化学计量比Ce:YIG薄膜以消除纳米团簇缺陷；并基于该方法在SiN平台上制备MZI型光隔离器。

Result: 近化学计量比Ce:YIG薄膜饱和法拉第旋转角达-5523 deg/cm，FoM为72.7 deg/dB，为非化学计量比薄膜的1.7倍；SiN基MZI型隔离器在1556 nm处实现创纪录的低插损1.2 dB与27.6 dB隔离比。

Conclusion: 化学计量比控制是提升Ce:YIG磁光性能的有效途径，该成果为集成磁光非互易光子器件的实用化迈出重要一步。

Abstract: Integrated optical isolators play a crucial role in photonic integrated circuits (PICs) by effectively stabilizing optical signals, making them indispensable components for advanced photonic systems. However, integrated magneto-optical (MO) isolators suffer from their high insertion loss, which is mainly caused by the relatively low FoM of Ce:YIG MO thin films deposited on silicon. Here, we show that film stoichiometry plays a key role in controlling the FoM of Ce:YIG. We observed garnet phase Fe-rich and Ce-rich nano-clusters in non-stoichiometric Ce:YIG. By using an iron-deficient target, we demonstrated that nearly stoichiometric Ce:YIG films on Si(100) could be fabricated, eliminating the nano-cluster defects. Consequently, the film exhibited a saturation Faraday rotation angle of -5523 deg/cm and a figure of merit of 72.7 deg/dB, which is 1.7 times higher compared to nonstoichiometric films. An MZI-type optical isolator on SiN fabricated by this method demonstrated a record low insertion loss of 1.2 dB and isolation ratio of 27.6 dB at 1556 nm wavelength. Our results provide an important step toward the practical application of integrated MO nonreciprocal photonic devices.

</details>


### [167] [Motion of dissipative solitons in quasi-one-dimensional potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30594)
*Valentin Besse,Herv{é} Leblond,Dumitru Mihalache,Boris A. Malomed*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在含准一维（空间周期或径向晶格）势的三次-五次复金兹堡-朗道方程框架下，数值研究了二维耗散孤子及孤子对的运动、横向倍增、斜向踢动叠加以及孤子碰撞等动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究耗散系统中二维孤子在准一维周期势与径向晶格势下的受控运动规律，特别是踢动（kick）激发的纵向/横向输运机制及势场对孤子轨迹的影响。

Method: 以三次-五次复金兹堡-朗道方程为基础，引入准一维势（空间周期势或径向晶格势），对单个或两个基孤子施加踢动作为初始条件，采用数值方法求解其演化。

Result: 对周期势，平行于势轴的踢动因该方向保持伽利略不变性而引发自由运动；横向运动需超过一定踢动强度阈值，并可能伴随脉冲倍增；斜向踢动表现为纵、横向运动的直接叠加。此外考察了纵向与对角方向运动孤子间的碰撞。对径向势，向中心踢动的孤子可从中心势丘弹回或穿过，形成复杂轨迹并发生孤子分裂。

Conclusion: 准一维势中各向异性结构显著调控二维耗散孤子的运动模式：纵向自由输运、横向阈值激发及径向势中的反弹/穿透与分裂，表明势场几何与踢动方向是控制孤子动力学的关键参数。

Abstract: We consider the motion of two-dimensional (2D) dissipative solitons and soliton pairs in the framework of the cubic-quintic complex Ginzburg-Landau equation with quasi-1D potentials, spatially periodic or radial-lattice ones. The equation is solved numerically for inputs representing one or two fundamental solitons, which are set in motion by a kick. For the quasi-1D potential, the kick parallel to the potential axis naturally initiates free motion of the 2D solitons, as the model remains Galilean-invariant in this direction. Motion in the transverse direction, which may entail pulse multiplication, commences if the kick is strong enough. Oblique kicks lead to a straightforward superposition of the longitudinal and transverse motions. Also considered is the collision between 2D solitons moving in the longitudinal and diagonal directions. In the radial potential, the soliton kicked towards the center may bounce back from the central potential hill or pass it, producing complex trajectories and splitting of the moving soliton.

</details>


### [168] [A Highly Tunable (Ti,Zr)N$_x$-Based MIM Plasmonic Sensor for Multidomain Biological, Chemical, and Physical Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30614)
*Mohammad Abrar Kabir,Aosaf Jarif Siddiquee,Abdullah Taharat,A. K. M. Rakib,Rakibul Hasan Sagor*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出以三元氮化物(Ti,Zr)Nx为等离子体材料的方形环谐振腔嵌圆纳米棒MIM折射率传感器，实现2146.496 nm/RIU灵敏度与4.669×10⁻⁷ RIU检测极限，并覆盖多类生物与理化传感场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MIM等离子体折射率传感器依赖金、银等贵金属，在可调性、热稳定性和CMOS工艺兼容性方面存在根本限制，亟需可替代的等离子体材料与结构方案。

Method: 设计方形环谐振腔并嵌入圆形纳米棒构建MIM结构，采用三元氮化物(Ti,Zr)Nx作为等离子体材料（首次用于MIM传感平台），利用其成分可调性调控光学响应；通过数值仿真在葡萄糖、血红蛋白贫血分级、五种血液成分辨识、六类癌细胞识别，以及气压、CO2（PHMB功能化）等场景验证性能。

Result: 最大折射率灵敏度2146.496 nm/RIU，检测极限4.669×10⁻⁷ RIU，优于已报道的CMOS兼容等离子体折射率传感器；气压灵敏度6.158 nm/MPa且热交叉灵敏度可忽略；CO2灵敏度330.12 pm/ppm，为同类结构报道最高值。

Conclusion: (Ti,Zr)Nx基MIM方形环-纳米棒谐振结构兼具CMOS兼容性、成分可调性与高热稳定性，在生物医学诊断与理化检测中展现高灵敏、多场景适用性，为无贵金属等离子体传感平台提供可行路径。

Abstract: Metal-Insulator-Metal (MIM) plasmonic refractive index (RI) sensors have attracted considerable attention for label-free, real-time analyte detection; however, their reliance on noble metals such as gold and silver imposes fundamental constraints on tunability, thermal stability, and CMOS process compatibility. In this work, we propose a novel MIM plasmonic RI sensor based on a square ring resonator with embedded circular nanorods, employing the ternary nitride (Ti,Zr)N$_x$ as the plasmonic material, marking its first application in a MIM sensing platform. Compared to binary nitrides and other alternative plasmonic materials reported in the literature, (Ti,Zr)N$_x$ offers broader compositional tunability and greater flexibility in tailoring the optical response while remaining fully CMOS-compatible. The sensor is validated across a comprehensive range of biosensing and physicochemical analyte scenarios, including glucose monitoring for diabetes assessment, hemoglobin-based anemia classification across four severity levels, simultaneous discrimination of five major blood constituents, and cancer cell identification across six tissue types. For physical and chemical sensing, the structure achieves an air pressure sensitivity of 6.158 nm/MPa with negligible thermal cross-sensitivity, and a CO$_2$ detection sensitivity of 330.12 pm/ppm via polyhexamethylene biguanide (PHMB) functionalization, which is the highest reported among comparable structures in the literature. The proposed sensor achieves a maximum RI sensitivity of 2146.496 nm/RIU with a detection limit of $4.669 \times 10^{-7}$ RIU, outperforming previously reported CMOS-compatible plasmonic RI sensors.

</details>


### [169] [Pulse-to-pulse Power Fluctuations in Ultra-Fast Fiber Amplifiers with Gain-Managed Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30794)
*Maksim Kozlov,Colin J. McKinstrie,Nitzan Haviv,Pavel Sidorenko*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出预测增益管理非线性（GMN）超快光纤放大器脉冲间功率波动的理论框架，涵盖泵浦与种子功率噪声及ASE贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GMN区域放大器中信号谱展宽与频率相关增益共同作用，使传统RIN预测方法失效，且需避免昂贵蒙特卡洛模拟。

Method: 建立直接求解统计平均量的数值模型（无需随机源蒙特卡洛）；在信号带宽趋于零、ASE可忽略时退化为半解析单色模型。

Result: 信号及其扰动信号的非线性谱展宽与频率相关增益显著改变技术RIN，在高功率高重频与低功率低重频两种工况下均成立；低功率低重频下ASE贡献可与泵浦、种子涨落相当。

Conclusion: 所提框架可高效、准确地预测GMN光纤放大器的脉冲间功率噪声，并揭示ASE在低重频区不可忽略。

Abstract: We present a theoretical framework for predicting pulse-to-pulse power fluctuations caused by pump and seed power noise and by amplified spontaneous emission in ultrafast fiber amplifiers operating in the gain-managed nonlinear regime. The numerical model evaluates statistically averaged quantities directly, without Monte Carlo simulations with stochastic sources, and reduces to a semi-analytical monochromatic model in the limit of vanishing signal bandwidth and negligible ASE. We show that nonlinear spectral broadening of the signal and of its perturbations, combined with the frequency-dependent gain, significantly alters the technical relative intensity noise in both the high-power, high-repetition-rate and the low-power, low-repetition-rate regimes, and that in the latter regime the contributions related to amplified spontaneous emission become comparable to those induced by pump and seed fluctuations.

</details>


### [170] [Programmable Broadband Polarization-Preserving Optical Delay Line with Faraday Mirrors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.30814)
*Salem F. Hegazy,Salah S. A. Obayya,Bahaa E. A. Saleh*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种可编程宽带光延迟线，延迟可达1 μs、分辨率纳秒级，且能保持任意未知偏振态；其核心为级联单模光纤段双向通过+FARADAY镜反射自动补偿偏振，并发现双程传播可缓解FM带来的色散畸变，50 nm带宽内平均保真度优于99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光纤延迟线在引入延迟的同时会因光纤双折射使偏振态随机漂移，且宽带工作受色散限制；需一种既能大范围可编程延迟、又能保持原始任意未知偏振态且具备宽带特性的延迟线。

Method: 延迟线由级联标准单模光纤段构成，光在每段内双向传输，两端以Faraday镜（FM）终端反射；每段含短路径/长路径，由光开关选择；FM自动补偿光纤引入的随机偏振波动以恢复初始偏振态。通过分析光纤与FM的色散评估宽带性能，并进行数值实验。

Result: 意外发现光纤中双程传播可缓解FM引入的色散畸变；数值实验表明在偏离中心波长50 nm范围内输出偏振态平均保真度优于99%，且平均保真度常高于无光纤情形。

Conclusion: 该双向+FARADAY镜架构可实现μs级、纳秒分辨率的偏振保持宽带光延迟线，双程传播的色散补偿机制使其宽带偏振保真度优异，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We report a broadband optical delay line programmable for delays up to 1 μs with nanosecond resolution, while preserving an arbitrary and unknown polarization state of the original light. The delay line comprises bidirectional pass in cascaded segments of standard single-mode fibers, along with terminal reflection from Faraday mirrors (FMs) at both ends. Each segment contains either a short path or a long path, selected by an optical switch. The FMs auto-compensate for random polarization fluctuations introduced by the optical fibers, thereby restoring the initial polarization state. Broadband operation of the delay line was assessed by investigating dispersion of both the fiber and the FMs. Surprisingly, we found that the two-pass propagation in the fiber can mitigate dispersive distortion introduced by the FM. Numerical experiments demonstrated that the output polarization state of the delay line has a mean fidelity better than 99% over a range of 50-nm deviation from the central wavelength. Remarkably, the mean fidelity often surpasses that in the absence of the fiber itself.

</details>


### [171] [Localized Strain Tuning of Second-Harmonic Generation in MoS2 Monolayers and Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.31097)
*Martín Aversa,Florencia Cammarata,Camila Borrazás,Nadia C. Vega,David Comedi,Andrea V. Bragas,Gustavo Grinblat*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过AFM针尖对悬空MoS2膜施加局部机械形变，实现对二次谐波产生的可逆调制，得到约每1%有效应变调制12% SHG的特征灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究原子级薄二维材料中非线性光学发射的机械调控机制，特别是局部形变与界面粘附对SHG的影响。

Method: 利用CVD生长的MoS2单层与少层薄片，悬空于微结构Si3N4基底上；用AFM针尖施加局域机械形变，在可控接近–回撤循环中同步测量力与SHG强度，并将光学调制与拉脱力及唯象有效应变估计相关联。

Result: 悬空区域SHG强于基底支撑区域，夹持边界附近明显抑制；压缩加载阶段的SHG响应因膜而异，而回撤中粘附介导的拉伸一致降低SHG直至针尖脱附后恢复；单层与双层行为相当，灵敏度约12% SHG/1%有效应变。

Conclusion: 粘附介导的机械形变构成一种独立且可逆的调控原子级薄膜非线性光学发射的机制。

Abstract: We investigate the modulation of second-harmonic generation (SHG) in CVD-grown MoS2 monolayers and few-layer flakes through localized mechanical deformation applied by an atomic force microscope (AFM) tip. MoS2 membranes suspended over microstructured Si3N4 substrates allow simultaneous measurements of force and SHG intensity during controlled approach and retraction cycles. Suspended regions exhibit enhanced SHG relative to substrate-supported regions, with pronounced suppression near the clamping boundaries. While the SHG response during compressive loading varies among individual membranes, adhesion-mediated pulling during retraction consistently reduces the SHG intensity until tip detachment, when the signal recovers. By correlating the optical modulation with the measured pull-off force and a phenomenological estimate of effective strain, we obtain a characteristic sensitivity of approximately 12% SHG modulation per 1% effective strain, with comparable behavior in monolayers and bilayers. These findings reveal adhesion-mediated mechanical deformation as a distinct and reversible mechanism for controlling nonlinear optical emission in atomically thin membranes.

</details>


### [172] [Experimental Demonstration of 7-Dimensional N-Vector Model with Dimensional Inflation and Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18153)
*Daniele Veraldi,Marcello Calvanese Strinati,Paolo Charlesworth,Matteo Spaziani,Silvia Gentilini,Davide Pierangeli,Claudio Conti*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 首次实验实现由高维哈密顿量（最高七维）描述的物理系统，基于全息光学计算机通过时空复用实现维度膨胀，并演示了N-矢量模型与维度退火求解Ising基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维理论模型（如场论）通常难以在实验平台上实现，缺乏可扩展、桌面级的物理验证手段；同时组合优化问题需要新方法。

Method: 采用全息光学计算机，通过对低维系统进行空间与时间复用实现"维度膨胀"，构建最高七维的哈密顿量；以N-矢量模型为演示对象，并提出"维度退火"——动态降低维数，使系统由高维空间经绝热演化收敛到复杂图上Ising模型的基态。

Result: 实验实现了七维哈密顿量系统与N-矢量模型，并成功通过维度退火在复杂图中找到Ising模型基态。

Conclusion: 该方案可扩展，为高维理论模型提供桌面级实现途径，并为组合优化提供新方法。

Abstract: We report the first experimental realization of a physical system described by a high-dimensional Hamiltonian comprising up to seven dimensions. Our setup is based on a holographic optical computer, which realizes a dimensional inflation by spatial and temporal multiplexing of lower dimensional systems. We demonstrate an $N$-vector model, with applications in field theory and computing. The approach is scalable and enables table-top implementation of high-dimensional theoretical models, and novel methods for combinatorial optimization. We experimentally demonstrate dimensional annealing: by dynamically reducing the number of dimensions, the ground state of an Ising model is found in complex graphs by an adiabatic evolution from high-dimensional spaces.

</details>
